Once triggered, the backdoored model generates frozen future frames that no longer respond to subsequent user actions, while preserving normal behavior on benign action sequences. In addition, we explore a stealthier attack in which multimodal triggers jointly poison action, text, and image inputs. Experiments show that BadAction achieves average attack success rates of 91.0% with action-only triggers and 80.4% with multimodal triggers. Moreover, extensive defense evaluations show that BadAction successfully bypasses existing backdoor detection methods, revealing a critical security gap in the interactive video generation pipeline. Project page: https://wsad55.github.io/badaction01/.

</details>


### [72] [TSMD: Temporal-Stream Modality Dropout for Robust Video Highlight Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39051)
*Bo-Yuan Cheng,Kuan-Yu Chen,Po-Han Huang,Jeng-Lin Li,Jian-Jiun Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对多模态视频高光检测中模态缺失问题，提出TSMD方法，通过结构化缺失模拟与联合损失函数提升鲁棒性，在多个数据集上显著优于TripleSumm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法假设视觉、音频、文本流连续可用，但实际中可能出现局部帧缺失或整条流中断；同时MSE损失与评价指标及高光峰值特性不匹配。

Method: 提出Temporal-Stream Modality Dropout (TSMD)，结合结构化缺失模拟与联合目标函数，包含逐点MSE、逐视频Pearson相关和面向峰值的RankNet损失项，设有时序、流级别和混合三种变体。

Result: 在MoSu和Mr. HiSum数据集上，TSMD-Temporal在50%独立时序移除下mAP@15分别提升7.06和3.41点；TSMD-Stream在完全流移除下最佳；TSMD-Mix综合表现最优或次优。

Conclusion: TSMD有效应对时序与流级别模态缺失，三种变体各具优势且互补，验证了缺失模拟与损失设计对鲁棒高光检测的重要性。

Abstract: Existing multimodal video highlight detectors typically assume that visual, audio, and textual streams are continuously available. In practice, however, inputs may suffer from localized frame missingness or complete-stream outage. We formulate this robustness challenge along two dimensions: temporal missingness, where frames are missing independently in each modality, and stream-level missingness, where one modality is unavailable throughout a video. Moreover, we find that the mean squared error (MSE) loss is misaligned with both the evaluation metrics and the peak-driven nature of highlights. Therefore, we propose Temporal-Stream Modality Dropout (TSMD), which combines structured missingness simulation with a joint objective comprising pointwise MSE, per-video Pearson correlation, and peak-oriented RankNet loss terms. TSMD has three variants: temporal, stream-level, and mixed dropout. On the MoSu and Mr. HiSum datasets, TSMD-Temporal improves mAP@15 by 7.06 and 3.41 points over TripleSumm under 50% independent temporal removal, whereas TSMD-Stream performs the best under complete-stream removal. TSMD-Mix retains most of these complementary benefits and ranks the best or the second-best across the evaluated temporal and stream-level conditions.

</details>


### [73] [Agentic Tool-Augmented Reasoning for Explainable Image Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39066)
*Zhiya Tan,Jing Huang,Changtao Miao,Lin Tan,Xin Zhang,Weiwei Feng,Jianshu Li,Joey Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ATAR框架，借助22种取证工具与多轮推理，实现图像伪造的自主检测、定位与可解释解释，在零样本基准上显著超越现有MLLM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像伪造检测只输出二值分数或像素掩码，缺乏可解释证据；而现有多模态大模型方法往往先给出预定分类结果再生成事后解释，而非从证据出发进行推理，与人类司法鉴定专家的取证工作流程不符。

Method: 提出Agentic Tool-Augmented Reasoning（ATAR）框架，整合7个互补领域的22种取证工具进行多轮自主推理。采用双流取证推理范式：高层语义异常路径放大可疑区域做细粒度检查，低层伪造痕迹路径调用工具提取客观证据。并引入取证课程学习：通用经验SFT阶段用自动师生辅导流程合成多轮工具使用推理轨迹；取证场景RL阶段用工具先验课程引导早期工具探索并逐步移交控制权，配合结构化证据奖励提供过程级监督。

Result: 在IMDL、Deepfake检测、DMDL和AIGC检测任务上实验：在六个零样本IMDL基准上取得78.5%平均图像级F1，超过最强MLLM基线11.8个百分点；在其他任务上与专用检测器保持竞争力，同时生成更忠实、更有依据的解释。

Conclusion: ATAR通过工具增强的智能体推理与课程学习，实现了从证据出发的伪造检测与解释，验证了类司法取证工作流程在图像取证中的有效性。

Abstract: Conventional image forgery detection methods produce binary scores or pixel-level masks without interpretable evidence, while recent multimodal large language model (MLLM)-based approaches generate post-hoc explanations of predetermined classification results rather than reasoning from evidence. Inspired by the forensic workflow of human judicial experts, we propose Agentic Tool-Augmented Reasoning (ATAR), a framework integrating 22 specialized forensic tools across seven complementary domains to autonomously detect, localize, and explain image forgeries through multi-turn reasoning. A Dual-Stream Forensic Reasoning paradigm combines a high-level semantic anomaly path, which magnifies suspicious regions for fine-grained inspection, with a low-level forgery artifact path, which invokes forensic tools to extract objective evidence. We further introduce Forensics Curriculum Learning: during General Experience SFT, an automated teacher-student mentoring pipeline synthesizes multi-turn tool-usage reasoning trajectories; during Forensic Scene RL, a Tool Prior Curriculum guides early tool exploration and progressively transfers control to the agent, while a Structured Evidence Reward provides fine-grained process-level supervision. Experiments across IMDL, Deepfake detection, DMDL, and AIGC detection show that ATAR achieves 78.5% average image-level F1 on six zero-shot IMDL benchmarks, surpassing the strongest MLLM baseline by 11.8 percentage points, and remains competitive with specialized detectors on other tasks while producing substantially more faithful and grounded explanations.

</details>


### [74] [MRI Super-Resolution with RCDM/WaveMix and Task-Aware Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39083)
*Kavitha Viswanathan,Harsh Choudhary,Amit Sethi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究1.5T脑MRI增强是否真正改善下游组织分割，发现增强效果高度依赖于分割模型和标签质量：对ABIDE上的小波分割器有显著提升，却损害U-Net；对IXI因标签CSF不可靠而全面降低Dice。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低场1.5T脑MRI的超分辨与质量增强通常只用图像保真度指标评估，但其真正目的是改善下游分析。作者希望探究增强是否真的提升组织分割效果，以及这种效果对不同分割器是否有差异。

Method: 提出一种非配对、物理引导的训练流程，构建轻量级（≤2.5M参数）循环卷积增强器：包含六模块随机1.5T退化算子、只添加扫描仪特定纹理而不改变解剖结构的残差对抗网络，以及带反恒等惩罚的循环一致目标以排除直接复制解。随后用相同标签与受试者级划分，从头训练U-Net、Swin-UNet和小波token混合分割器，在原始或增强的1.5T图像上进行对比，涵盖三种增强器变体和两个数据集。

Result: 在ABIDE（41名留出受试者，FreeSurfer标签）上，增强显著提升小波分割器（平均Dice +0.014，Wilcoxon p=3.5e-5；CSF +0.018，灰质 +0.013），显著降低U-Net（-0.008，p=5.1e-4），对Swin-UNet无影响。在IXI（FSL-FAST标签）上，增强使全部九种组合的Dice下降，几乎完全由CSF造成，原因可追溯到标签中空间上不合理的CSF体素惩罚了更平滑的预测。

Conclusion: 低场MRI的增强应针对每个下游模型分别验证，并使用可靠的标签进行评估，不能仅凭图像保真度指标判断增强效果。

Abstract: Super-resolution and quality enhancement of 1.5\,T brain MRI are normally validated with image-fidelity metrics, although their purpose is to improve downstream analysis. We study whether enhancement improves tissue segmentation, and for which segmenters. We propose an unpaired, physics-guided training pipeline for a lightweight ($\le$2.5\,M parameter) recurrent convolutional enhancer: a six-module stochastic 1.5\,T degradation operator, a residual adversarial network that adds scanner-specific texture without moving anatomy, and a cycle-consistent objective with an anti-identity penalty that rules out the copy solution. We then train U-Net, Swin-UNet and wavelet token-mixing segmenters \citep{jeevan2023wavemix} from scratch on either raw or enhanced 1.5\,T images of the same subjects, using identical labels and subject-level splits, for three enhancer variants and two datasets. On ABIDE (41 held-out subjects, FreeSurfer labels) enhancement significantly improves the wavelet segmenter (mean Dice $+0.014$, Wilcoxon $p=3.5\times10^{-5}$; CSF $+0.018$, grey matter $+0.013$), significantly degrades the U-Net ($-0.008$, $p=5.1\times10^{-4}$) and leaves Swin-UNet unchanged. On IXI, whose labels come from FSL-FAST, enhancement lowers Dice for all nine pairings, almost entirely through CSF; we trace this to spatially implausible CSF voxels in the labels that penalise smoother predictions. Enhancement of low-field MRI should therefore be validated per downstream model and against reliable labels.

</details>


### [75] [UGOD: Uncertainty-Guided Opacity and Dropout for Sparse-View 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39089)
*Zhihao Guo,Peng Wang,Zidong Chen,Xiangyu Kong,Yan Lyu,Guanyu Gao,Chenghao Qian,Ziyang Wang,Xinqi Fan,Liangxiu Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UGOD，用视图相关不确定性分数引导稀疏视图3D高斯泼溅的渲染贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视图下3D高斯泼溅易过拟合，许多高斯基元约束不足，其错误贡献仍通过alpha混合累加，破坏新视图合成。缺乏不确定性估计使渲染器无法区分不可靠与约束良好的基元。

Method: 引入轻量不确定性头，基于高斯属性和视角方向预测视图相关不确定性分数；通过可微不透明度调制机制在合成前衰减高不确定性基元；训练时用分离的软丢弃分支施加不确定性控制的连续保留掩码作为正则，且分离不确定性分数以避免该随机正则的梯度偏置或坍缩不确定性预测。

Result: 在Mip-NeRF 360和LLFF上，UGOD提升了稀疏视图新视图合成质量，并产生比对比方法更紧凑的高斯表示。

Conclusion: 高斯不确定性可为稀疏视图重建提供有效的渲染时控制。

Abstract: Sparse-view 3D Gaussian Splatting is prone to overfitting because limited observations leave many Gaussian primitives weakly constrained, yet their contributions are still accumulated through alpha blending. Without uncertainty estimation, the renderer cannot distinguish unreliable primitives from well-constrained ones, allowing their erroneous contributions to corrupt novel-view synthesis. We introduce UGOD, an uncertainty-guided framework that estimates a view-dependent uncertainty score for each Gaussian and uses it to regulate its rendering contribution. A lightweight uncertainty head conditioned on Gaussian attributes and viewing direction predicts this score, which then drives a differentiable opacity-modulation mechanism that attenuates high-uncertainty primitives before compositing. During training, a detached soft-dropout branch applies an uncertainty-controlled continuous keep mask to discourage the model from relying on poorly constrained Gaussians and thereby reduce overfitting. Crucially, detaching the uncertainty score prevents gradients from this stochastic regulariser from biasing or collapsing the uncertainty prediction. Experiments on Mip-NeRF~360 and LLFF show that UGOD improves sparse-view novel-view synthesis while producing more compact Gaussian representations than the compared methods. These results demonstrate that Gaussian uncertainty provides an effective rendering-time control for sparse-view reconstruction.

</details>


### [76] [DeCoPrune: Efficient KV-Cache Pruning for Autoregressive Video Diffusion via Denoising Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39096)
*Zeqi Xiao,Qingle Liu,Kaiwen Zhang,Yifan Zhou,Zihan Ding,Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 DeCoPrune，将自回归视频扩散的 KV 缓存压缩视为去噪一致性问题，并发布 CMBench 基准验证其长程信息保留能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散支持流式生成与交互控制，但 KV 缓存随生成历史线性增长。现有压缩策略依赖固定窗口或基于局部注意力和相似度的信号，未直接衡量某个 chunk 是否提供超出保留上下文的信息，导致长程信息保留不足。

Method: DeCoPrune 是免训练方法：利用模型内在信号，即中间干净预测与最终去噪值之间的差异（discrepancy），将高差异 token 保留在长期缓存中，低差异 token 剪枝，因为这些高差异 token 携带难以从已有上下文预测的视觉证据。同时引入 CMBench 基准，包含 58 段约一分钟的生成或真实世界上下文片段，以及 116 个 Reappear/Revisit 延续任务。

Result: 在 LingBot World v2 上，DeCoPrune 取得 DINO 分数 0.6701（0-1 区间），累计历史 KV token 数减少 85.43%，延续生成相对 FullKV 提速 4.14 倍；头部特化变体在 86.19% 剪枝率下达到 0.6783，接近 FullKV 的 0.6803，并在相近预算下超过所评估的压缩基线。

Conclusion: 去噪一致性可作为有效的缓存压缩信号，在显著降低自回归推理成本的同时支持长程信息保留。项目主页、代码与基准已公开。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion supports streaming generation and interactive control, but its KV cache grows with the generated history. Existing compression strategies discard history using fixed windows or select tokens through local attention and similarity signals, without directly measuring whether a chunk contributes information beyond the retained context. We introduce DeCoPrune, a training-free method that treats cache compression as a denoising-consistency problem. We find that tokens with larger discrepancies between intermediate clean predictions and final denoised values tend to carry visual evidence less predictable from the retained context. DeCoPrune uses this model-intrinsic signal to retain high-discrepancy tokens in the long-term cache while pruning low-discrepancy tokens. To evaluate information retention, we introduce CMBench, comprising 58 approximately one-minute generated or real-world context episodes and 116 Reappear or Revisit continuation tasks requiring recall of earlier events or objects. Experiments with LingBot World v2 show that DeCoPrune achieves a DINO score of 0.6701 on a 0-1 scale, with an 85.43% reduction in cumulative historical KV token counts and a 4.14-fold continuation-generation speedup over FullKV. Its head-specialized variant reaches 0.6783 at an 86.19% pruning ratio, approaching FullKV's 0.6803 score and exceeding the evaluated compression baselines at similar budgets. These results indicate that denoising consistency can support long-range information retention while reducing autoregressive inference cost. Our project homepage is https://decoprune.github.io. The code is available at https://github.com/DeCoPrune/CMBench, and the benchmark at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Aoraku/CMBench.

</details>


### [77] [CamAgent: An LLM-Agent Framework for Multi-Species Camera-Trap Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39112)
*Yutong Deng,Qi Song,Xi Guo,Tianming Wang,Lei Bao,Jianping Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CamAgent是一个基于大语言模型的自主智能体框架，将碎片化的相机陷阱数据分析流程整合为统一生态系统，通过自然语言交互自动编排并执行物种识别、数据管理、占据建模、活动节律与共现网络等分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱积累了海量多维野生动物监测数据，但现有研究流程需人工拼接各类分析工具与脚本，编程门槛高，端到端时空分析困难，导致原始媒体档案难以转化为生态学洞见。

Method: 提出CamAgent框架：解析自然语言表达的生态学意图，进行计算路由调度，并调用专门工具，涵盖计算机视觉感知（如SpeciesNet）、兼容CamtrapDP的数据管理、检测校正的占据建模、时间活动分析与物种共现网络，在自动化多阶段流程的同时保留数据质量控制与分析规范。

Result: CamAgent显著降低了保护工作者的手工编程负担，实现了透明、可扩展且高度集成的相机陷阱生态学分析范式。

Conclusion: 将LLM智能体引入相机陷阱生态学，可有效弥合工具碎片化问题，为野生动物监测数据的端到端分析提供统一、自动化且可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Camera traps accumulated vast, multidimensional data for wildlife monitoring, yet translating raw media archives into meaningful ecological insights remains highly fragmented. Current research workflows require laboriously stitching together disparate analysis tools and scripts, creating steep programming hurdles and complicating end-to-end spatiotemporal analyses. To overcome this fragmentation, we present CamAgent, an autonomous Large Language Model (LLM) agent framework that integrates camera-trap analytical workflows into a unified intelligent ecosystem. CamAgent interprets natural-language ecological intent, schedules computational routing, and executes specialized tools spanning computer-vision perception (e.g., SpeciesNet), CamtrapDP-compatible data management, detection-corrected occupancy modeling, temporal activity analysis, and species co-occurrence networks. The framework automates multi-stage analytical pipelines while maintaining essential data-quality controls and analytical conventions. Consequently, CamAgent significantly reduces manual programming overhead for conservationists, establishing a transparent, scalable, and fully integrated paradigm for camera-trap ecology. Our project is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/artifact72c6f4.

</details>


### [78] [Beyond Local Linearity: Scale-Resolved Geometry of Learned Image Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39115)
*Jakub Szymkowiak,Wojtek Pałubicki,Kamil Adamczewski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种尺度分辨的统计量，对比编码器特征位移与局部线性预测随扰动幅度增大的差异，发现多种图像编码器中普遍存在"高原-上升-峰值-衰减"的凸起（bump）轮廓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何分析多为局部性的，只能刻画无穷小扰动，无法说明学习到的表示在有限输入变化下如何响应，限制了对其敏感性、不变性与鲁棒性的理解。

Method: 引入尺度分辨统计量，将编码器在有限扰动下的实际特征位移与其局部线性预测进行比较，并随扰动幅度增大进行扫描；在多种图像编码器、不同初始化、训练阶段、标签与输入条件及训练分布和鲁棒性目标下进行对比分析。

Result: 在多样图像编码器中观察到特征性的"高原-上升-峰值-衰减"轮廓（bump）。该凸起在初始化时不存在，标准训练早期出现；在随机标签或随机噪声输入下不形成；其形状还随训练分布和鲁棒性目标而变化。

Conclusion: 偏离局部几何可作为编码器表示如何被学习塑造的一种标志，为表示敏感性、不变性与鲁棒性的分析提供了超越无穷小尺度的手段。

Abstract: Understanding how learned representations respond to finite input changes is important for characterizing their sensitivity, invariances, and robustness. Yet existing geometric analyses are predominantly local and describe only infinitesimal perturbations. We introduce a scale-resolved statistic that compares an encoder's measured feature displacement with its local linear prediction as the perturbation magnitude increases. Across diverse image encoders, we discover a characteristic plateau-rise-peak-decay profile, which we call the bump. The bump is absent at initialization, emerges early during standard training, and does not form under randomized labels or random-noise inputs. Its shape also varies with the training distribution and robustness objective. These results establish departures from local geometry as a signature of how encoder representations are shaped by learning.

</details>


### [79] [GRC-Pose: Generation-Reconstruction Correspondence for Prior-Free 6D Object Pose Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39116)
*Shiyang Liu,Weiquan Lin,Luping Xiao,Jiadong Tang,Yi Yang,Yu Gao,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GRC-Pose，将无先验6D物体姿态跟踪建模为生成-重建对应问题，结合学习到的对应预测与鲁棒位姿估计，实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无先验6D物体姿态跟踪希望在无CAD模型、无参考图像、无标注的情况下从单目RGB视频恢复未见物体的轨迹。几何基础模型提供物体中心与场景中心的互补线索，但SAM3D CAD使用任意的物体局部表面参数化，而重建证据处于序列特定的世界坐标系且表面覆盖不完整，二者难以直接结合。

Method: 将跟踪表述为生成-重建对应（GRC）问题，提出GRC-Pose框架。其中GeoCorr-Matcher估计加权的物体-场景对应及每个匹配的不确定性；FGH-Solver通过多个鲁棒几何估计器和序列级后验推断整合匹配；后验门控记忆仅在遮挡和视角变化中保留由内点支持的观测。

Result: 在SAM3D CAD下，GRC-Pose在HOT3D上取得SOTA的平均召回率和运动保持率，运动保持率较先前工作提升58%；在YCBInEOAT和LINEMOD等经典基准上也保持很强竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过生成-重建对应有效融合物体中心与场景中心线索，GRC-Pose在无先验6D姿态跟踪中表现优异且具泛化性。

Abstract: Prior-free 6D object pose tracking seeks to recover the trajectory of an unseen object from a single RGB video without object-specific CAD models, posed reference images, or pose annotations. Geometric foundation models provide complementary object-centric and scene-centric cues, yet SAM3D CAD is indexed by an arbitrary object-local surface parameterization, whereas reconstructed evidence is expressed in a sequence-specific world frame with partial surface coverage. To exploit this complementarity, we formulate tracking as generation-reconstruction correspondence and introduce GRC-Pose, a correspondence-based framework that combines learned correspondence prediction with robust pose estimation. Concretely, GeoCorr-Matcher estimates weighted object-scene correspondences and per-match uncertainty for each pose candidate. FGH-Solver integrates these matches through multiple robust geometric estimators and sequence-level posterior inference, while a posterior-gated memory retains only inlier-supported observations through occlusion and viewpoint change. Extensive evaluation shows that with SAM3D CAD, GRC-Pose achieves state-of-the-art Average Recall and motion retention on HOT3D, improving the latter by 58% over prior art. On classical benchmarks including YCBInEOAT and LINEMOD, it remains highly competitive.

</details>


### [80] [Is Better Teacher Supervision Enough? Unlocking Student-side Learning in Multimodal On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39120)
*Siyuan Liu,Kanghui Tian,Yue Duan,Yutao He,Shangdong Yang,Jian Zhang,Yinghuan Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出S-OPD，一个多模态在线策略蒸馏框架，通过教师校准策略对比与策略一致性两个目标强化学生的感知学习，无需额外标注、参数或推理开销，在八个基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态在线策略蒸馏（OPD）主要着力于增强教师端指导（如丰富教师输入、精炼教师反馈），但作者发现学生的感知能力有限也是关键瓶颈：即便提供oracle视觉事实，弱教师和强教师训练的学生性能仍可显著提升，说明问题在于学生自身的感知学习不足。

Method: 提出S-OPD框架，包含两个目标：1）教师校准的策略对比（Teacher-calibrated Policy Contrast），利用教师基于token级别的门控，区分学生在原图与被遮蔽图像下的策略，强化学生对视觉证据的依赖；2）策略一致性（Policy Agreement），对齐学生在原图与噪声扰动图像下的策略，提升对视觉噪声的感知鲁棒性。该方法可无缝嵌入现有OPD框架。

Result: 在八个基准、不同学生规模和蒸馏范式下均取得一致性能提升，LogicVista上最高提升4.25分；与现有教师端监督方法结合可带来进一步增益。无需额外数据标注、模型参数或推理操作。

Conclusion: 学生感知能力不足是多模态OPD的重要瓶颈，通过显式强化学生感知学习（对比与一致性目标）可有效提升蒸馏效果，且方法通用、兼容现有框架并易于集成。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves reasoning by providing token-level supervision from a teacher on a student's own trajectories. Existing methods primarily focus on enhancing this teacher-side guidance (e.g., by enriching teacher inputs and refining teacher feedback), yet we find that limited student perception is another critical bottleneck in multimodal OPD. By providing oracle visual facts, the performance of OPD-trained students can still be substantially improved for both weak and strong teachers. To address this bottleneck, we propose S-OPD, a simple multimodal on-policy distillation framework that explicitly strengthens student perceptual learning through two objectives. Specifically, Teacher-calibrated Policy Contrast separates student policies under original and masked images with teacher-based token-level gating, strengthening the student's reliance on visual evidence during reasoning. Policy Agreement aligns student policies under original and noise-perturbed images, further improving perceptual robustness to visual noise. Notably, our method can be seamlessly plugged into existing OPD frameworks, requiring no additional data annotations, model parameters or inference operations. Extensive experiments on eight benchmarks across student scales and distillation paradigms demonstrate consistent performance improvements, with gains of up to 4.25 points on LogicVista. When combined with existing teacher-side supervision methods, our method can yield further gains. Code is available at https://github.com/Sirilaw/S-OPD.

</details>


### [81] [How to Reduce Localization Ambiguity? Geometry-Semantic Constrained BEV Representation Learning for Satellite-Ground Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39127)
*Junming Feng,Panwang Xia,Qiong Wu,Xudong Lu,Zeyu Jiao,Kun Lv,Zherong Wu,Yi Wan,Peifeng Ma,Li-Ta Hsu,Zhi Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoSem-BEV，通过几何与语义约束改善BEV表示学习，提升星地定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有星地定位方法在BEV空间建立地面与卫星特征对应时，存在深度约束不足导致的几何歧义（同一地面特征可对应不同距离）以及相似外观造成的描述子匹配歧义，且描述子学习缺乏显式语义监督来区分这些位置。

Method: 提出GeoSem-BEV，一种几何-语义约束的BEV表示学习方法。包含：径向深度监督约束距离分配，垂直高度监督约束高度聚合，共享显式语义监督促进跨视图语义预测一致性并帮助区分语义相似的位置。

Result: 在VIGOR未知朝向设置下，跨区域和同区域平均朝向误差分别降低37.2%和38.1%；在DReSS-D上相应误差降低10.8%和15.6%；在KITTI-CVL上，10度朝向噪声下同区域平均朝向误差降低26.8%。

Conclusion: 几何与语义约束改进特征放置与描述子可区分性，有效增强现有最先进BEV定位模型性能。

Abstract: Satellite-ground localization estimates the planar position and yaw orientation of a ground camera within a geo-referenced satellite image. Most recent methods map ground and satellite features into a shared bird's-eye-view (BEV) space and establish spatial correspondences. However, insufficient depth constraints can assign one ground feature to different distances along a viewing direction, creating geometric ambiguity in BEV feature placement. Similar appearances at different locations can also create descriptor matching ambiguity, while existing descriptor learning lacks explicit semantic supervision to distinguish them. We propose GeoSem-BEV, a geometry-semantic constrained BEV representation learning method. Radial depth supervision constrains distance assignment, and vertical height supervision constrains height aggregation. Shared explicit semantic supervision promotes consistent semantic predictions across views and helps distinguish locations with similar semantics. These constraints improve feature placement and descriptor discriminability, enhancing state-of-the-art BEV localization models. On VIGOR with unknown orientation, GeoSem-BEV reduces mean orientation error by 37.2% and 38.1% in the cross-area and same-area settings, respectively. The corresponding errors are reduced by 10.8% and 15.6% on DReSS-D. On KITTI-CVL, it reduces same-area mean orientation error by 26.8% under 10 degree orientation noise.

</details>


### [82] [GeoGAT: Bidirectional Temporal Sampling Meets Hierarchical Graph Attention for Global Video Geo-localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39128)
*Junchao Cui,Xuanzi Ma,Wenqi Shi,Hangyu Li,Biru Zhu,Chong Fu,Xiangyang Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoGAT，用双向时序采样+图注意力网络解决视频地理定位中的层级冲突问题，并在四个地理层级上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频地理定位方法多采用单向均匀采样处理视频帧，并为每个地理层级（城市、州/省、国家、大洲）训练独立分类器，导致关键地理线索丢失以及层级间预测冲突，在复杂的多镜头剪辑视频中尤为严重。

Method: GeoGAT将双向时序采样与图注意力网络（GAT）结合：提取前向和偏移反转的帧序列以构建互补的时空特征；将融合特征输入预定义的地理层级图，由GAT进行结构感知的消息传递；同时用双约束机制剪枝预测以消除跨层级冲突。此外构建了GeoGAT10k基准，包含来自全球166个城市的9,720个多镜头剪辑视频。

Result: 在CityGuessr68k和GeoGAT10k上，GeoGAT完全消除了层级冲突，并在四个地理层级上均达到SOTA。在CityGuessr68k上，城市层级比最强基线（分类与检索协议下）高出2.6个百分点；在更具挑战性的GeoGAT10k上，准确率提升超过24个百分点。

Conclusion: GeoGAT通过双向采样与图注意力结构建模有效解决了层级冲突与线索丢失问题，验证了其对复杂真实场景的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Global video geo-localization aims to infer the geographic location of a video worldwide, evaluating performance across four geographic hierarchies: city, state/province, country, and continent. Existing methods typically employ one-way uniform sampling to process video frames and train independent classifiers for each hierarchy, which leads to the loss of key geographic cues and prediction conflicts between hierarchies, especially for complex multi-shot edited videos. To address these limitations, we propose GeoGAT, which integrates bidirectional temporal sampling with graph attention networks (GATs). Specifically, GeoGAT extracts forward and offset-reversed frame sequences to construct complementary spatiotemporal features. These fused features are then fed into a predefined geographical hierarchy graph, where GATs perform structure-aware message passing, while a dual-constraint mechanism prunes predictions to eliminate cross-hierarchy conflicts. We construct GeoGAT10k, comprising 9,720 multi-shot edited videos from 166 cities worldwide, specifically to benchmark generalization ability on complex video structures. Experimental results on CityGuessr68k and GeoGAT10k demonstrate that GeoGAT eliminates hierarchical conflicts entirely and achieves state-of-the-art performance across all four geographic hierarchies. On CityGuessr68k, GeoGAT outperforms the strongest baseline, evaluated under both classification and retrieval protocols, by 2.6 percentage points at the city level. On the more challenging GeoGAT10k with multi-shot edited videos, the accuracy improvement exceeds 24 percentage points, validating strong generalization to complex real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [83] [Perceptual Color Difference Modeling Using Machine Learning and Human Similarity Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39130)
*Elnara Kadyrgali,Muragul Muratbekova,Adilet Yerkin,Nuray Toganas,Ayan Igali,Malika Ziyada,Aruzhan Burambekova,Jamaladdin Hasanov,Pakizar Shamoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究基于人类相似度判断数据，比较不同颜色表示（RGB、HSI、COLIBRI）与回归算法对色差估计的影响，发现颜色表示比算法选择更重要，COLIBRI结合LightGBM效果最佳（R²=0.703）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有色差指标（如CIEDE2000）旨在逼近人类感知，但仍与主观判断存在不一致。研究希望探索直接基于人类评价的数据驱动色差估计方法。

Method: 收集2,000个系统生成颜色对的相似度判断，每个由7名观察者用四点序数量表评分。用RGB通道差异、HSI差异和COLIBRI模糊语言类别等表示训练回归模型，并比较五种回归算法。

Result: 颜色模型选择对预测性能的影响大于回归算法选择。仅用COLIBRI特征时线性回归R²为0.595，优于RGB（0.479）和HSI（0.493）。LightGBM结合组合表示取得最佳性能，R²为0.703。

Conclusion: 在数值颜色坐标之外补充分级感知类别，能更好地捕捉人类感知色差，显示数据驱动模型在感知对齐色差估计方面的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate assessment of color differences is essential for applications ranging from digital design to quality control. While existing color difference metrics, such as CIEDE2000, aim to approximate human perception, they may still exhibit inconsistencies with perceptual judgments. In this study, we investigate a data-driven approach to color-difference estimation based directly on human evaluations. We collect similarity judgments for 2,000 systematically generated color pairs, each rated by seven observers using a four-point ordinal scale. These judgments are then used to train regression models using different color representations, including RGB channel differences, HSI differences, and COLIBRI fuzzy linguistic categories. Experiments with five regression algorithms show that the choice of color model has a greater influence on prediction performance than the choice of regression algorithm. Using COLIBRI features alone, linear regression achieves an R2 of 0.595, outperforming RGB and HSI representations, which achieve R2 values of 0.479 and 0.493, respectively. The best performance is obtained by LightGBM using the combined representation, reaching an R2 of 0.703. The results indicate that human perceptual color differences are better captured when numerical color coordinates are complemented by graded perceptual categories, highlighting the potential of data-driven models for perceptually aligned color-difference estimation.

</details>


### [84] [Uncertainty-Aware Consistency Distillation for Few-Step Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39132)
*Lingyu Liu,Yaxiong Wang,Li Zhu,Zhedong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出不确定性感知一致性蒸馏（UACD），按时空区域重加权一致性监督，实现4步视频生成，VBench 2.0达0.556。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步视频生成器采样步数多、延迟与算力开销大，需蒸馏为少步学生模型；而现有一致性蒸馏中，教师提供的目标并非同等可靠，随时间快速变化的区域（如移动的树叶阴影、流水）更难学习。

Method: 观察到监督可靠性取决于内容的局部难度而非语义复杂度：变化小的区域端点预测一致，时序变化大的区域偏差大且与最大感知误差重合。据此构建两条独立扰动的教师引导一致性路径，以其学生端点预测形成一致性共识目标，学生直接预测与该目标的偏差作为不确定性代理；对高不确定性区域用指数权重放松一致性惩罚，其余区域保留完整惩罚。并结合特征空间对抗训练与语义对齐，用参数高效的LoRA对50步Wan模型适配。

Result: 在VBench 2.0上取得4步生成的SOTA（平均分0.556），并在用户研究中优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 用局部、无参数的不确定性估计重加权一致性监督，可在激进降步下保持感知质量，验证了监督可靠性随内容局部难度变化的观点。

Abstract: We study few-step video generation, i.e., distilling a multi-step video generator, which typically requires tens of sampling steps, incurring substantial latency and compute, into a few-step student. Consistency distillation is a common recipe, in which a multi-step teacher provides the consistency targets for a few-step student. However, these teacher-guided targets are not equally trustworthy, and the content is harder to learn where it varies rapidly over time, e.g., moving foliage shadows or flowing water. We observe that supervision reliability follows the local difficulty of the content rather than semantic complexity: regions that change little yield consistent endpoint predictions, whereas regions with large temporal variation produce larger discrepancies that coincide with the largest perceptual errors. Motivated by this observation, we propose Uncertainty-Aware Consistency Distillation (UACD), which reweights consistency supervision at each spatiotemporal region using a local, parameter-free uncertainty estimate. Specifically, we construct two independently perturbed teacher-guided consistency paths, whose student endpoint predictions provide a consensus target; the discrepancy between the student's direct prediction and this target is the uncertainty proxy. We then relax the consistency penalty on high-uncertainty regions through an exponential weight, while keeping the full penalty elsewhere, since the student cannot be expected to match targets that are hard to learn. To preserve perceptual quality under aggressive step reduction, we integrate feature-space adversarial training with semantic alignment. With parameter-efficient LoRA adaptation of the 50-step Wan model, our method achieves state-of-the-art 4-step generation on VBench 2.0 (0.556 mean score) and is preferred over competing methods in a user study.

</details>


### [85] [Feature-Aware Token Attack for Compression-Triggered Stealthy Failures in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39134)
*Shilinlu Yan,Bowen Chen,Yuechen Zhang,Zhenhong Zhou,Li Sun,Sen Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Feature-Aware Token Attack (FATA)，通过耦合注意力抑制与基于余弦相似度的特征保持，构造在完整 token 推理下正确、但在视觉 token 压缩后失效的对抗图像。在 LLaVA-1.5-7B 上，FATA 实现 96.3% 完整 token 精度保持与 22.1% 条件致盲率，优于 CAA 的 89.8% 和 15.7%，且检测率最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉 token 压缩可提升大型视觉语言模型效率，但会暴露完整 token 评估所遗漏的失败模式。作者希望研究一类新型对抗样本：在完整 token 推理下保持正确，却在压缩后引发错误，从而揭示压缩推理路径的额外脆弱性。

Method: 提出 FATA（Feature-Aware Token Attack）。该方法在固定的干净图像显著 token 集合上，将注意力抑制与基于余弦相似度的特征保持相结合。在主实验 LLaVA-1.5-7B 设置中，FATA 仅使用视觉编码器梯度，不需要访问部署的压缩器、token 预算或下游任务。

Result: 在四个视觉依赖任务子集、四种压缩器及受控重建协议下，FATA 达到 96.3% 的完整 token 精度保持率（SR）和 22.1% 的条件致盲率（CBR），而 CAA 分别为 89.8% 和 15.7%。消融实验支持两个目标在平衡压缩路径失败与完整 token 保持中的作用。在 5% 假阳性率下，FATA 在三种检测器中的测得检测率是四种攻击中最低的。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，对抗鲁棒性评估应同时在完整 token 与压缩推理两种路径下进行，而非仅依赖完整 token 评估。

Abstract: Visual-token compression improves the efficiency of large vision-language models, but can expose failures that full-token evaluation misses. We study adversarial images that preserve full-token correctness yet induce errors after compression, even when both inference paths succeed on the clean image. Creating such failures is challenging because perturbing token importance can also damage the visual content needed for full-token inference. We propose Feature-Aware Token Attack (FATA), which couples attention suppression with cosine-based feature preservation on a fixed set of salient clean-image tokens. In the primary LLaVA-1.5-7B setting, FATA uses only vision-encoder gradients, without access to the deployed compressor, token budget, or downstream task. Across four visually dependent task subsets and four compressors under a controlled reconstruction protocol, FATA achieves SR = 96.3% full-token accuracy retention and CBR = 22.1% conditional blinding, compared with 89.8% and 15.7% for CAA. Ablations support the role of both objectives in balancing compressed-path failure against full-token preservation. FATA also has the lowest measured detection rate among four attacks across three evaluated detectors at a 5% false-positive rate. These findings motivate assessing adversarial robustness jointly across full-token and compressed inference.

</details>


### [86] [Asking the World: Generalist Physical Reasoning through Agentic World Modeling and Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39135)
*Shenxiang Zeng,Chen Yang,Peiyao Chen,Guohui Zhang,Jiansheng Fan,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ATW 通用智能体，通过自适应地构建并探测可执行世界模型来完成视频物理推理，在多个数据集与真实场景上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频物理推理需要推断观测之外的潜在物理属性与动态。直接使用 VLM 推理在复杂物理任务上不可靠，而预定义工具流程依赖任务与领域先验，泛化能力受限。

Method: ATW 包含两个自适应阶段：World Modeling 从视频中校准世界，World Probing 对世界进行查询、模拟与干预以获取与问题相关的证据。配套构建基于 Warp 的可编程多物理引擎 PolyWorld Engine，支持刚体、软体、布料、绳索、流体及其耦合交互，并用基于 CEM 的系统辨识恢复任务相关动态。

Result: 在 CLEVRER 上达到 80.82% 逐题准确率，比直接 Gemini-3-Flash 高 46.50 点、比 GPT-5.5 高 13.58 点、比 PhysMind 高 8.27 点；ContPhy 上达 70.56%，超过 Gemini-3-Flash 28.10 点、GPT-5.5 3.53 点；三个真实场景平均 71.67%，高于 GPT-5.5 28.33 点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，智能体式的世界建模与探测是一种有效且以执行为基础的通用的物理推理方法。

Abstract: Physical reasoning from video requires inferring latent physical properties and dynamics beyond direct observation. Direct VLM inference remains unreliable on complex physical tasks without explicit modeling and validation, while predefined tool pipelines rely on task- and domain-specific priors that limit generalization across materials, dynamics, and reasoning tasks. We introduce Asking the World (ATW), a generalist agent that constructs and interrogates task-relevant executable worlds through two adaptive stages: World Modeling calibrates a world from video, while World Probing queries, simulates, and intervenes on it to obtain question-relevant evidence. Rather than prescribing the operations in either stage, ATW determines how to model and probe according to the scene and question. We develop PolyWorld Engine, a lightweight and highly programmable Warp-based multiphysics simulator for constructing and probing worlds with rigid bodies, soft bodies, cloth, ropes, fluids, and their coupled interactions. CEM-based system identification recovers task-relevant dynamics during World Modeling. The resulting world becomes an active workspace for question-directed physical experiments rather than a predetermined downstream tool. We evaluate ATW on CLEVRER, ContPhy, and three real-world scenarios. Using Gemini-3-Flash as its base VLM, ATW achieves 80.82% overall per-question accuracy on CLEVRER, improving direct Gemini-3-Flash by 46.50 points, GPT-5.5 by 13.58 points, and PhysMind by 8.27 points. On ContPhy, it reaches 70.56% overall accuracy, surpassing Gemini-3-Flash by 28.10 points and GPT-5.5 by 3.53 points. Across the three real-world scenarios, ATW achieves 71.67% accuracy, 28.33 points above GPT-5.5. These results establish agentic world modeling and probing as an effective, execution-grounded approach to generalist physical reasoning.

</details>


### [87] [When Can Text Replace Vision? Structural Bottlenecks in Diagram Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39142)
*Yunbei Zhang,Janet Wang,Jihun Hamm,Chandan K Reddy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种诊断协议，用于区分"文本化错误"是因为结构化表示丢失了问题所需信息，还是因为求解器未能利用已有信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化文本能否替代视觉进行图表推理尚不明确；错误答案可能源于表示缺失信息，也可能源于求解器未利用现有信息，需区分这两种原因。

Method: 在同一求解器与生成设置下比较三种输入条件：原始图像、由视觉语言模型提取的问题盲结构、由图源得到的金标准结构。采用有效性触发的恢复测试、问题相关保真度度量以及匹配边干预。

Result: 在240个公开FlowGen图表的保留测试集上，金标准结构准确率达87%，而直接视觉与学习文本均低于30%；仅重试无效提取可提升schema有效率但准确率几乎不变；问题相关拓扑比整图拓扑更能预测正确性；单条答案相关边编辑使准确率降至近零；提供结构比视觉更省token，但学习式获取会消除该优势。

Conclusion: 应通过所保留的答案相关证据及求解器对该表示的利用能力来评估获取文本，而非仅关注整体准确率。

Abstract: Can structured text replace vision for diagram reasoning? A wrong answer after textualization can arise because the representation omits information the question needs, or because the solver fails to use information that is present. We introduce a diagnostic protocol to distinguish these explanations. Using the same solver model and generation settings, we compare three input conditions: the original image, question-blind structure extracted by a vision-language model, or gold structure derived from the diagram source. Validity-triggered recovery tests truncation and schema failure, question-relevant fidelity measures preservation of answer-critical structure, and matched edge interventions test the effect of error location. On a reserved holdout of 240 public FlowGen diagrams, evaluated under a frozen protocol, gold structure reaches 87% accuracy while direct vision and learned text both remain below 30%. The aggregate comparison includes source-derived relation labels that may not be printed in the image and uses different learned and gold graph encodings, so it does not isolate extraction error alone. Retrying only invalid extractions makes nearly every public representation schema-valid yet leaves accuracy essentially unchanged. The public learned-text deficit relative to gold more than doubles with structural difficulty. Question-relevant topology predicts correctness better than whole-graph topology. In an exposed intervention study, a single answer-relevant edge edit reduces the primary solver's original-answer accuracy to near zero, while matched irrelevant edits largely preserve it. Supplied structure requires fewer solving tokens than vision, but learned acquisition removes this advantage at single use. These comparisons motivate evaluating acquired text by the answer-relevant evidence it preserves and by the solver's ability to use that representation.

</details>


### [88] [MindWorldBench: Evaluating Mental-State-to-Behavior Reasoning in Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39147)
*Ruiqi Li,Xuanyi Liu,Sijia Li,Haofeng Wang,Yuxin Liu,Feng Xie,Songchao Tan,Shiqi Wang,Hanwei Zhu,Yizong Wang,Chuanmin Jia,Siwei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 MindWorldBench，用于评测视频生成模型在心理状态条件下的行为推理能力，发现模型虽具备视觉逼真度却难以对齐潜在心理状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前图像到视频模型在视觉真实性和物理合理性上表现良好，但尚未探索心理状态推理。行为由信念、欲望和感知驱动，需超越显式指令进行推断，因此需要专门评测模型在心理状态条件下的视频生成能力。

Method: 将任务形式化为“心理状态到行为”的推理，模型需从世界状态和潜在变量生成动作，且不提供显式动作提示。采用零动作提示与反事实设计，构建 744 条提示以隔离心理状态的因果效应，并用自动化流程评估视频质量、常识合理性和心理状态一致性。

Result: 对 11 个模型的评测显示，尽管模型具备视觉保真度和物理推理能力，却无法使行为与潜在心理状态对齐。研究发现一种“全知偏差”失败模式，即模型默认采用客观世界状态而非人类主观信念。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了视觉生成与认知推理之间的脱节，表明视频生成系统需要显式的心理状态建模。项目网站：https://richard2049-lee.github.io/MindWorldBench/

Abstract: Current image-to-video models achieve visual realism and physical plausibility, but reasoning about mental states remains unexplored. Actions are driven by belief, desire, and perception, requiring inference beyond explicit instructions. We introduce MindWorldBench to evaluate mental-state-conditioned video generation. We formalize this as mental-state-to-behavior reasoning, where models generate actions from a world state and latent variables without explicit action prompts. MindWorldBench utilizes Zero-Action Prompting and a counterfactual design with 744 prompts to isolate the causal effects of mental states. An automated pipeline evaluates video quality, commonsense plausibility, and mental-state consistency. Evaluations of 11 models show that despite visual fidelity and physical reasoning, models fail to align behaviors with latent mental states. We identify a failure mode, termed Omniscient Bias, where models default to the objective world state rather than human's subjective belief. These results demonstrate a disconnect between visual generation and cognitive reasoning, suggesting a need for explicit mental-state modeling in video generation systems. Project website: https://richard2049-lee.github.io/MindWorldBench/

</details>


### [89] [OP-CAD: On-Policy Clean-Audio Distillation for Robust Audio-Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39150)
*Xingming Shui,Dapeng Chen,Bowei Liu,Jingqi Tian,Minfu Li,Kun Yi,Jiapeng Hong,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OP-CAD 是一种基于课程式特权自蒸馏的框架，通过选择性 token 级监督提升音视频大模型在噪声与竞争语音下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境中的全模态大模型会受到外部噪声干扰，影响对多模态输入的理解。现有 on-policy 蒸馏虽能提供密集反馈，但均匀的 token 加权无法优先关注受声学干扰影响的预测位置。

Method: 提出 OP-CAD：训练从轻微到严重的环境噪声与竞争语音逐步推进，并在每个阶段进行选择性 token 级监督。学生模型基于受损音视频输入生成回答，冻结教师模型使用干净音频与已验证答案监督相同回答前缀。通过比较教师在干净、受损、纯视觉上下文下的预测来衡量对音频移除与损坏的敏感性，并用有界加权规则强调相应位置。

Result: OP-CAD 在所有评估的噪声条件下均优于对比方法；配对分析显示在强干扰下能更好保留原本干净条件下正确的回答，且未观察到整体干净准确率下降。

Conclusion: 将干净教师监督定向到声学敏感预测位置，有助于实现鲁棒的音视频推理。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models deployed in real-world environments encounter external noise that can interfere with their perception and understanding of multimodal inputs. We study their robustness in audio-visual understanding, focusing on question answering under environmental noise and competing speech. The challenge is to resist acoustic interference while preserving useful audio evidence. On-policy distillation provides dense teacher feedback on student-generated responses, but uniform token weighting does not explicitly prioritize positions affected by acoustic interference. We introduce OP-CAD (On-Policy Clean-Audio Distillation), a curriculum-based privileged self-distillation framework for robust audio-visual understanding. Training progresses from mild to severe environmental noise and competing speech, with selective token-level supervision at each stage. The student generates responses from corrupted audio-visual input, while a frozen teacher uses clean audio and the verified answer to supervise the same response prefixes. To allocate this supervision, OP-CAD compares teacher predictions under clean, corrupted, and visual-only contexts without revealing the answer. These matched comparisons measure sensitivity to audio removal and corruption; a bounded weighting rule emphasizes positions identified by either signal while retaining supervision throughout the response. OP-CAD outperforms the compared methods across all evaluated noise conditions. Paired analyses further show improved preservation of clean-correct answers under strong interference, with no observed aggregate clean-accuracy penalty. These results demonstrate the value of directing clean-teacher supervision toward acoustically sensitive predictions for robust audio-visual reasoning.

</details>


### [90] [TripleFlow: Training-Free Video Object Removal by Bridging Residual Editing and Native Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39157)
*Songhe Wang,Lifu Wei,Shuolin Xu,Charles A. Kamhoua,David Miller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TripleFlow是一个无需训练的视频物体移除框架，通过协调源流、残差流和合成流紧密耦合擦除与生成，实现时空一致的无缝背景补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频物体移除任务中，移除提示只说明书要擦除什么而非要生成什么，模型必须从周围上下文推断并重建被遮挡的特定背景。现有免训练方法的编辑机制主要充当局部擦除器，无法主动合成缺失的背景细节，并会留下鬼影伪影。

Method: 提出TripleFlow，一种无需训练的框架，将擦除与生成紧密耦合。它全程协调源流、残差流和合成流：复用单一目标预测，残差流负责隔离并抑制物体，合成流独立重建被遮挡背景。关键之处在于，TripleFlow在每个步骤将新合成的背景注入编辑轨迹，形成连续反馈循环。

Result: 在五个具有挑战性的基准测试上的广泛评估表明，TripleFlow建立了新的最先进水平，在重建保真度和时间一致性方面均显著优于现有基线。

Conclusion: TripleFlow通过持续反馈循环让生成的背景结构主动引导移除过程，实现与未编辑场景在时空上一致的无缝补全，验证了耦合擦除与生成这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: Video object removal presents a uniquely difficult editing challenge. Because a removal prompt specifies only what to erase rather than what to generate, the model must infer and reconstruct a highly specific occluded background entirely from the surrounding context. Existing training-free methods struggle with this because their editing mechanisms act primarily as localized erasers. They fail to actively synthesize the missing background details and often leave behind ghosting artifacts. To solve this, we propose TripleFlow, a training-free framework that tightly couples erasure and generation. It coordinates a source flow, a residual flow, and a synthesis flow throughout the entire process. By reusing a single target prediction, the residual flow isolates and suppresses the object, while the synthesis flow independently reconstructs the occluded background. Crucially, TripleFlow injects this newly synthesized background back into the editing trajectory at every step. This continuous feedback loop ensures that the generated structures actively guide the removal process, achieving seamless completion that is spatiotemporally consistent with the unedited scene. Extensive evaluations across five challenging benchmarks demonstrate that TripleFlow establishes a new state-of-the-art, significantly outperforming existing baselines in both reconstruction fidelity and temporal consistency.

</details>


### [91] [MEND: Label-Free Detection, Localisation, and Correction of Latent Hallucination in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39182)
*Ali J Alrasheed,Aryan Yazdan Parast,Basim Azam,James Bailey,Naveed Akhtar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MEND，用单一条件得分网络在推理时检测、定位并纠正潜空间世界模型中的幻觉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型成为计算机视觉新前沿，但其鲁棒性尚未被充分探索。作者发现潜空间世界模型存在"幻觉"现象：给定状态与动作，预测的下一潜变量可能解码为从未出现过的场景；由于该预测在统计上显得平常且被自回归反馈，误差既隐蔽又会累积。核心问题是：在冻结的自监督世界模型、且没有真实误差标签的情况下，能否在推理时检测、定位并纠正这种潜空间幻觉。

Method: 提出 Masked Empirical-Bayes Neural Denoising（MEND）：一个通过去噪得分匹配在真实转移动态上训练的单条件得分网络。其得分场承担三个角色——幅度用于检测幻觉，逐 token 的场用于将误差定位到具体图像块，并定义一个推理时的纠正方向。

Result: 在两个导航环境中，MEND 在不使用动作信息的情况下检测幻觉的 AUROC 最高达 0.80，超过单高斯密度基线；同时可实现误差定位（逐 token AUPRC 最高 0.87）并进行纠正。纠正能稳定降低单步潜变量误差并改进预测。

Conclusion: 作者发现部分误差与数据流形相切，因此当前重点放在检测与定位上，同时指出纠正方向具有潜力。单一得分场即可同时完成检测、定位与纠正。

Abstract: World Models are appearing as the next major frontier in computer vision. However, their robustness is currently largely unexplored. We identify the phenomenon of hallucination in latent World Models: given a state and an action, the predicted next latent can decode to a scene that never occurs. Because the prediction is statistically ordinary and is fed back autoregressively by the model, the error is both silent and compounding. We study whether such latent hallucination can be detected, localised, and corrected at inference time, on a frozen self-supervised world model in the absence of ground-truth error labels. We introduce Masked Empirical-Bayes Neural Denoising (MEND), a single conditional score network trained by denoising score matching on real transitions, whose score field serves three roles: its magnitude detects hallucination, its per-token field localises it to specific image patches, and it defines an inference-time correction direction. On two navigation environments MEND detects hallucination with an AUROC of up to 0.80 without using actions, exceeding a single-Gaussian density baseline while also localising the error (per-token AUPRC up to 0.87) and correcting it, all from one score field. Our correction reliably reduces single-step latent error and improves predictions. We identify that a part of the error is tangent to the data manifold, hence, we focus on detection and localisation while highlighting promises of the correction.

</details>


### [92] [Aligning Thoughts with Answers: Probability Rewards to Tame Thinking Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39183)
*Pengzhan Sun,Shiu-hong Kao,Shijie Li,Yongyi Su,Junbin Xiao,Arjun Reddy Akula,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言模型中的"思维-答案一致性"，针对视觉意图定位任务中IoU强化学习框架存在的"思维漂移"问题，提出新的RL范式Rita。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前基于IoU的强化学习框架存在"思维漂移"：模型虽给出正确边界框，但推理过程指向了不同的目标物体，即推理与答案不一致。

Method: Rita（ReInforcing Thinking-Answer consistency）引入两种无需推理标签的RL奖励：思维奖励与一致性奖励，均基于参考答案的条件概率构建；并采用难度感知的数据过滤策略，利用rollout错误率和奖励方差筛选易到中等难度的信息量样本用于RL。

Result: 在EgoIntention及新提出的RefEgo-Int基准上的大量实验表明，Rita持续优于监督微调方法和普通RL微调框架。

Conclusion: Rita作为新的强化学习范式，有效缓解思维漂移，提升视觉意图定位中的思维-答案一致性，并在多个基准上取得更优表现。

Abstract: This paper studies \textbf{thinking--answer consistency} in vision-language models. We focus on Visual Intention Grounding, where a model infers a target object based on a human intention query and predicts a bounding box. We reveal that previous IoU-based reinforcement learning (RL) frameworks suffer from ``thinking drift'', where the model produces a correct bounding box, despite having an incorrect reasoning process pointing to a different target object. Thus, we propose \textbf{Rita} (\textit{ReInforcing Thinking--Answer consistency}) as a novel RL paradigm to tame the drift. Specifically, Rita introduces two reasoning-label-free RL rewards, constructed from the conditional probability of reference answers: a \textbf{thinking reward} and a \textbf{consistency reward}. It also adopts a difficulty-aware \textbf{data filtering} strategy that selects informative easy-to-medium samples for RL using rollout error rate and reward variance. Extensive experiments on EgoIntention and the new RefEgo-Int benchmarks show that Rita performs consistently superior to the supervised finetuning approaches and vanilla RL-finetuned frameworks.

</details>


### [93] [Fiber-Resolved Microstructure Quantification from Multi-Shell Diffusion MRI using Detection Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39184)
*Sebastian Endt,Marcus Wirth,Johannes Reinhold Schlund,Marion Irene Menzel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将白质纤维方向与隔室微结构联合估计问题重新定义为类目标检测任务，采用DETR架构从标准多壳扩散MRI中预测每个体素可变数量隔室的MD、FA、主纤维方向与信号分数，并用匈牙利匹配解决隔室置换不变性，引入mAP作为可复现基准指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散MRI方法存在局限：要么只解析纤维方向而不量化微结构，要么在假设固定隔室数量和单一纤维方向的前提下量化微结构。能够同时恢复二者的非参数方法需要张量值扩散编码，并且要对病态逆拉普拉斯变换进行计算昂贵的蒙特卡洛反演。因此需要一种更高效、可从常规多壳线性编码数据中联合估计方向与微结构的方法。

Method: 作者将该问题重构为类似目标检测的任务，采用Detection Transformer（DETR）架构，从标准多壳扩散MRI（线性编码）中联合预测每个体素中可变数量隔室的平均弥散度（MD）、分数各向异性（FA）、主纤维方向和信号分数。训练时使用匈牙利匹配来处理隔室之间的置换不变性。同时引入平均精度均值（mAP）作为可复现的基准评价指标。

Result: 在最多包含五个隔室每体素的合成测试数据上评估，模型达到MD的R²=0.95、FA的R²=0.88，以及4.2°的中位角度误差，且性能随隔室信号分数自然变化。

Conclusion: 将纤维方向与微结构联合估计建模为类目标检测任务，并借助DETR架构与匈牙利匹配，可以在仅使用标准多壳线性编码扩散MRI的情况下有效预测可变数量隔室的微结构与方向参数，避免了对张量值编码和昂贵蒙特卡洛反演的需求，同时mAP为评估提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: Fiber orientation and compartmental microstructure are central to the characterization of white matter tissue in diffusion MRI, yet existing methods either resolve fiber orientations without quantifying microstructure, or quantify microstructure while assuming a fixed number of compartments and a single fiber direction. Nonparametric approaches that recover both require tensor-valued diffusion encoding and computationally expensive Monte-Carlo inversion of an ill-posed inverse Laplace transform. We propose to reframe this problem as an object detection-like task, adopting the Detection Transformer (DETR) architecture to jointly predict mean diffusivity (MD), fractional anisotropy (FA), main fiber direction, and signal fraction for a variable number of compartments per voxel from standard multi-shell diffusion MRI with linear encoding. Hungarian matching during training resolves permutation invariance across compartments. We introduce mean Average Precision as a reproducible benchmark metric. Evaluated on synthetic test data with up to five compartments per voxel, our model achieves $R^2=0.95$ for MD, $R^2=0.88$ for FA, and a median angular error of 4.2°, with performance scaling naturally with compartmental signal fraction.

</details>


### [94] [Uruqi: Learning Spatial Cognition from Visual Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39195)
*Shichao Li,Meiqi Wang,Fei Su,Zhicheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过在连续视觉体验中提供密集多轮监督，合成大量相机轨迹数据，提升视觉语言模型的自我运动跟踪与世界映射等原子空间能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型虽经空间后训练提升了空间智能，但在跟踪自身运动和运动中映射周围世界这两个原子空间能力上仍存在不足。

Method: 在每个训练片段内对交织的原子能力提供密集多轮监督，模拟持续运动智能体的视觉体验；合成了11,738条动机驱动的相机轨迹，覆盖多种3D场景，支持自我运动跟踪、持续物体映射和丰富空间操作。

Result: URUQI_Syn-8B在包含52k问题、2.7k片段的Uruqi基准上准确率从15.84%提升至47.73%；URUQI-SI-Mix-8B达到50.41%，与GPT-6 Astra的50.08%相当；仅用合成数据训练的URUQI_Syn-8B在三个外部空间基准上平均相对准确率较其InternVL3-8B骨干提升17.13%。

Conclusion: 连续视觉体验是发展视觉语言模型空间认知的可扩展监督来源。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence requires maintaining a coherent understanding of the world as the embodied agent moves. Like humans, the agent must use its own motion to interpret changes across observations and update object locations and spatial relations accordingly. Despite spatial post-training having substantially broadened the spatial intelligence of vision-language models (VLMs), they still struggle with two atomic spatial capabilities: tracking self-motion and mapping the surrounding world during motion. To address this gap, we provide dense multi-turn supervision over interleaved atomic capabilities within each training episode, mimicking the visual experience of a continuously moving agent that reasons as it observes. To scale this up, we synthesize 11,738 motif-driven camera trajectories over a broad range of 3D scenes, supporting self-motion tracking, persistent object mapping, and rich spatial operations within each visual experience. By training models to reason over these atomic questions, our URUQI$_{\mathrm{Syn}}$-8B improves accuracy from 15.84% to 47.73% on our Uruqi benchmark comprising 52k questions across 2.7k episodes. URUQI-SI-Mix-8B further reaches 50.41%, comparable to the 50.08% achieved by GPT-6 Astra. Trained solely on our synthesized data, URUQI$_{\mathrm{Syn}}$-8B achieves an average relative accuracy improvement of 17.13% over its InternVL3-8B backbone across three external spatial benchmarks. These results highlight continuous visual experience as a scalable source of supervision for developing spatial cognition in VLMs.

</details>


### [95] [DC-SAE: Deep Compression Semantic Autoencoder for Faster Diffusion Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39222)
*Xu Huang,Ye Huang,Zijun Liao,Yuwei Niu,Xiaojie Li,Menghan Zhou,De Wen Soh,Xiaotong Li,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DC-SAE解耦紧凑语义自编码器，实现高压缩比与快速扩散训练的统一，在ImageNet 512×512上达到32×空间压缩，PSNR 29.79、gFID 3.37。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压缩tokenizer对扩展潜空间图像生成模型至关重要，但激进压缩带来重建保真度与生成效率的根本性权衡：高压缩编码器加剧扩散训练难度、收敛缓慢。现有表征自编码器通过用预训练语义编码器替换VAE编码器提升潜特征表达能力以加速训练，但通常局限于中等压缩比，并丢失忠实重建所需的像素级细节。因此需要同时实现高压缩与快速扩散训练。

Method: 提出DC-SAE（Decoupled Compact Semantic Autoencoder，解耦紧凑语义自编码器），包含两个关键组件：(1) 宏观架构设计，利用语义编码器支撑更高的压缩比；(2) 像素级编码器，保留低层细节以保证高保真图像重建。

Result: 在ImageNet 512×512分辨率上，DC-SAE实现32×空间压缩，PSNR 29.79、gFID 3.37，在PSNR和gFID上分别超越此前最优高压缩tokenizer基线DC-AE达13.5%和54.9%，同时保持相当吞吐量并具有更快的扩散模型训练收敛。在文本到图像生成任务上，使用DC-SAE的1.6B参数DiT在1024×1024分辨率下于GenEval取得0.84、DPG-Bench取得86.007。

Conclusion: DC-SAE通过解耦语义编码与像素级细节编码，成功兼顾紧凑潜表示与高效训练动态，在图像生成任务上表现强劲，验证了高压缩与快速扩散训练可以兼得。

Abstract: High-compression tokenizers are essential for scaling latent image generative models. However, aggressive compression creates a fundamental tradeoff between reconstruction fidelity and generation efficiency: high compression image encoder always increases the learning difficulty of diffusion training, resulting in slow model convergence. Recent representation autoencoders speed up the diffusion training by improving the latent feature's expressive capability by replacing VAE encoders with pretrained semantic encoders, yet they are typically limited to moderate compression and lose pixel-level details necessary for faithful reconstruction. To achieve both high compression and fast diffusion training, we propose DC-SAE, a Decoupled Compact Semantic Autoencoder designed for high-compression image generation with accelerated diffusion model convergence. DC-SAE consists of two key components: (1) a macro-level architecture design that leverages semantic encoders to enable higher compression ratios, and (2) a pixel-level encoder that preserves low-level details, ensuring high-fidelity image reconstruction. We empirically demonstrate that DC-SAE performs strongly on image generation tasks, achieving both compact latent representations and efficient training dynamics. Specifically, on the ImageNet dataset with $512 \times 512$ resolution, DC-SAE achieves $32\times$ spatial compression, with 29.79 PSNR and 3.37 gFID, substantially outperforming the previous state-of-the-art high-compression tokenizer baselines DC-AE by 13.5% and 54.9% on PSNR and gFID, respectively, maintaining comparable throughput and faster diffusion model training convergence. Beyond class-conditional generation, a $1.6$B-parameter DiT using DC-SAE achieves 0.84 on GenEval and 86.007 on DPG-Bench for text-to-image generation at $1024\times1024$ resolution.

</details>


### [96] [Emergent Multi-View Geometry Through Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39227)
*David Nordström,Thibaut Loiseau,Vincent Lepetit,Michael Felsberg,Guillaume Bourmaud,Fredrik Kahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出Poincar3，一种无需RGB重建的自监督多视角表示学习方法，通过自蒸馏（掩码块与图像级蒸馏结合观察额外视角的教师模型）从零训练，在对应估计、相机位姿估计与3D重建上超越DINOv3、MuM、Muskie等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Poincaré关于静止观察者无法获得空间概念的论断启发，指出多数视觉表示学习方法基于单张图像，而多视角方法依赖RGB重建，会将几何与外观纠缠在一起，因此需要一种不依赖RGB重建、能更好学习几何与相机运动信息的多视角自监督方法。

Method: 提出Poincar3，从多视角通过自蒸馏学习表示而非RGB重建；结合掩码块级蒸馏与图像级蒸馏，并使用观察额外视角的教师模型；无需显式3D监督即可从头训练；同时采用轻量级Poincaré适配器。

Result: 在对应估计、相机位姿估计和3D重建任务上，Poincar3优于DINOv3、MuM、Muskie等以往单视角与多视角自监督方法；通过轻量级Poincaré适配器发现所学特征比现有自监督表示更准确地编码相机运动。

Conclusion: 不依赖RGB重建的多视角自蒸馏可学到更强的几何与相机运动表示，为视觉表示学习提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Over a century ago, Henri Poincaré argued that a motionless observer cannot acquire the notion of space. Yet, most visual representation learning methods operate on individual images, while those that leverage multiple views rely on RGB reconstruction, entangling geometry with appearance. We propose Poincar3, a self-supervised method that learns representations from multiple views through self-distillation instead of RGB reconstruction. We combine masked patch and image-level distillation with a teacher that observes additional views, enabling training from scratch without explicit 3D supervision. Poincar3 outperforms both previous single and multi-view self-supervised approaches such as DINOv3, MuM, and Muskie on correspondence estimation, camera pose estimation, and 3D reconstruction. Using a lightweight Poincaré adapter, we also find that our learned features encode camera motion more accurately than existing self-supervised representations.

</details>


### [97] [Universal Cross-Prompt Adversarial Attacks on Promptable Concept Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39265)
*Ziqi Zhou,Yifan Hu,Yufei Song,Haowen Jiang,Xianlong Wang,Shengshan Hu,Dezhong Yao,Leo Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 AdvPCS，一种针对提示式概念分割（PCS）模型的通用跨提示对抗攻击方法，通过极小极大提示优化、全局-局部感知欺骗和时间转移偏差攻击，生成单一通用对抗扰动，在多种提示（点、框、文本）下都能有效降低 SAM3 等 PCS 模型的分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM3 将可提示分割扩展到概念级预测，但现有研究仅揭示 SAM 和 SAM2 对对抗样本的脆弱性，SAM3 在概念分割范式下的鲁棒性尚未被探索，且已有针对 SAM 系列的攻击跨提示迁移能力有限。

Method: 提出 AdvPCS 通用跨提示对抗攻击，包含三个组件：(1) 极小极大提示优化策略，通过双层优化先以内层最大化在候选点、框、文本提示上增强多样性，再以外层最小化依据检测器置信度选择响应最高的最难攻击提示；(2) 全局-局部感知欺骗攻击，在联合提示下最小化全局和局部存在概率；(3) 时间转移偏差攻击，最大化帧间语义不一致并破坏记忆指针。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法生成的单一通用对抗扰动可跨不同视频帧泛化，在点、框和文本提示下均取得强攻击效果；特别在 SA-CO 数据集上，文本提示下将多种 PCS 模型的平均 mIoU 降至 5% 以下。

Conclusion: AdvPCS 首次系统研究了 SAM3 在概念分割范式下的对抗鲁棒性，证明单一通用扰动即可实现强跨提示、跨帧攻击，揭示了 PCS 模型存在的严重安全隐患。

Abstract: The Segment Anything Model (SAM) achieves remarkable performance in visual segmentation. The latest SAM3 extends promptable segmentation to concept-level prediction, broadening the scope of segmentation foundation models. While recent works reveal that SAM and SAM2 are vulnerable to adversarial examples, the robustness of SAM3 under the concept segmentation paradigm remains unexplored. In addition, existing adversarial attacks on SAM-series models exhibit limited cross-prompt transferability. To this end, we propose AdvPCS, a universal cross-prompt adversarial attack for Promptable Concept Segmentation (PCS), including a min-max prompt optimization strategy, a global-local perception deception attack, and a temporal transition deviation attack. Specifically, we first identify the hardest-to-attack prompts via min-max bilevel optimization. In the inner maximization, we enhance diversity over candidate point, box, and text prompts. In the outer minimization, we select prompts with the highest responses based on the confidence scores output by the detector. Given the selected prompts, we apply the perception deception attack to minimize both global and local existence probabilities under joint prompting and employ the temporal memory misalignment attack to maximize inter-frame semantic inconsistency and corrupt memory pointers. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets show that a single universal adversarial perturbation (UAP) generated by our method generalizes across frames from different videos and achieves strong attack performance under point, box, and text prompts. In particular, it reduces the average mIoU of various PCS models on the SA-CO dataset to below 5% under text prompts, demonstrating strong attack ability.

</details>


### [98] [PLRS-IC: A Dual-Calibration Framework for Chest X-Ray Vision-Language Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39266)
*Qixing Zhao,Jinpeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出PLRS-IC双重校准框架，解决胸片视觉-语言对齐中的投影视觉错配与语义重叠两大歧义，在九个零样本基准上提升分类、定位与分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度视觉-语言对齐可实现胸片零样本分类、定位与分割，但受两类歧义阻碍：一是局部层面，正位与侧位胸片对同一临床征象外观不同，共享的图块-文本相似度几何并非最优；二是全局对比优化层面，实例级目标将跨患者但共享相同阳性概念的样本对错误地当作严格负样本惩罚。

Method: 提出统一的PLRS-IC双重校准框架。局部对齐阶段采用投影条件低秩残差相似度（PLRS），通过有界、参数高效的低秩残差，使图块-文本匹配动态适配投影特定流形。全局优化阶段采用信息量校准的软假阴性抑制（IC-SFNS），利用语料导出的信息论先验，在不改变原始对比分配的前提下软化语义重叠负样本的惩罚。

Result: 在九个公开零样本基准设定上的大量实验表明，该框架在分类、定位和分割任务上均取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 验证了双重校准在医学视觉-语言预训练中的必要性。

Abstract: Fine-grained vision-language alignment in chest radiography enables zero-shot classification, grounding, and segmentation without task-specific annotations. However, this alignment is fundamentally hindered by two intertwined sources of ambiguity: projection-induced visual mismatch and patient-agnostic semantic overlap. First, at the local feature level, frontal and lateral radiographs exhibit distinct appearances for the same clinical finding, rendering a shared patch-text similarity geometry inherently suboptimal. Compounding this visual ambiguity is a semantic mismatch during global contrastive optimization, where instance-level objectives penalize cross-patient pairs as strict negatives even when they share identical positive clinical concepts. To address this dual ambiguity, we propose PLRS-IC, a unified dual-calibration framework for chest X-ray representation learning. At the local alignment stage, Projection-Conditioned Low-Rank Residual Similarity (PLRS) dynamically adapts patch-text matching to projection-specific manifolds using a bounded, parameter-efficient low-rank residual. At the global optimization stage, Information-Content-Calibrated Soft False-Negative Suppression (IC-SFNS) leverages a corpus-derived information-theoretic prior to soften the penalty of semantically overlapping negatives without altering original contrastive assignments. Extensive experiments across nine public zero-shot benchmark settings demonstrate that our framework yields consistent improvements in classification, grounding, and segmentation, validating the necessity of dual-calibration in medical vision-language pre-training.

</details>


### [99] [MegaAvatar: Controllable Talking Avatar Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39273)
*Junyao Gao,Sibo Liu,Weidong Zhang,Cairong Zhao,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MegaAvatar 是一个基于 Wan2.2-TI2V-5B 的可控说话数字人生成框架，通过引入 SMPL-X 3D 引导实现身体姿态与头部运动的全局控制，并辅以音频与人脸交叉注意力模块实现精细表情与身份保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的说话数字人方法主要依赖音频或参考图像进行条件控制，缺乏对整体身体姿态和头部运动的全局控制能力，难以实现身体层面的可控生成。

Method: 在 Wan2.2-TI2V-5B 基础上，将驱动用 SMPL-X 序列渲染为密集网格帧，用轻量级 3D 卷积编码器编码并注入潜变量 token 以提供运动控制；同时扩展音频与人脸交叉注意力模块，分别实现细粒度表情控制与输入身份保持；并训练音频到 SMPL-X 的预测模型，仅凭参考图像与音频即可预测 SMPL-X 序列，使框架支持无需用户提供 SMPL-X 的音频驱动推理。

Result: 实验表明，MegaAvatar 能够生成高质量说话数字人视频，具备可控的身体与头部运动、与语音同步的面部表情以及一致的身份保持，并支持灵活的分辨率与视频长度推理。

Conclusion: MegaAvatar 通过融合 SMPL-X 3D 引导与多模态交叉注意力，实现了兼具全局运动控制与细粒度表情控制的说话数字人生成，并提供音频驱动版本，相关代码、数据集与模型将开源。

Abstract: This report presents \textbf{MegaAvatar}, a controllable talking avatar generation framework built on top of the Wan2.2-TI2V-5B model. Compared with previous talking-avatar methods that mainly rely on audio or reference-image conditioning, we introduce additional SMPL-X-derived 3D guidance, enabling global control over body pose and head motion. Specifically, we render the driving SMPL-X sequence into dense mesh frames and encode them with a lightweight 3D convolutional encoder, whose outputs are injected into the latent tokens to provide overall motion control. Furthermore, we extend Wan2.2-TI2V-5B with additional audio and face cross-attention modules to enable fine-grained expression control and preserve the input identity, respectively. In addition, we implement an audio-to-SMPL-X model to predict an SMPL-X sequence conditioned on the reference image and input audio, allowing MegaAvatar to support audio-driven inference without user-provided SMPL-X frames. Experiments show that MegaAvatar achieves high-quality talking avatar generation with controllable body and head motion, speech-synchronized facial expressions, and consistent identity preservation. MegaAvatar also supports inference with flexible resolutions and video lengths. Codes, dataset, models will be avaliable in https://github.com/Jeoyal/MegaAvatar

</details>


### [100] [BMASH: Ball-Motion-Aware Soccer Header Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39300)
*Ahmed Endris Hasen,Muhammad Shahzad Khan,Nikolaos Passalis,Jenni Raitoharju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出足球头球定位任务并构建 BMASH 框架，在 Video Swin 基线基础上融合球检测得到的球在场与运动特征，在比赛级划分下提升了片段级 AP 与 ROC-AUC，全视频连续定位中取得相当的事件级 F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广播体育视频在事件分析与球员安全等应用中价值日益提升，但足球头球事件短暂且视觉特征细微，现有动作识别方法难以准确识别，头球定位仍具挑战。

Method: 首先将 Video Swin 适配为头球定位的强动作识别基线；随后提出 BMASH（球运动感知融合框架），将 Video Swin 的动作表征与帧级足球检测得到的球存在性及运动特征相结合，融合球员动作上下文与球的动态信息以区分头球与视觉相似事件。评估采用比赛级划分，含独立测试比赛与轮换验证折，涵盖中心窗口分类与全视频连续定位两种设定。

Result: Video Swin 提供了强基线；BMASH 在片段级 AP 和 ROC-AUC 上优于对应的 Video Swin 基线；在全视频连续定位中，BMASH 取得相当的事件级 F1，但呈现不同的精确率—召回率权衡。

Conclusion: 融合球运动信息的 BMASH 能有效提升足球头球定位性能，验证了结合球员动作上下文与球动态特征的有效性，并为该任务建立了可比基线。

Abstract: Recent advances in computer vision have made broadcast sports videos increasingly useful for event analysis, performance assessment, and player-safety applications. In soccer, however, header spotting remains a challenging problem due to the subtle and short-lived nature of header events. This paper focuses on soccer header spotting: identifying moments in broadcast videos where the ball contacts a player's head. We first adapt and evaluate Video Swin as a strong action-recognition baseline for this task, and then introduce BMASH, a ball-motion-aware fusion framework that integrates detector-derived ball features. BMASH combines Video Swin action representations with ball-presence and motion features from frame-level soccer-ball detection, integrating player-action context with ball dynamics to distinguish headers from visually similar events. We evaluate BMASH using game-level splits with separate test matches and rotating validation folds, considering both centered-window classification and continuous full-video spotting. Results show that Video Swin provides a strong baseline for header spotting, while BMASH improves clip-level AP and ROC-AUC over the corresponding Video Swin baseline. In continuous full-video spotting, BMASH achieves a comparable event-level F1-performance with a different precision--recall trade-off.

</details>


### [101] [Rethinking Generative Image Compression at Extremely Low Bitrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39315)
*Tianyu Zhang,Zhaoyang Jia,Houqiang Li,Dong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究生成式图像压缩在极低码率下的行为，发现现有编解码器会出现"语义崩溃"。作者提出 RAE-CoD，在表示自编码器空间中进行扩散压缩，直接对齐压缩与源表示，仅用16比特即可保留256×256图像的可识别结构，实现从条件重建到无条件生成的平滑过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式图像压缩在低码率下能产生视觉上合理的重建，但当码率趋近于零时的行为尚不明确。实验发现代表性编解码器在低于正常工作码率时会发生"语义崩溃"，不是平滑地丢失源细节，而是产生畸形或不可识别的内容，因此需要分析其成因并提出新方法。

Method: 作者分析出两个因素：一是码率降低时重建损失与语义目标在梯度和视觉结果上冲突加剧；二是像素空间及以重建为导向的 VAE 扩散模型在语义保持上效率下降。据此提出 RAE-CoD，一种构建在表示自编码器（RAE）空间中的压缩导向扩散（CoD）方法，实现压缩表示与源表示的直接对齐。采用五个视觉基础模型（VFM）和双盲视觉-语言模型协议进行评估。

Result: 在 MSCOCO-30K 上，RAE-CoD 在 0.001–0.008 bpp 范围内表现突出：相对 VFM 特征 MSE 与 Fréchet Distance 比值较最佳竞争者分别至少降低 25.7% 和 69.1%。重建的语义可识别性和质量几乎保持不变，源一致性平滑下降，用向无条件生成的平滑过渡取代了突兀的语义崩溃。

Conclusion: RAE-CoD 在极低码率下有效缓解了语义崩溃问题，仅需16比特即可保留可识别、结构自然的图像内容，实现了从条件重建向无条件生成的优雅过渡，为极低码率生成式压缩提供了新思路。

Abstract: Generative image compression produces visually plausible reconstructions at low bitrates, yet their behavior as the rate approaches zero remains largely unexplored. When pushed below normal operating rates, representative codecs undergo semantic collapse: rather than gracefully losing source-specific detail, they produce malformed or unrecognizable content. Our analysis identifies two factors. As the bitrate decreases, reconstruction losses increasingly conflict with semantic objectives on gradients and visual results, while pixel-space and reconstruction-oriented VAE diffusion models become less efficient on semantic preservation. Guided by these findings, we introduce RAE-CoD, a compression-oriented diffusion (CoD) built in a representation autoencoder (RAE) space with direct alignment between compressed and source representations, preserving recognizable, naturally structured content for a $256\times256$ image with as few as 16 bits. We evaluate this framework using five vision foundation models (VFM) and a blinded vision-language model protocol. On MSCOCO-30K, RAE-CoD stands out from all evaluation. At 0.001-0.008 bpp, it reduces relative VFM feature MSE and Fréchet Distance ratio by at least 25.7% and 69.1% over the best competitors. Meanwhile, semantic recognizability and quality of the reconstructions remain nearly constant while source consistency falls smoothly, replacing abrupt semantic collapse with a graceful transition toward unconditional generation. Code will be released at https://github.com/LuizScarlet/RAE-CoD.

</details>


### [102] [TexTailor: Texture-Preserving Video Virtual Try-On via Adaptive Garment Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39335)
*Zijing Qin,Jun Zhou,Ruicheng Zhang,Jiaqi Hou,Zunnan Xu,Ronghui Li,Zhenyu Xie,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TexTailor：基于预训练视频扩散Transformer的高保真视频虚拟试穿框架，通过时序自适应调制、帧对齐位置编码与多源注入，提升高分辨率下的细节保持与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频虚拟试穿方法多聚焦低分辨率场景，扩展到高分辨率时面临两大挑战：一是对丰富服装参考信息利用不足；二是跨模态交互中缺乏服装与视频表征间的显式位置建模，削弱了细粒度局部对应关系。

Method: 提出TexTailor框架，构建于预训练视频扩散Transformer之上。核心设计包括：时序自适应调制机制（在去噪过程中动态调整服装视觉表征）、帧对齐位置编码策略（增强服装与视频的对应关系）、多源注入设计（减少异构条件间的干扰）。

Result: 在多个视频虚拟试穿基准（含高分辨率Eevee数据集）上进行大量实验，结果显示该方法在服装细节保持、时序一致性和整体视频质量方面均取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: TexTailor通过强化服装信息利用与显式位置建模，有效缓解高分辨率视频虚拟试穿的瓶颈，具备较好的高保真与一致性表现。

Abstract: Video virtual try-on has attracted increasing attention due to its broad potential in digital fashion and intelligent e-commerce. However, existing methods primarily focus on low-resolution settings and still face substantial challenges when extended to high-resolution scenarios. These limitations can be attributed to two main factors: (1) the insufficient utilization of rich garment reference information, and (2) the lack of explicit positional modeling between garment and video representations during cross-modal interaction, which weakens fine-grained local correspondence. To address these issues, we propose TexTailor, a high-fidelity video virtual try-on framework built upon a pretrained video Diffusion Transformer. Specifically, we introduce a timestep-adaptive modulation mechanism to dynamically adjust garment visual representations throughout denoising. We further develop a frame-aligned positional encoding strategy to strengthen garment-to-video correspondence, together with a multi-source injection design that reduces interference among heterogeneous conditions. Extensive experiments on multiple video virtual try-on benchmarks, including the high-resolution Eevee dataset, demonstrate that TexTailor achieves competitive performance in garment detail preservation, temporal consistency, and overall video quality.

</details>


### [103] [Rethinking Multi-Image Re-Representation in Multi-Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39363)
*Gengyuan Zhang,Xiao Han,Xinyu Xie,Tong Liu,Volker Tresp*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究多图像理解中"多图像重表征"问题，提出Mosaic通用视觉工具框架，包含十种可组合图像操作，并推出MosaicBench基准；实验表明文本与视觉重表征的收益高度依赖任务；进一步用强化学习训练出MosaicAgent-8B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多图像理解要求多模态大模型不仅识别单张图像内容，还需组织跨图像的视觉证据；现有研究对提示式思维链推理与智能体式视觉工具使用这两种"重表征"方式的优劣缺乏系统比较。

Method: 将提示式Chain-of-Thought推理与智能体视觉工具使用统一视为推理过程中视觉证据的重组织方式；提出Mosaic通用多图像视觉框架，让MLLM通过十种可组合图像操作主动构建视觉中间产物；在现有基准与新建的MosaicBench上比较五种重表征设置；并用强化学习（仅使用准确率与格式奖励）训练MosaicAgent-8B。

Result: 文本与视觉重表征的相对收益强烈依赖任务类型：视觉重表征在需要精确视觉证据的任务（假设检验、精度比较、方向敏感推理）中尤为有效，而在高层语义主导的任务上收益较小或不稳定。MosaicAgent-8B无需演示轨迹或专门工具使用奖励，即可在多步中组合视觉操作，并自发涌现多样的解题模式。

Conclusion: 多图像理解中不存在普适的重表征方式，应根据任务特性选择文本或视觉重表征；Mosaic框架与强化学习训练的智能体展示了主动构建视觉中间产物的可行性与优势，代码与数据将开源。

Abstract: Multi-image understanding requires MLLMs not only to recognise the content of individual images, but also to organise visual evidence distributed across them. We study this problem through multi-image re-representation, viewing prompted Chain-of-Thought reasoning and agentic visual tool use as different ways of re-organising visual evidence during reasoning. We introduce Mosaic, a general-purpose multi-image visual harness that enables an MLLM to actively construct visual intermediates with ten composable image operations. We compare five re-representation settings on existing multi-image benchmarks and on MosaicBench, a new grounding-focused benchmark for fine-grained multi-image understanding. Our experiments show that the relative benefits of textual and visual re-representation are strongly task-dependent. Visual re-representation is particularly effective for tasks requiring precise visual evidence, including hypothesis testing, precision comparison, and orientation-sensitive reasoning, while tasks dominated by higher-level semantic content show smaller or less consistent gains. Building on this finding, we train MosaicAgent-8B to use Mosaic with reinforcement learning using only accuracy and format rewards. Without demonstration trajectories or rewards for specific tool-use, the agent learns to compose visual operations over multiple steps and exhibits diverse problem-solving patterns unpromptedly. Code and data will be released at https://github.com/gengyuanmax/Mosaic.

</details>


### [104] [COBICount: Separating Object and Background Responses for Remote Sensing Object Counting Without Training on Target Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39366)
*Junjing Zheng,Zhiyi Zhou,Ningrui Yang,Hongying Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出COBICount，用于遥感目标计数的源域专属（source-only）迁移场景，通过分离响应生成、接受与背景抑制来缓解候选来源歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数有监督遥感计数方法依赖密度图预测，并假设目标类别、尺寸和背景相似；跨区域、跨传感器或跨类别应用时需目标数据或额外训练，成本高甚至不可得。源域专属计数降低了数据准备需求，但迁移更难，模型易在真实目标与重复背景结构（如道路边缘、停车网格、屋顶边界、水边界）上产生高密度响应，导致候选来源歧义。

Method: COBICount将过程分为三部分：Candidate Evidence（CE）生成可能的响应；Candidate Acceptance（CA）保留以目标为中心的紧凑响应；Bias Isolation（BI）减少与重复背景结构相关的响应。三者输出组合为最终密度图。

Result: 在RSOC Building上训练，并直接在DOTA Large Vehicle、Small Vehicle和Ship上评估，COBICount在目标域平均MAE上达到最低，为174.132。模型参数量5.07M，512x512输入下为17.41 GFLOPs。

Conclusion: COBICount无需目标数据或针对每个目标域训练即可提升迁移性能，代码将公开于https://github.com/yixuxi22/COBICount。

Abstract: Remote sensing object counting estimates how many buildings, vehicles, or ships appear in overhead images. Most supervised counters predict a density map, whose sum gives the object count, and assume similar categories, sizes, and backgrounds. Applying them across regions, sensors, or categories often requires target data or further training, which may be costly or unavailable. We study source-only counting. Training for the counting task and model selection use one group of images that shares an object category and similar imaging conditions, with one point marking each object. Target images and information remain unavailable until the model is fixed. This reduces data preparation but makes transfer harder. A model trained on one source may place high density values, called responses, on real objects and repeated background structures. Road edges, parking grids, roof boundaries, and water boundaries may then be counted as objects, creating candidate origin ambiguity. COBICount separates response generation, acceptance, and background suppression. Candidate Evidence (CE) generates possible responses. Candidate Acceptance (CA) keeps compact responses centered on objects. Bias Isolation (BI) reduces responses associated with repeated background structures. Their outputs form the final density map. Trained on RSOC Building and evaluated directly on DOTA Large Vehicle, Small Vehicle, and Ship, COBICount achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE) averaged over the target domains among the compared methods, 174.132. It uses 5.07 million parameters and 17.41 billion floating point operations for a 512x512 input. COBICount improves transfer without target data or training for each target. The code will be available at: https://github.com/yixuxi22/COBICount.

</details>


### [105] [EgoTools: Towards Tool-Centric Reasoning in Real-World Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39378)
*Shulin Tian,Junsu Kim,Shuai Liu,Hao Li,Yujiao Shen,Sihan Li,Zhe Yang,Yeongon Kim,Feiyu Li,Jialin Wu,Yichi Zhang,Wenhui Wang,Runmao Yao,Yuhao Dong,Zhaoxi Chen,Fangzhou Hong,Antonino Furnari,Jingkang Yang,Hongyuan Zhu,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoTools，首个面向第一人称工具使用理解的综合套件，包含100小时工具中心的自中心录制数据（EgoTools-Data）和1000题诊断基准（EgoTools-Bench），覆盖感知、几何、流程与因果推理四个方向，并验证其作为训练资源的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的具身任务（从日常活动到专业流程）要求智能体在物理约束下行动并跟踪对象与任务状态，其中工具使用是核心。这类任务需要推理可供性、手-工具-物几何关系、流程进展及对目标对象的因果效应。但现有多模态视频模型虽在字幕和通用视频问答等感知类任务上表现优异，在工具中心的具身推理上仍受限，主要原因在于缺乏真实第一人称数据和诊断性基准。

Method: 作者构建EgoTools综合套件，由两部分组成：（1）EgoTools-Data，一个大规模语料库，包含100小时工具中心的第一人称录制，配有同步音频、密集字幕、推理密集型叙述及补充3D信息；（2）EgoTools-Bench，一个诊断基准，含1000个问答对，分四个赛道，覆盖从感知与几何到流程与因果推理的工具使用理解。此外，作者还通过严格源视频分离下的全监督微调，验证该数据作为训练资源的价值。

Result: 实验显示当前模型难以将工具使用落地到视觉证据：Gemini-3.1-Pro整体准确率为66.9%，但在感知与定位赛道仅51.7%。在全部1000题基准上，全监督微调使Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct从50.0%提升至60.9%（严格源视频分离条件下）。

Conclusion: EgoTools为真实世界第一人称工具使用理解的训练与诊断评估建立了统一资源，实验结果既揭示了现有模型在视觉证据落地上仍存在明显短板，也证明了所构建数据在提升模型能力方面的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world embodied tasks, from everyday activities to professional procedures, require agents to act under physical constraints while tracking evolving object and task states. Tool use sits at the heart of such tasks, as many everyday and professional activities are tool-mediated. Understanding them requires reasoning about affordances, hand-tool-object geometry, procedural progress, and causal effects on target objects. Yet despite strong performance on perception-oriented video tasks such as captioning and general video QA, current multimodal video models remain limited in this form of tool-centric embodied reasoning. Progress in this direction has been limited by the lack of real-world egocentric data and diagnostic benchmarks. To address this gap, we introduce EgoTools, the first comprehensive suite for egocentric tool-use understanding.
