It consists of two complementary components: EgoTools-Data, a large-scale corpus of 100 hours of tool-centric egocentric recordings with synchronized audio, dense captions, reasoning-heavy narrations, and supplementary 3D information; and EgoTools-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark of 1,000 QA pairs across four tracks that cover tool-use understanding from perception and geometry to procedure and causal reasoning. Experimental results show that current models still struggle to ground tool use in visual evidence: Gemini-3.1-Pro achieves 66.9% overall accuracy but only 51.7% on Perception & Grounding. Beyond evaluation, we validate EgoTools-Data as a training resource. On the full 1,000-question benchmark, full supervised fine-tuning improves Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct from 50.0% to 60.9%, under strict source-video separation. Together, these results establish EgoTools as a unified resource for both training and diagnostic evaluation of real-world egocentric tool-use understanding.

</details>


### [106] [InfoAgent: Traceable Generation and Repair of Evidence-Grounded Infographics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39380)
*Yifan Li,Tong Li,Qi Zeng,Lishuai Gao,Ruwei Pan,Cong Wei,Shaohua Kevin Zhou,Zhuoliang Kang,Xiaoming Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架InfoAgent，以证据绑定型视觉-符号程序合成实现可靠信息图生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的信息图生成需要事实、符号与视觉关系在渲染与修订过程中保持一致，而修正单一元素还需追踪其支撑证据及受影响依赖，现有方法难以满足。

Method: InfoAgent以InfoAgent Visual Description(IVD)类型化依赖图记录事实负载、证据来源、执行路径与验证义务；检索设计先验引导编译；分层执行结合栅格合成与可编辑符号/绑定对象并保留轨迹；依赖感知修复定位修改、复查受影响依赖。

Result: 在IGenBench上达93.0 Q-ACC与59.0 I-ACC；新基准InfoGraphicBench-Evidence上200个测试请求完整清单通过率从21.5%(Same-IVD Prompt)提升至23.5%(初始分层输出)、修复后28.5%；120个审核修复案例中局部修复平均编辑12.4%画布，全局重生成达67.3%。

Conclusion: 证据绑定与依赖感知修复机制可显著提升信息图生成的可靠性与修订效率，且未解决义务保持显式可追踪。

Abstract: Reliable infographic generation requires facts, symbols, and visual relations to remain consistent through rendering and revision. Correcting one element also requires tracking its supporting evidence and the dependencies affected by the change. We present \textbf{InfoAgent}, a training-free framework for \emph{evidence-bound visual-symbolic program synthesis}. Its Infographic Visual Description (IVD) records factual payloads, evidence provenance, execution routes, and verification obligations in a typed dependency graph. Retrieved design priors guide compilation, and layered execution combines raster synthesis with editable symbolic and binding objects while retaining their traces. Dependency-aware repair localizes corrections, rechecks affected dependencies, and requires protected obligations to remain satisfied under the declared checkers. Unresolved obligations remain explicit. On IGenBench, InfoAgent achieves 93.0 Q-ACC and 59.0 I-ACC. We also introduce InfoGraphicBench-Evidence, where complete-checklist pass rates on 200 test requests increase from 21.5\% for Same-IVD Prompt to 23.5\% for the initial layered output and 28.5\% after repair, using the same evidence and initial IVD. On 120 audited repair cases, localized repair edits 12.4\% of the canvas on average, compared with 67.3\% for global regeneration.

</details>


### [107] [PCB-MC: Missing Component Analysis in Printed Circuit Boards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39427)
*Betsy Villa Brochero,Ian Gibson,Estefania Talavera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PCB-MC 数据集，用于 PCB 缺失元件检测，并提供基准评估与防泄漏的板型感知交叉验证协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCB 缺失元件检测与常规目标检测本质不同，模型需要定位"不存在"的元件，这是一个尚未被充分研究的开放问题。现有数据集和评估协议不足以支持该任务的可复现研究。

Method: 在 RF100 数据集基础上构建 PCB-MC，包含 197 种不同板型，每种板型对应唯一 PCB 设计，并配有多份增强样本；采用足迹级（footprint level）标注。同时提出板型感知交叉验证划分，防止训练集与测试集之间的布局泄漏。评估了多种监督与无监督方法。

Result: 监督模型在未见过的板型设计上表现出很高的假阴性率；无监督异常检测方法因缺乏与板型特定参考的空间对齐而完全失效。

Conclusion: 在多样化 PCB 布局上的缺失元件检测仍是开放挑战。作者公开 PCB-MC 及全部训练协议，以支持工业检测中结构性缺失检测的可复现研究。

Abstract: Detecting missing components on printed circuit boards (PCBs) differs fundamentally from conventional object detection, as the model must localize components that are not present. We introduce PCB-MC, a curated dataset for missing component detection with footprint level annotations built on top of the RF100 dataset. The dataset contains 197 distinct board types, each corresponding to a unique PCB design, with multiple augmented samples per type. We also provide benchmark results on PCB-MC by evaluating a diverse set of supervised and unsupervised methods. To ensure fair evaluation, we propose board type aware cross validation splits that prevent layout leakage between training and test sets. Supervised models showcase high false negative rates on unseen board designs, and unsupervised anomaly detection methods fail entirely due to the lack of spatial alignment with a board specific reference. These results confirm that missing component detection on diverse PCB layouts remains an open challenge. We release PCB-MC and all training protocols to support reproducible research on structural absence detection in industrial inspection.

</details>


### [108] [Towards Trustworthy AI for Glioma Diagnosis: A Task-Aware Evaluation of Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39429)
*Gonzalo Esteban Mosquera Rojas,Sebastian R. van der Voort,Carolin M. Pirkl,Sandeep Kaushik,Marion Smits,Stefan Klein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多任务深度学习框架（MRI胶质瘤分割与分级/分子分型）中的不确定性量化，采用MCD并与DE、MCDE对比，给出任务感知的评估策略与实用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险医学影像分析中，可信AI需具备不确定性量化能力；但现有多任务框架下UQ的任务依赖性、聚合方式及方法选择缺乏系统评估。

Method: 在MRI胶质瘤多任务框架（肿瘤分割 + IDH突变、1p/19q共缺失、肿瘤分级预测）中使用Monte Carlo Dropout，开展任务感知的预测、偶然与认知不确定性分解分析；评估MC样本收敛性、校准、错误检测、选择性预测、与分割性能的关联及体素级不确定性聚合对病例级可靠性的影响；并与Deep Ensembles、Monte Carlo Deep Ensembles对比，考察分割质量与分类的交互，以及融合分割与分类不确定性的复合信任分数。

Result: 各任务中不确定性估计均支持有效的错误检测；校准依赖dropout率，中等dropout率给出最可靠概率；不确定性分解具有任务相关的可解释性，但未稳定优于单一预测不确定性；DE与MCDE操作效用相当，无方法在各任务与指标上持续占优；复合信任分数在选择性预测中未稳定超过分类不确定性。

Conclusion: 研究提供了任务感知的UQ评估策略，并为可信胶质瘤诊断AI的开发提供实践指导，强调应根据具体任务与指标选择不确定性方法与聚合方式。

Abstract: Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) is a key requirement for trustworthy AI in high-stakes medical image analysis. In this work, we evaluate UQ in a multi-task Deep Learning framework for MRI-based glioma diagnosis that performs tumor segmentation and predicts IDH mutation status, 1p/19q co-deletion status, and tumor grade. Monte Carlo Dropout (MCD) is used for a detailed task-aware analysis of predictive, aleatoric, and epistemic uncertainty. We assess MC sample convergence, calibration, error detection, selective prediction, associations with segmentation performance, and the effect of voxel-wise uncertainty aggregation on case-level reliability. We also compare MCD with Deep Ensembles (DE) and Monte Carlo Deep Ensembles (MCDE), examine interactions between segmentation quality and classification, and evaluate a composite trust score integrating segmentation and classification uncertainty. Across tasks, uncertainty estimates supported meaningful error detection, while calibration depended on the dropout rate, with moderate rates yielding the most reliable probabilities. Uncertainty decomposition provided task-dependent interpretability but did not consistently improve error detection over predictive uncertainty alone. DE and MCDE showed comparable operational utility, with no method consistently dominating across tasks and metrics. The composite trust score did not consistently outperform classification uncertainty for selective prediction. Overall, our results provide a task-aware evaluation strategy and practical guidance for the development of trustworthy AI for glioma diagnosis.

</details>


### [109] [CAST: Causal Advantage-Structured Training with Spatially Grounded Compositional Rewards for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39441)
*Shu Yu,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CAST，一种针对预训练扩散模型的强化学习微调方法，通过自动确定SDE采样窗口、基于因果场景图的原子级奖励分解及像素空间优势投影，解决窗口选择、奖励饱和与样本低效三大问题，在GenEval 2等基准上显著优于Flow-GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将在线强化学习扩展到流匹配的扩散模型图像生成时面临三个限制：(1) 窗口选择依赖人工设定SDE采样窗口（注入探索噪声的去噪步）；(2) 奖励饱和，基于人工标注训练的评分模型对最新SOTA开源扩散模型给出的分数极高且几乎无法区分，导致优势估计基本失效；(3) 样本低效，单一标量奖励将不同失败模式压缩为几乎相同的分数，对针对性改进提供的梯度指导极少。

Method: CAST（Causal Advantage-Structured Training）包含三点：(1) 识别每个模型在去噪轨迹中确定物体及其空间布局的步骤，据此设定SDE窗口；(2) 通过因果场景图（CSG）将每个提示分解为可验证原子（物体、数量、属性、空间关系等可独立检验的最小语义单元），并对每个原子分别奖励；(3) 通过教师强制注意力将带符号的原子级优势投影到像素空间，用于对SDE策略目标进行空间加权。

Result: 在FLUX.2-dev和Qwen-Image-2512两个最强开源扩散模型上用CAST微调，并在组合性基准GenEval 2和整体质量基准Qwen-Image-Bench上评估。在几乎相同的训练预算下，CAST在最难的GenEval 2提示上相对基座模型的提升最高达Flow-GRPO的3.07倍，同时整体生成质量也得到提升。

Conclusion: CAST通过自动窗口选择、原子级奖励分解和像素空间优势投影，有效克服了扩散模型强化学习微调中的窗口选择、奖励饱和与样本低效问题，在组合性生成上取得显著提升且不损失整体质量。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning has been extended to flow matching for diffusion model (DM) image generation. However, this paradigm faces three limitations: (1) Window selection. Existing methods manually set the stochastic differential equation (SDE) sampling window, i.e., the denoising steps where exploration noise is injected. We instead determine it from each model's denoising trajectory. (2) Reward saturation. Current methods rely on scoring models trained on human annotations; we find that such scores are extremely high and nearly indistinguishable on the latest SOTA open-source DMs, making advantage estimation largely ineffective. (3) Sample inefficiency. A single scalar reward collapses different failure modes into almost identical scores, leaving minimal gradient guidance for targeted improvement. To address these issues, we propose CAST (Causal Advantage-Structured Training), an RL fine-tuning method for pretrained DMs, which (1) identifies the denoising step at which each model fixes the objects and their spatial arrangement in the image and uses that timing to set the SDE window, (2) decomposes each prompt via Causal Scene Graphs (CSG) into verifiable-atoms, i.e., minimal semantic units such as an object, count, attribute, or spatial relation that can each be checked independently, and rewards each atom separately, and (3) projects the signed atom-level advantages into pixel space through teacher-forced attention and uses them to spatially weight the SDE policy objective. We fine-tune two of the strongest open-source DMs, FLUX.2-dev and Qwen-Image-2512, with CAST, and evaluate them on GenEval 2, a compositional benchmark, and on Qwen-Image-Bench for overall quality. Within almost the same training budget, CAST's improvement over the base model on the most challenging GenEval 2 prompts is up to 3.07x that of Flow-GRPO, while overall generation quality also improves.

</details>


### [110] [ResARC: Residual-Aware AutoRegressive Coding for Ultra-Low Bitrate Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39451)
*Qin Yan,Ruixiao Dong,Yutao Xie,Li Li,Ying Chen,Kai Li,Daowen Li,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ResARC提出一种残差感知的自回归图像编解码器，在解码端同时补偿量化残差与生成残差，在超低码率下显著提升分布保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐进式自回归图像编解码器通过将连续潜变量量化为离散token、传输由粗到细的前缀token并在解码端生成剩余后缀token，实现生成式压缩。但其重建质量受限于两个残差：离散化带来的量化残差，以及后缀token自回归生成不完美导致的生成残差。

Method: 提出ResARC，在解码端显式补偿两种残差。量化残差由以自回归解码上下文为条件的扩散Transformer生成，且无需额外边信息；生成残差在编码端计算，并通过学习到的生成残差编解码器（Generation Residual Codec）高效压缩传输以供解码端校正。恢复的残差与重建潜表示融合后经适配的VAE解码器解码。

Result: 大量实验表明，ResARC在超低码率区间实现了有竞争力的感知相似度，同时相比领先的生成式编解码器大幅提升了分布保真度。

Conclusion: ResARC通过残差感知的解码端补偿机制，有效缓解了量化残差与生成残差对重建质量的限制，为生成式图像压缩提供了更优方案，代码与模型即将发布。

Abstract: Progressive autoregressive image codecs provide an appealing paradigm for generative compression by quantizing continuous latents into discrete tokens, transmitting coarse-to-fine prefix tokens and generating the remaining suffix tokens at the decoder. However, their reconstruction quality is fundamentally limited by two residuals introduced along this pipeline: the quantization residual, arising from information loss during discrete tokenization, and the generation residual, resulting from imperfect autoregressive generation of the suffix tokens. To address these limitations, we introduce ResARC, a residual-aware autoregressive codec that explicitly compensates for both residuals at the decoder. Specifically, we generate the quantization residual with a diffusion transformer conditioned on the autoregressive decoding context, while requiring no additional side information. In parallel, we compute the generation residual at the encoder and employ a learned Generation Residual Codec to efficiently compress and transmit it for decoder-side correction. The recovered residuals are then integrated with the reconstructed latent representation and decoded through an adapted VAE decoder. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ResARC achieves competitive perceptual similarity while substantially improving distributional fidelity over leading generative codecs across the ultra-low bitrate regime. Code and models will be released soon.

</details>


### [111] [DensePed-Lite: Quality-Aware Adaptive Detection for Dense Pedestrians under Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39467)
*ZiAn Wang,MingZhe Liu,Chaoyi Guo,ChangChun Li,Fangming Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DensePed-Lite，一个基于“网络应根据观测质量自适应调整行为”这一统一原则的轻量行人检测框架，通过 UQE、MPSC、CTDM 三个协同机制应对遮挡、尺度变化与实时性挑战，在 CityPersons 和 CrowdHuman 上取得更优的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实密集场景下的行人检测面临严重遮挡、剧烈尺度变化和严格实时性要求。现有轻量检测器难以兼顾精度与效率，且常忽视质量感知的特征建模以及分类与定位之间的一致性，导致拥挤场景下性能不稳定。

Method: 提出统一框架 DensePed-Lite，核心原则为：在遮挡条件下网络应依据所观测信息的质量自适应调整行为，而非假设信息完整。该原则具体落实在遮挡危害最大的三个环节：针对不可靠置信度评分的 UQE、针对空间覆盖碎片化的 MPSC、针对多尺度融合不一致的 CTDM。三个机制相互增强而非孤立作用，且未显著增加模型复杂度。

Result: 在 CityPersons 和 CrowdHuman 数据集上的实验表明，DensePed-Lite 相比近期最先进的轻量方法实现了更优的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: DensePed-Lite 适合在密集行人场景中实时部署，为轻量行人检测提供了一种质量自适应的统一解决思路。

Abstract: Pedestrian detection plays a crucial role in computer vision with applications in autonomous driving, surveillance, and public safety. However, real-world dense scenes bring severe challenges, including heavy occlusion, drastic scale variations, and strict real-time requirements. Existing lightweight detectors struggle to balance accuracy and efficiency while often neglecting quality-aware feature modeling and consistency between classification and localization, leading to unstable performance under crowded conditions. To address these issues, we propose DensePed-Lite, a unified framework built on a single principle: under occlusion the network should adapt its behavior to the quality of what it observes rather than assume complete information. This principle is realized at three points where occlusion does the most damage: unreliable confidence scoring (UQE), fragmented spatial coverage (MPSC), and incoherent multi-scale fusion (CTDM). The three mechanisms reinforce one another instead of acting in isolation, all without significantly increasing complexity. Experiments on CityPersons and CrowdHuman validate that DensePed-Lite achieves a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off compared with recent state-of-the-art lightweight methods, making it suitable for real-time deployment in dense pedestrian scenarios.

</details>


### [112] [From Wrecks to Wisdom: Recovering Crash Mechanics from Real-World Multi-View Photos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39486)
*Ondřej Valach,Václav Diviš,Ivan Gruber*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究在缺乏结构化事故元数据时，能否仅从事故后车辆照片中恢复碰撞力学信息，并提出基于多视角照片的多任务预测框架与评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实事故记录中碰撞构型、主受力方向、速度变化ΔV等关键元数据常缺失或损坏，而事故后照片广泛可得且包含丰富的变形证据，因此有必要研究从照片直接恢复碰撞力学信息的可行性。

Method: 将碰撞理解建模为基于单案例多视角照片集的有监督预测，目标包括六项碰撞变形分类（CDC）描述符及重构ΔV的纵向与横向分量。每张照片由共享视觉骨干编码，视图级特征融合为案例级表示，再由目标专用预测头输出各描述符。使用事故调查抽样系统（CISS）约1.5万训练案例（源自约150万张照片）、1.15k验证与1.15k测试案例，建立评测协议。

Result: 仅凭事故后影像即可为若干非平凡碰撞力学描述符提供可用信号，但弱可观测与长尾目标仍具挑战。在比较的训练方案中，所选联合训练配方将主受力方向的平均绝对角度误差从20.1度降至14.05度，纵向ΔV的MAE从8.04 km/h降至7.45 km/h。

Conclusion: 研究表明仅用事故后照片可部分恢复碰撞力学信息，为未来与结构化事故元数据的多模态融合提供了参考基准，同时提示弱可观测与长尾目标仍是难点。

Abstract: Estimating accident mechanics from real-world crashes is important for vehicle-safety analysis, injury modeling, crash-severity prediction, and operational workflows such as insurance claim triage. In standard crash records, key metadata such as impact configuration, principal direction of force, and change in velocity ($ΔV$) may be missing, delayed, or corrupted, while post-crash photographs are widely available and contain rich visual evidence of deformation. We study how much crash-mechanics information can be recovered directly from vehicle photos when structured signals are absent. We formulate crash understanding as supervised prediction from per-case multi-view photo sets. Targets include six Collision Deformation Classification (CDC) descriptors and the longitudinal and lateral components of reconstructed $ΔV$. Each photo is encoded by a shared visual backbone, and the resulting view-level features are fused into a case-level representation from which target-specific heads predict crash descriptors. Using 15.2k training cases from the Crash Investigation Sampling System, drawn from about 1.5M photos before filtering, together with 1.15k validation and 1.15k test cases, we define an evaluation protocol for vision-based crash descriptor estimation from incomplete multi-view evidence. Post-crash imagery alone provides usable signal for several non-trivial crash-mechanics descriptors, while weakly observable and long-tailed targets remain challenging. Within the compared training regimes, the selected joint-training recipe reduces mean absolute angular error for principal direction of force from 20.1 to 14.05 degrees and longitudinal $ΔV$ MAE from 8.04 to 7.45 km/h. Our work provides a reference point for future multimodal fusion with structured crash metadata.

</details>


### [113] [OmniReasoning: Pushing the Limits of Audio-Visual Joint Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39490)
*Junming Lin,Yuxuan Wang,Zhenxin Lei,Yuxin Liu,Ruixun Liu,Yinsong Yan,Ling Wang,Minghao Han,Yunfei Chu,Shun Lei,Xueyao Zhang,Qize Yang,Jin Xu,Yiwu Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对全模态模型忽视音视频联合推理的问题，提出了基准OmniReasoningBench、数据引擎OmniQA及学习方法MFSD，并训练出OmniReasoning-30B-A3B，在多个基准上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态模型虽能统一处理音频、视觉与语言，但其基准、训练数据和学习方法多将各模态独立对待，导致音视频联合推理能力缺乏评估且未被充分激发，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 提出三项工作：1）OmniReasoningBench基准，包含1,150道需音视频双重证据的选择题与开放题，涵盖视频内推理和视频外推理；2）数据引擎OmniQA，自动构造以证据为支撑、明确需要音视频联合推理的问答对及时间戳线索链以指导思维过程标注，并生成OmniReasoning-SFT-112K和OmniReasoning-RL-19K训练数据；3）在线策略自蒸馏方法MFSD，在模态特定线索上下文下评估每个采样响应，解耦单线索贡献与跨模态交互，实现token级信用分配。

Result: OmniReasoning-30B-A3B在OmniVideoBench上达到50.0%，在OmniReasoningBench上达到42.5%，相比基座模型Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Thinking分别提升12.8和9.3个百分点；在通用及长视频基准（如Video-MME-v2）上也取得显著增益。

Conclusion: 该工作通过基准、数据引擎与学习方法为全模态联合推理提供了坚实基础，有助于推动未来相关研究。

Abstract: Recent advances have enabled unified omni-modal models in understanding audio, vision, and language. However, existing benchmarks, training data, and learning methods largely treat the modalities independently, leaving the capability of audio-visual joint reasoning poorly evaluated and insufficiently elicited. We address this gap with a benchmark, data engine, and learning method. First, we introduce OmniReasoningBench, a benchmark where both audio and visual evidence are indispensable. It comprises 1,150 multiple-choice and open-ended questions across two tasks, reasoning over video and reasoning beyond video. Second, we develop a data engine OmniQA. It automatically constructs evidence-grounded QA pairs that explicitly necessitate audio-visual joint reasoning, together with time-stamped clue chains that guide the annotation of thinking process. Besides our benchmark, this engine produces training data OmniReasoning-SFT-112K and OmniReasoning-RL-19K. Finally, we propose an on-policy self-distillation method Modality-Factored Self-Distillation (MFSD). It evaluates each sampled response under modality-specific clue contexts, disentangling the contributions of individual clues and their cross-modal interactions for token-level credit assignment. With our training data and learning method, our model OmniReasoning-30B-A3B achieves 50.0% on OmniVideoBench and 42.5% on OmniReasoningBench, improving the base model Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Thinking by 12.8 and 9.3 percentage points, respectively. Moreover, it delivers substantial gains on general and long-video benchmarks, including Video-MME-v2. We hope our work offers a solid step for facilitating future research in omni-modal joint reasoning.

</details>


### [114] [Front-to-Back: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Asymmetric Cross-View Vehicle Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39492)
*Moseli Mots'oehli,Thulani Babeli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Front2Back-ReID 基准，评测零样本视觉语言模型与检索基线在前摄到后摄车辆再识别任务上的表现，模型仍逊于人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一车辆在前摄与后摄中外观变化大且无共享视角，跨摄像头车辆重识别很难，需要新的评测基准来检验现有模型的真实能力。

Method: 构建 Front2Back-ReID 基准，含 20 段南非录制序列中 500 个经人工核验的车辆换场样本。每个样本要求在前摄图中标注车辆后，从后摄图至少三个候选中匹配同一车辆。评测 7 个零样本视觉语言模型、4 个图像检索基线以及 25 名人类被试，输入形式包括完整前摄 RGB 图像、裁剪目标车辆和二元轮廓。

Result: 最强 VLM 在目标裁剪上 Rank-1 为 76.6%，冻结 SigLIP2 基线为 74.0%，差异不具统计显著性。人类完整图像达 94.0%，目标裁剪达 92.2%。启用推理在两种模式下均提升所有模型的三种输入条件准确率，且 VLM 在完整场景上的表现普遍差于目标裁剪。

Conclusion: 通用 VLM 在前摄到后摄车辆匹配上尚未稳定超过强视觉检索方法，人类仍显著更可靠。

Abstract: Matching the same vehicle across front and rear cameras is difficult because the cameras do not share a view and the vehicle's appearance changes substantially. We introduce Front2Back-ReID, a benchmark of 500 manually verified vehicle handovers from 20 recording sequences in South Africa. Each example asks a model to match a vehicle highlighted in a front-camera image to the same vehicle among at least three candidates in a later rear-camera image. We evaluate seven zero-shot vision-language models, four image-retrieval baselines, and 25 human participants. Models are tested using full front RGB images, cropped target vehicles, and binary silhouettes. The strongest VLM achieved 76.6 percent Rank-1 accuracy on target crops, compared with 74.0 percent for the frozen SigLIP2 baseline; this difference was not statistically clear. Human participants achieved 94.0 percent accuracy with full images and 92.2 percent with target crops. Under our evaluation setup, enabling reasoning improved accuracy across all three input conditions for every model evaluated in both modes. We also found that VLMs generally performed worse on full scenes than on target crops. These results show that general-purpose VLMs do not yet consistently outperform strong visual retrieval for front-to-rear vehicle matching, while humans remain substantially more reliable.

</details>


### [115] [PartiCam: Camera Controlled Video Generation with Reward Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39504)
*Amine Ouasfi,Runjia Li,Junlin Han,Eric Marchand,Philip H. S. Torr,Adnane Boukhayma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PartiCam 是一种免训练的粒子滤波方法，用于改进相机控制的视频生成，通过全局-局部细化框架提升相机轨迹遵循度与视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视频扩散模型难以生成严格遵循指定相机轨迹的视频；免训练方法无需构建大规模相机标注数据集，可引导预训练模型在测试时实现目标相机运动。但现有基于采样的引导方法轨迹不稳定：要么探索过广而无法遵循目标相机运动，要么过早坍缩而随时间的视觉多样性下降。

Method: 提出面向扩散奖励引导的全局-局部细化框架：基于序列蒙特卡洛（SMC）引导，并引入基于粒子滤波重采样的局部细化阶段。

Result: 实验显示相机轨迹遵循度大幅提升、漂移减少、视觉质量更好，且无需重新训练模型。生成的相机控制视频数据还可用于训练相机条件视频扩散模型。

Conclusion: PartiCam 通过粒子滤波的局部细化，在免训练条件下实现了准确且一致的相机控制视频生成，兼顾轨迹遵循与视觉多样性。

Abstract: We present PartiCam, a training-free Particle filtering rooted method for improved Camera controlled video generation. Generating videos that follow a precisely specified camera trajectory remains challenging for large video diffusion models. Training-free approaches are backbone-agnostic and avoid the need to construct large camera-annotated datasets by steering pretrained models toward the desired camera motion at test time. This enables the generation of camera-controlled video data that can subsequently be used to train camera-conditioned video diffusion models. Existing sampling-based guidance approaches often suffer from unstable trajectories: they either explore too broadly and fail to respect the target camera motion or collapse early and lose visual diversity over time. We introduce a global-local refinement framework for diffusion reward guidance, enabling accurate and consistent camera control during video generation. Our method builds on Sequential Monte-Carlo (SMC) guidance, but introduces a local refinement stage based on particle filtered resampling. Experiments show large improvements in camera trajectory adherence, reduced drift, and better visual quality, without requiring model retraining.

</details>


### [116] [Lens Flare Removal and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39527)
*Tarun Yenamandra,Jonathon Luiten,Daniel Cremers,Nathan Matsuda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出大尺寸镜头光晕去除数据集与扩散模型，并引入基于主点对称性的光晕表示模型，与3DGS联合优化，实现光晕与场景的分解、编辑与跨视角迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镜头光晕源自相机成像系统而非真实场景，会严重损害3D重建等下游任务；现有方法仅能处理光源附近的小光晕，对充满整幅图像的大光晕无能为力。同时光晕也是常用的艺术效果，但多视角下光晕的一致表示与重建尚未被探索。

Method: 1) 构建结合真实数据与程序化生成管线的大光晕去除数据集，微调扩散模型进行去除；2) 提出利用光晕关于相机主点对称性的光晕表示模型；3) 提出联合优化该光晕模型与3DGS的流水线，结合去光晕模型将3D场景分解为光晕与场景；4) 重建的光晕为显式、可重渲染形式，支持编辑与迁移到新图像和新3D场景。

Result: 在已有基准与新建的大面积反射光晕基准上评估去除效果，直接量化光晕/场景分解质量，并证明该流水线对自动光源定位误差具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法统一了大规模光晕去除与光晕的多视角建模，实现了可分解、可编辑、可迁移的显式光晕表示，对下游3D重建任务与艺术创作均具价值。

Abstract: The presence of lens flares in images can significantly reduce the quality of downstream application results for tasks such as 3D scene reconstruction. This is because lens flares are a property of the camera imaging system, and not a part of the underlying scene being modeled. There are previous methods that tackle the removal of small flares focused around a light source. However, existing methods struggle with large flares, such as those that fill the entire image. In this work, we compile a novel dataset for large-flare removal, combining publicly available real-world data with a procedural generation pipeline. We fine-tune a diffusion-based model on our dataset to remove complex, large lens flares. On the other hand, lens flares remain effective artistic tools, widely used in the media. While there are ways to simulate 2D flares, representing and reconstructing lens flares consistently across multiple views has not yet been explored. To achieve this, we introduce a flare representation model that leverages the symmetry of lens flares about the camera's principal point. We propose a computational pipeline to jointly optimize this flare model and a Gaussian splatting model (3DGS). This enables the decomposition of a 3D scene into lens flares and the scene itself, using our flare-removal model. Because the reconstructed flare is explicit and re-renderable, it can be edited and transferred to novel images and new 3D scenes. We evaluate removal on an established benchmark and a new one for large reflective flares, quantify the flare/scene decomposition directly, and show that the pipeline is robust to errors in automatic light-source localization.

</details>


### [117] [Comparative study of adapting pre-trained models for driving behavior video captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39542)
*Sayak Mallick,Philipp Geiger,Augustin Kelava*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该报告在自动驾驶领域对比了多种微调与提示方法，通过在视频数据集上适配大语言模型，将驾驶情境的低维理解引入模型。在BDD-X数据集上，全量微调框架在部分自动评测指标上表现良好，甚至超越基线；同时尝试了LoRA和提示工程并讨论其局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已具备很强的多模态数据理解能力，作者希望借此在自动驾驶场景中，让模型获得对驾驶情境的低维理解，并系统比较不同微调与提示方法的效果。

Method: 以主测试模型SpaceTimeGPT为对象，在BDD-X（Berkeley DeepDrive eXplanation）视频数据集上进行适配实验，采用全量微调框架，并进一步在VideoLLaVA模型上尝试LoRA（低秩适配）和提示工程。

Result: 在BDD-X数据集上，全量微调框架在部分自动评测指标上表现良好，且在某些指标上超越了基线；LoRA与提示工程的实验则揭示了其局限性。

Conclusion: 全量微调在自动驾驶视频理解任务中具有一定优势，而LoRA和提示工程虽具潜力但存在局限，值得进一步探讨改进方向。

Abstract: This report examines and compares some of the many fine tuning and prompting methods existing, applying them within the domain of autonomous driving. The idea is to compare these methods by adapting a Large Language Model (LLM) on a video dataset. LLM's have become extremely good at achieving a good understanding of different forms of data and this study aims to induce a low dimensional understanding of driving situations into our primary test model SpaceTimeGPT. Experiments on BDD-X (Berkeley DeepDrive eXplanation) dataset demonstrate good performance of the full fine tuning framework on some automatic metrics, and in some metrics, it even surpasses the baseline. We also try Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) and prompt engineering on VideoLLaVA model and discuss its limitations.

</details>


### [118] [Learning Normal Diffusion Dynamics for Backdoor Defense in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39548)
*Junjian Li,Xiaolong Liu,Peng Sun,Liantao Wu,Linghan Chen,Yudong Gao,Honglong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NDDL框架，从"转移动力学"视角防御T2I扩散模型的后门攻击，仅用良性样本学习正常扩散轨迹演化规律，通过观测与预测转移的偏差检测后门并定位触发器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I扩散模型后门防御多依赖内部表示中的特定异常模式进行检测，随着攻击机制日益多样，这类方法泛化性受限。因此需要一种不依赖特定攻击模式、更具普适性的防御视角。

Method: 作者观察到良性扩散轨迹在交叉注意力、潜在空间与噪声空间中呈现结构化且依赖时间步的转移模式，而后门攻击会偏离该正常演化。基于此提出NDDL：仅使用良性样本，构建紧凑的多空间轨迹表示，训练时间步条件化的动力学模型预测扩散演化；推理阶段利用观测转移与预测转移之间的偏差量化动力学不一致性以检测后门；并通过低语义词汇替换实现无需先验知识的触发器定位。

Result: 在多种后门攻击上的大量实验表明，NDDL在检测效果与泛化能力上均表现有效。

Conclusion: 从转移动力学角度进行后门防御是可行且有效的，NDDL无需后门先验知识即可实现检测与触发器定位，对不同攻击机制具备良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks pose a serious threat to the secure deployment of text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models. Existing defenses typically detect backdoors from specific abnormal patterns in internal representations, which may limit their generalizability with the emergence of increasingly diverse attack mechanisms. In this paper, we study backdoor defense of T2I diffusion models from a transition-dynamics perspective. We observe that benign diffusion trajectories exhibit structured and timestep-dependent transition patterns from cross-attention, latent and noise spaces, whereas backdoor attacks tend to induce deviations from such normal evolution. Motivated by these observations, we propose Normal Diffusion Dynamics Learning (NDDL), a novel backdoor defense framework that learns the normal transition dynamics of diffusion trajectories utilizing only benign samples. NDDL constructs compact multi-space trajectory representations and trains a timestep-conditioned dynamics model to predict the diffusion evolution. In the inference phase, deviations between the observed and predicted transitions are exploited to quantify dynamics inconsistency for backdoor detection. NDDL further enables trigger localization without any prior knowledge of the embedded backdoor by performing substitution with low-semantic words. Extensive experiments for diverse backdoor attacks demonstrate the effectiveness and generalizability of our proposed NDDL.

</details>


### [119] [EffGS: Efficient and High-Fidelity Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39553)
*Changbai Li,Shuo Yang,Yichen Yang,Shuwei Shao,Huobin Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EffGS，一种面向3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的通用加速框架，兼顾训练与渲染效率，并在有界与大规模场景中保持甚至超越原始3DGS的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用加速方法在扩展到更复杂、大规模场景时会出现严重的渲染质量退化，因此需要一种更具通用性的加速框架，在提升效率的同时不牺牲重建质量。

Method: EffGS融合三项机制：1）频率感知引导，通过结合像素级重建误差与高斯差分掩膜的importance scoring机制，在训练中提供分阶段的空间引导；2）局部化密度控制，将稠密化与剪枝限制在采样视图中具有有效投影足迹的高斯上；3）自适应基元尺度调制，通过可学习的逐高斯尺度调制调整有效基元范围，同时保留Compact Box光栅化规则。

Result: 在有界与大规模场景数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在重建质量、训练时间和基元数量之间取得了良好的平衡。组件消融实验与相同基元预算下的对比进一步验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: EffGS是一种通用性更强的3DGS加速框架，可在有界与大规模场景中提升训练和渲染效率，同时保持与原始3DGS相当或更优的重建质量。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables real-time novel view synthesis, but existing general-purpose acceleration methods suffer severe rendering quality degradation when extended to more complex, large-scale scenes. To address this issue, we propose EffGS, a more general acceleration framework that improves training and rendering efficiency while maintaining reconstruction quality comparable to or better than vanilla 3DGS across bounded and large-scale scenes. EffGS combines frequency-aware guidance, localized density control, and adaptive primitive scale modulation. First, an importance scoring mechanism combines pixel-wise reconstruction errors with a difference-of-Gaussians mask scheduled over training to provide stage-dependent spatial guidance. Second, localized densification and pruning restricts density modifications to Gaussians with valid projected footprints in the sampled views. Third, learnable per-Gaussian scale modulation adjusts effective primitive extent during optimization while retaining the Compact Box rasterization rule. Extensive experiments on bounded and large-scale scene datasets demonstrate a favorable balance between reconstruction quality, training time, and primitive count. Component ablations and matched-primitive-budget comparisons further support the effectiveness of the framework.

</details>


### [120] [Life-Bench: A Benchmark and Knowledge Graph Framework for Multimodal Personalization Beyond Concept Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19001)
*Xia Hu,Honglei Zhuang,Brian Potetz,Alireza Fathi,Bo Hu,Babak Samari,Howard Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Life-Bench——一个完全合成、人工验证的多模态基准（超11,800问答对、10项任务），按证据范围分为概念识别、事件理解与聚合推理三类；并提出基于个人知识图谱的 LifeGraph 框架。系统评估显示准确率随证据范围扩大而急剧下降，聚合任务低于0.40，表明多模态个人历史的个性化推理仍是开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益承担个人助手角色，用户期望其能对多模态生活历史进行推理（识人、理解事件、聚合模式），但现有基准主要停留在概念级识别，缺乏覆盖更广证据范围的评测工具，因此需要构建新的基准与方法。

Method: 构建 Life-Bench：完全合成、经人工验证的多模态基准，包含10项任务、超11,800问答对，按所需证据范围组织为概念识别、事件理解、聚合推理三类；其以照片为中心的个人历史在嵌入统计上与真实用户账户分布对齐。同时提出 LifeGraph 个人知识图谱框架，提供结构化检索并按需访问源视觉证据；并以 Life-Bench 系统评测四种检索范式。

Result: 准确率随证据范围扩大而显著下降，聚合类任务准确率低于0.40；没有任何单一检索范式在所有类别中占优。LifeGraph 在事件与聚合任务上表现尤为有潜力，但所有方法在概念识别之外的表现仍较有限。

Conclusion: 多模态历史记录上的个性化推理仍是开放挑战，Life-Bench 可作为推动未来进展的测试平台。

Abstract: As large language models increasingly power personal assistants, users expect them to reason over multimodal life histories, from recognizing people to understanding events to aggregating patterns, yet existing benchmarks primarily target concept-level recognition. We introduce Life-Bench, a fully synthetic, human-verified multimodal benchmark of over 11,800 question-answer pairs across 10 tasks, organized by required evidence scope: concept identification, event understanding, and aggregated reasoning. The benchmark's photo-centric personal histories are distributionally aligned with real user accounts under embedding statistic. The interconnected structure of personal data invites graph-based solutions; we propose LifeGraph, a personal knowledge graph framework providing structured retrieval with on-demand access to source visual evidence, showing particular promise on event and aggregated tasks. Systematic evaluation of four retrieval paradigms on Life-Bench demonstrates that accuracy degrades sharply with evidence scope, falling below 0.40 on aggregated tasks, and that no single paradigm dominates across categories. Performance beyond concept recognition remains modest for all evaluated methods, establishing personalization over multimodal histories as an open challenge and Life-Bench as a testbed for future progress.

</details>


### [121] [RESUME: Recurrent State Updates from Motion and Residual Signals for Efficient Video Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39563)
*Can Zhang,Xiaotian Han,Junyuan Shang,Yuchen Ding,Zhenyu Zhang,Shuohuan Wang,Dianhai Yu,Ruirui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RESUME，一种有状态的编解码表示，将预测帧作为对携带参考状态的更新进行编码，在保持相同 token 预算下提升视频语言模型的时间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频语言模型独立编码采样 RGB 帧，长视频要么耗尽 token 预算，要么丢失采样帧间的变化；编解码感知前端虽读取运动矢量与残差，但部署时每个预测帧仍被单独 token 化，token 仅依赖当前原始特征而非携带的参考。

Method: RESUME：锚点 I 帧初始化紧凑隐状态，后续每个预测帧作为对该状态的更新被消费，共享读出模块从累积状态中输出与 VideoLM 兼容的 token，从而在表示层面保留编解码预测并以轨迹形式交给语言模型。

Result: 在十个基准上，增益集中于时间推理：TempCompass、TOMATO、MVBench 上分别比 LLaVA-Video-7B 提升 2.8、5.1、3.9 分，且优于编解码基线 CoPE-7B；在通用与长视频问答上保持竞争力。冻结过渡测试显示锚点依赖、顺序敏感性及超出训练视野的有效展开行为。

Conclusion: 将编解码预测保持在表示层并以状态轨迹形式提供给语言模型，可在相同 token 预算下显著增强时间推理，同时不牺牲通用与长视频问答性能。

Abstract: Existing video language models encode sampled RGB frames independently, so a long video must either exhaust the token budget or drop the changes between sampled frames. Codec-aware front-ends read the motion vectors and residuals that encoding produced, but in their deployed form each predictive frame is still tokenized on its own: the tokens are a function of the current primitives, not of a carried reference. We argue that a more natural function is of both---the current primitives and a carried reference. A clip and its time reversal share the same frames and differ only in the order of changes---an axis that symmetric pooling discards by construction, and that is non-empty in the frozen vision features VideoLMs use---and the codec recurrence already composes those changes in order against a reference state. We introduce RESUME, a stateful codec representation: an anchor I-frame initializes a compact latent state, each subsequent predictive frame is consumed as an update to that state, and a shared readout exposes VideoLM-compatible tokens from the accumulated state. Codec prediction is thereby kept at the representation level and handed to the language model as a trajectory, not as a set of independent token groups. At the same per-predictive-frame token budget as prior codec-aware methods, a predictive frame enters the language model as a readout of what the front-end already knows, not as an encoding of the current primitives alone. Across ten benchmarks, the gains concentrate on temporal reasoning: on all three temporal benchmarks RESUME improves over both the RGB-frame baseline LLaVA-Video-7B (by 2.8, 5.1, and 3.9 points on TempCompass, TOMATO, and MVBench) and the codec-based baseline CoPE-7B, while staying competitive on general and long-form QA. Frozen-transition tests further show anchor dependence, order sensitivity, and useful rollout behavior beyond the training horizon.

</details>


### [122] [From Given to Gathered Evidence: Agentic Learning for Longitudinal Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39566)
*Minye Shao,Chaohui Yu,Yixuan Wu,Fan Wang,Ling Shao,Yang Long*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CASE：面向“主动寻找证据”的临床智能体框架，含角色化智能体、工具调用框架与智能体后训练方法，并基于UK Biobank构建纵向多模态基准（50,401个问题）。8B模型在匹配条件下答案准确率相对GPT-5.4与Claude Opus 4.8提升超16%与10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统医学基准多评估对“已给定证据”的推理，而无法衡量智能体在临床记录与纵向影像中主动检索、选择与比较证据的能力。

Method: 构建CASE角色化临床智能体与工具调用harness；基于UK Biobank建立纵向多模态基准，问题关联患者级环境（临床上下文与基线与随访多序列MRI），智能体自主选择就诊、器官、模态、切片与专家工具；采用14,734条前沿模型交互轨迹做SFT，再进行智能体强化学习，结合特权在线自蒸馏与基于评分标准的LLM反馈。

Result: CASE超越问答模仿，形成可迁移的探究策略，增强基于证据的纵向推理；匹配评估下，基于Qwen3-VL-8B的智能体答案准确率相对GPT-5.4提升超16%、相对Claude Opus 4.8提升超10%。

Conclusion: 主动式证据检索与后训练可显著提升紧凑视觉语言模型的临床纵向推理能力，为临床智能体评测提供新范式。

Abstract: Foundation models can serve as clinical agents through tool-use harnesses. However, conventional medical benchmarks assess reasoning over preselected evidence rather than the ability to seek it across clinical records and longitudinal imaging. We propose CASE: a series of role-specific Clinical Agents for Seeking Evidence, together with a tool-use harness and an agentic post-training framework for compact vision-language policy models. We further introduce a longitudinal multimodal benchmark built on UK Biobank, comprising 50,401 clinical questions derived from real-world ICD-10-coded diagnoses of 4,739 participants. Each question links to a patient-specific environment containing clinical context and multi-sequence MRI from baseline and follow-up visits, where agents autonomously select which visits, organs, modalities, slices, and specialist tools to inspect and compare. Supervised fine-tuning transfers evidence-seeking workflows from 14,734 frontier-model interaction trajectories, followed by agentic reinforcement learning on the learner's own environment interactions. Privileged on-policy self-distillation and rubric-based LLM feedback refine evidence-to-conclusion reasoning without prescribing tool sequences. Experiments show that CASE moves beyond question-answer imitation toward transferable investigation policies, strengthening evidence-grounded longitudinal reasoning. Under matched evaluation conditions, our Qwen3-VL-8B based agent achieves over 16% and 10% relative improvements in answer accuracy over GPT-5.4 and Claude Opus 4.8. Code will be available at https://github.com/VinyehShaw/CASE.

</details>


### [123] [ShieldCLIP: Selective Safety Alignment for Harmful Content Mitigation in Multimodal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39688)
*Tobia Poppi,Silvia Cappelletti,Samuele Poppi,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi,Diego Garcia-Olano,Rita Cucchiara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShieldCLIP 提出按"每个模态自身的安全状态"而非样本来源进行条件化安全对齐，配合新的 ViSUv2 数据集，在保留良性表征的同时显著降低多模态模型的有害输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP 等多模态编码器的网络规模训练数据中嵌入了有害关联，需要安全对齐加以抑制，但又不能过度改变良性表征。由于伦理和现实约束无法大规模采集真实不安全内容，现有数据集将安全真实样本与生成样本配对，却把所有生成样本都标为不安全，即便其中某一模态本身是安全的。这种按"来源"而非"模态安全状态"标注的方式，导致安全内容被错误地一并重定向。

Method: 提出 ShieldCLIP 框架：首次根据每个模态实际观察到的安全状态（而非样本来源）来条件化安全对齐，只重定向不安全的部分，保留安全内容。同时构建 ViSUv2 数据集，包含 195k 四元组，覆盖 578 个概念、28 个类别，并带有独立的逐模态安全标签。基于这些标签，ShieldCLIP 定义了超越配对级监督的四路条件目标：安全内容被锚定，不安全模态被重定向到其安全对应物，混合对仅更新不安全分支，当两个模态都不安全时施加一致性约束。

Result: 在跨模态检索、使用 Stable Diffusion v1.4 和 SDXL 的文本到图像生成、以及使用 LLaVA 的图像到文本生成等设置上评估，ShieldCLIP 相比此前的安全对齐编码器和强缓解基线，持续减少有害输出，同时保留原始嵌入空间的效用。广泛的消融研究进一步表明，逐模态监督和选择性对齐目标都对性能提升有贡献。

Conclusion: ShieldCLIP 通过按模态安全状态进行选择性对齐，在保持嵌入效用与降低有害输出之间取得更好平衡；源代码、训练模型和受控访问的 ViSUv2 将公开发布。

Abstract: Multimodal encoders such as CLIP underlie many downstream systems, but their web-scale training data embed harmful associations that safety alignment must suppress without unnecessarily changing benign representations. Because ethical and practical constraints prevent collecting real unsafe content at scale, existing datasets pair safe real samples with generated counterparts, but label every generated sample unsafe, even when one modality is individually safe. To address this, we introduce ShieldCLIP, the first framework to condition safety alignment on the observed safety state of each modality rather than the origin of a sample, preserving safe content while redirecting only what is unsafe. We also introduce ViSUv2, a 195k-quadruplet dataset with independent per-modality safety labels across 578 concepts and 28 categories. Using these labels, ShieldCLIP defines a four-way conditional objective beyond pair-level supervision: safe content is anchored, unsafe modalities are redirected to their safe counterparts, mixed pairs update only the unsafe branch, and coherence is enforced when both are unsafe. We evaluate ShieldCLIP on cross-modal retrieval, text-to-image generation with Stable Diffusion v1.4 and SDXL, and image-to-text generation with LLaVA. Across these settings, ShieldCLIP consistently reduces harmful outputs over prior safety-aligned encoders and strong mitigation baselines, while preserving the utility of the original embedding space. Extensive ablation studies further show that both modality-specific supervision and the selective alignment objective contribute to these gains. Source code, trained models, and ViSUv2 (under a controlled-access protocol) will be made publicly available at https://aimagelab.github.io/ShieldCLIP/.

</details>


### [124] [Invariant Shape Analysis of Surfaces with Spherical Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39567)
*T. Shaska,M. -R. Siadat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种对闭合三维形状的球谐描述子，通过共形参数化归一化与旋转群多项式不变量，精确消除参数化、姿态、尺度依赖，同时保留手性信息，实现截断展开的完备性与轨道距离稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球谐描述子依赖参数化、姿态和尺度，而常用的旋转不变约简（功率谱与双谱）丢弃了谐波频带间的相对朝向，无法区分形状与其镜像。作者希望构造一个能精确去除这三种依赖、同时不丢失其他信息（尤其是手性）的描述子。

Method: 先用共形重心归一化的共形参数化处理参数化与尺度，再引入旋转群的多项式不变量；将每个谐波频带视为二元形式，把旋转商转化为经典不变量理论，使反射表现为某个不变量的符号，从而记录手性。描述子对截断展开是完备的，并在轨道距离上稳定，同时提供数值诊断。

Result: 基准测试验证了理论保证；在双侧解剖结构上，该描述子能将镜像对与不对称对区分开，而奇偶盲的描述子无法做到这一点。

Conclusion: 该描述子精确消除了参数化、姿态与尺度依赖且不丢失手性信息，具备完备性与稳定性，在需要区分镜像的解剖结构等场景中优于传统奇偶盲描述子。

Abstract: Spherical harmonic descriptors of closed 3D shapes depend on the parameterization, the pose and the scale of the surface, and the standard rotation-invariant reductions, the power spectrum and the bispectrum, discard the relative orientation of the harmonic bands and cannot distinguish a shape from its mirror image. We construct a descriptor that removes all three dependencies exactly and loses nothing else: a conformal parameterization normalized by its conformal barycenter, followed by polynomial invariants of the rotation group. Identifying each harmonic band with a binary form turns the rotation quotient into classical invariant theory and makes reflections visible as the sign of an invariant, so chirality is recorded. The descriptor is complete for the truncated expansion, stable in the orbit distance, and comes with numerical diagnostics. Benchmarks confirm the guarantees, and on bilateral anatomical structures the descriptor separates mirror-image pairs from asymmetric pairs, which parity-blind descriptors cannot.

</details>


### [125] [MCD: Causal Distillation of Multimodal In-Context Learning in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39920)
*Yanshu Li,Jiaqian Li,Canran Xiao,Xi Xiao,Tianyang Wang,Yongtai Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态因果蒸馏（MCD），通过迁移教师模型在多模态上下文学习中使用因果证据的方式，提升小模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉语言模型具备强大的多模态上下文学习能力，但随模型规模减小而显著退化。现有知识蒸馏方法主要直接对齐输出分布或隐藏表示，只教学生“教师预测什么”，而未揭示复杂上下文中哪些证据因果性地支持该预测。因此学生可能模仿答案却依赖语言先验或虚假线索。

Method: 提出多模态因果蒸馏（MCD）框架，迁移强教师模型在上下文学习中如何利用多模态证据。MCD采用结构保持的token干预来识别并验证因果证据，然后迁移教师在保留或移除该证据时的响应方式，将蒸馏与模型使用上下文证据的因果模式相连接。

Result: 在三个LVLM系列和七个基准上实验，MCD平均提升学生性能7.23分，比普通蒸馏高4.68分；进一步分析证实了增益的泛化性。

Conclusion: 将蒸馏与因果证据使用模式相结合，能有效提升小模型的多模态上下文学习能力，并具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) exhibit strong multimodal in-context learning (ICL) capabilities, yet this ability degrades substantially as model size decreases. Knowledge distillation offers a natural way to bridge this gap, but existing methods primarily align output distributions or hidden representations directly. Such alignment teaches the student what the teacher predicts without revealing which evidence in the complex context causally supports that prediction. Consequently, a student can imitate the teacher's answer while continuing to rely on language priors, prompt structure, or other spurious cues. To address this limitation, we introduce Multimodal Causal Distillation (MCD), a distillation framework that transfers how a strong teacher uses multimodal evidence during ICL. MCD uses structure-preserving token interventions to identify and verify causal evidence, then transfers how the teacher responds when that evidence is retained or removed. This design connects distillation to the causal patterns by which the model uses contextual evidence during multimodal ICL. Experiments across three LVLM families and seven benchmarks show that MCD improves student performance by 7.23 points on average and outperforms vanilla distillation by 4.68 points, while further analyses confirm the generalizability of these gains.

</details>


### [126] [Steering Fields: Adaptive Vector Fields for Safe Image Generation and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39573)
*Simone Facchiano,Jan Eric Lenssen,Bernt Schiele,Wolfgang Stammer,Fabio Galasso,Jonas Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"Steering Fields"（引导场），将传统固定引导向量推广为在生成过程每一步自适应重新估计引导方向的方法，用于流模型文本生成图像的安全引导与图像编辑，在安全引导基准与语义保真度上均达最新水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本生成图像流模型质量接近真实照片，因此控制其输出（抑制有害内容、促进良性替代）成为核心挑战。现有引导范式是对选定激活添加一个全局引导向量，但该向量固定、与样本无关、沿整条轨迹均匀施加，无法适应生成过程不断变化的状态，常导致非预期的全局改变。

Method: 提出Steering Fields，作为引导向量的泛化：在生成过程的每一步自适应地重新估计引导方向，作用于流模型的含噪状态。方法不依赖显式空间掩码或物体先验，具备可组合性，可同时诱导与抑制概念，并显式暴露引导强度与内容保持之间的连续权衡。

Result: 在安全引导基准上取得新的最优表现；作为结构保持的图像编辑技术，达到最优语义保真度（CLIP、VQAScore），且方法模型无关、无需反演（inversion-free）。轨迹自适应估计能自然保留局部结构，效果类似图像编辑。

Conclusion: Steering Fields通过轨迹自适应地重估引导方向，克服了固定全局引导向量的局限，兼顾安全引导与结构保持的图像编辑，且具备模型无关、免反演、可组合等优点，在多项任务上达到最新水平。

Abstract: As state-of-the-art text-to-image flow models achieve near-photorealistic quality, controlling their outputs, e.g., suppressing harmful content while promoting benign alternatives, has become a central challenge. The current steering paradigm consists of adding a global steering vector to selected activations. While functional, a fixed and example-agnostic vector applied uniformly along the entire trajectory cannot adapt to the changing state of the generation and often causes unintended global changes. We introduce Steering Fields, a generalization of steering vectors that adaptively re-estimates the steering direction at each step of the generative process. Steering Fields operate on the noisy states of flow models, expose a continuous trade-off between steering strength and content preservation, and are compositional, enabling the simultaneous induction and inhibition of concepts, setting a new state of the art on safety steering benchmarks. Despite using no explicit spatial masks or object priors, the trajectory-adaptive estimation naturally preserves local structure, in a manner reminiscent of image editing. In fact, Steering Fields can serve as a structure-preserving image-editing technique that achieves state-of-the-art semantic fidelity (CLIP, VQAScore), while remaining model-agnostic and inversion-free.

</details>


### [127] [MemLife: Curating and Reasoning over Long-Term Egocentric Video Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40195)
*Guangzhi Xiong,Xinyuan Zhang,Xiao Yang,Hyokun Yun,Kai Zhang,Shiun-Zu Kuo,Hyeonjeong Ha,Xilun Chen,Kai Sun,Lucas Liang,Guangqiang Dong,Ejaz Ahmed,Ahmed A Aly,Anuj Kumar,Raffay Hamid,Aidong Zhang,Xin Luna Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MemLife多模态记忆系统与MemOpt强化学习框架，用于长时程第一视角视频的个性化问答，无需训练或查询时访问原始视频即可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程第一视角视频可达数百小时，逐查询重处理原始视频计算成本过高；现有基于文本压缩的记忆系统存在记忆丢失关键证据、检索空间增大导致检索竞争而定位失败的问题。

Method: MemLife构建以实体为基础、第一视角的文本情节，并采用时间索引的智能体阅读器进行检索，无需训练与查询时视频访问；进一步提出MemOpt，一个强化学习框架，用于优化记忆写入器，使其生成忠实、信息丰富且可检索的记忆。

Result: MemLife在四个长时程基准上较最强免训练基线提升4.6%–12.0%；MemOpt在不同视频与问题分布上持续提升MemLife 2.7%–5.0%，且增益可泛化到不同的写入器、阅读器骨干与记忆系统。

Conclusion: 通过实体化文本情节记忆与智能体检索，并用强化学习优化记忆写入，可在不访问原始视频、无需训练的条件下有效解决长时程第一视角视频记忆的可扩展性与检索可靠性问题。

Abstract: Long-term egocentric video enables personalized AI assistants to reason about daily life. However, as video histories grow to hundreds of hours spanning months or years, reprocessing raw clips for every query becomes computationally prohibitive. Memory systems offer a scalable alternative by compacting videos into text representations, but often fail on practical benchmarks: either the memory does not preserve key evidence, or the retriever fails to locate relevant entries due to retrieval competition in growing search spaces. To address these challenges, we introduce MemLife, a multimodal memory system that constructs entity-grounded, first-person text episodes and retrieves them via a time-indexed agentic reader. Without training or query-time video access, MemLife improves over the strongest training-free baseline by 4.6--12.0% across four long-horizon benchmarks. To further improve memory quality, we propose MemOpt, a reinforcement learning framework that optimizes the memory writer to produce faithful, informative, and retrievable memories. MemOpt consistently improves MemLife by 2.7--5.0% across different video and question distributions, with gains that generalize across writer and reader backbones and memory systems.

</details>


### [128] [A Generalizable and Explainable Framework for Synthetic Video Detection Using First-Digit Gradient Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39585)
*Sidharth Shanu,Gautam Kumar,Tej Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Sobel梯度值与首位数字定律的AI视频检测方法，不依赖生成器或场景特定特征，在多个数据集上训练测试，并揭示了零样本检测的失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI视频生成器不仅能生成更多样化的场景（从风景到街景到动物视频），且越来越难以检测。现有CNN检测器虽有效但缺乏内部可解释性，而取证类检测器通常针对单一预设场景预训练，或过于复杂难以得出有意义的洞察。

Method: 利用Sobel梯度值结合首位数字定律（first-digit law）进行分析，通过线性判别分析（LDA）可视化判别信号，并使用多层感知机（MLP）进行分类。该方法不含生成器或场景特定特征，模型也不了解容器格式、编解码器、比特率或压缩伪影。

Result: 模型在GenBuster-200K、GenBusterBench、GenVA、FaceForensics++ C23和CelebDF上进行训练与测试；同时展示了即使特征集携带判别信号，零样本检测仍会失败。

Conclusion: Sobel梯度与首位数字定律结合可作为一种通用、可解释的AI视频检测方案，但零样本场景下的检测性能仍存在明显局限。

Abstract: AI video generators have not only become harder to detect but are used to generate a diverse set of scenarios from landscapes to street views to animal videos. This creates a problem where CNN-based detectors are effective but offer no insight into their inner workings, while forensics-based detectors are often pretrained for a set scenario or become too complex to derive meaningful insights. We present a novel approach to AI video detection using Sobel gradient values analysed with the first-digit law. Using linear discriminant analysis, we visualise the discriminatory signal, while a multi-layer perceptron is used for classification. The detection method has no generator- or scenespecific features, and the model has no knowledge of container formats, codec, bitrate, or compression artefacts. The model is trained and tested on GenBuster-200K, GenBusterBench, GenVA, FaceForensics++ C23, and CelebDF. We also show how zero-shot detection fails even though the feature set carries a discriminatory signal.

</details>


### [129] [MatLoom: Layered Text-to-Material Generation in a Compact Program Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40322)
*Anson Y. Lam,Shuqing Li,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MatLoom 是一种面向文本到材质生成的紧凑、分层导向语言，将材质生成为可执行程序，在保留可编辑构建规则的同时实现高提示词对齐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材质生成不应只产出外观，还应产出构建材质本身的规则，以便后续编辑与复用；现有方法通常只生成结果而不保留构造过程。

Method: 提出 MatLoom 分层语言，每个程序组合带 alpha 遮罩的图层，共享空间表达式定义覆盖范围与 PBR 通道，显式表达图案、颜色与浮雕的依赖；独立解释器将程序求值为材质图，源码保留命名字段与图层参数。无需任务微调，采用解析器引导修复与基于预览的批评来修订设计，并固定候选源进行噪声种子搜索。

Result: 在 141 条提示、6 个骨干模型的基准上，最佳配置在全部四项平面布局提示对齐指标上均高于三个扩散基线；初始程序在平均 BLIPScore 上已超过全部基线，程序中位长度 21 行；30 人 20 提示的盲测四选一中，本方法渲染获得 59.2% 选择，而最受欢迎基线为 19.3%。

Conclusion: 紧凑的可执行程序能够在生成提示对齐材质的同时，将材质构造过程作为资产的一部分予以保留。

Abstract: Material generation should produce not only an appearance, but also the rules that construct it. We introduce MatLoom, a compact, layer-oriented language for text-to-material generation with pretrained language models. Each program composes alpha-masked layers whose shared spatial expressions define coverage and physically based rendering (PBR) channels, making dependencies between patterns, color, and relief explicit. A standalone interpreter evaluates the program into material maps, while the source retains named fields and layer parameters for subsequent authoring. Without task-specific fine-tuning, our pipeline uses parser-guided repair and preview-based critique to revise material designs, then searches noise seeds while keeping each candidate's remaining source fixed. On a curated benchmark of 141 prompts evaluated with six backbones, our best-performing configuration achieves higher mean scores than three diffusion baselines on all four flat-layout prompt-alignment metrics. Its initial programs already exceed all three baselines on mean BLIPScore, before critique or seed search. Retained programs have a median length of 21 lines when pooled across backbones. In a blind four-way comparison involving 30 participants and 20 prompts, our renders receive 59.2% of choices, compared with 19.3% for the most-preferred baseline. Compact executable programs thus offer a way to generate prompt-aligned materials while retaining their construction as part of the asset.

</details>


### [130] [KilometerVision: A New Frontier for Large-Scale Spatial Intelligence in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39588)
*Aravindh Mahendran,Michael King,Matthew Koichi Grimes,Antoine Yang,Tyler Zhu,Joseph Heyward,Tengda Han,Shiry Ginosar,Chen Sun,Dima Damen,Simon Osindero,Noah Snavely,Simon Lynen,João Carreira,Viorica Pătrăucean*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出首个从真实世界视频探测地理布局理解的基准，覆盖最远1公里距离，并揭示当前视觉语言模型几乎完全依赖2D视觉识别与文本匹配，而非真正的路径整合或几何全景知识来完成空间推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动视觉语言模型在大规模空间智能方面的前沿，弥补现有基准在真实视频与公里级地理布局理解上的缺失；同时借鉴认知科学，按人类空间意识的分层阶段来评估模型，以检验其是否具备真正的空间认知能力。

Method: 受认知科学文献启发，构建首个从真实世界视频探测地理布局理解的基准，空间跨度最远达1公里；评估按人类空间意识的分层阶段展开：通过地标进行锚定、以路线连接地标，并整合为全局心理地图；据此对当前视觉语言模型开展大量实验。

Result: 实验揭示当前AI模型处理空间信息存在根本性分歧：视觉语言模型并未利用真正的路径整合，也未形成几何全景知识，而是几乎完全依赖2D视觉识别与文本匹配来绕过复杂的空间推理。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型在大规模空间智能上存在本质缺陷，依赖浅层视觉与文本线索而非真实空间认知；该基准已公开，可用于推动后续研究。

Abstract: We push the frontier of large-scale spatial intelligence in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and introduce the first benchmark that probes geographical layout understanding from real-world videos, spanning up to 1km distances. Inspired by the cognitive science literature, we evaluate models against the hierarchical stages of human spatial awareness: anchoring via landmarks, connecting them through routes, and integrating these into global mental maps. Extensive experiments reveal a fundamental divergence in how current AI models process spatial information. Instead of utilising true path integration or forming geometric survey knowledge, we find that VLMs rely almost entirely on 2D visual recognition and text-matching to bypass complex spatial reasoning. The benchmark is publicly available at https://perception-test-challenge.github.io/kilometervision.html.

</details>


### [131] [SPOON: Towards Coherent Compositional 3D Scene Generation from Uncalibrated Multi-view Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39590)
*Guibiao Liao,Mochu Xiang,Heng Li,Ken Deng,Zijie Wang,Guanbin Li,Ping Tan,Shenghua Gao,Yizhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPOON 将多视角组合式 3D 生成重新表述为场景级、几何驱动的位姿推理，通过 Guide-Route-Reconcile 范式协调各视角物体位姿，显著提升场景布局一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像条件式 3D 生成器能生成高质量物体几何，但如何将生成资产在空间上组织成全局一致的场景、同时与多视角观测保持一致，仍具挑战。已有方法要么将场景布局与物体生成纠缠在一起，要么独立地从各视角观测估计空间位置，导致位姿假设模糊、跨视角不一致，产生混乱的"物体-相机大杂烩"。

Method: 提出 SPOON 框架，把多视角组合式 3D 生成重新表述为场景级、几何接地（geometry-grounded）的位姿推理。不再独立处理各视角的物体位姿假设，而是通过 Guide-Route-Reconcile 范式，利用重建得到的多视角几何来协调这些假设，逐步将物体位姿与相机配置组织为一致的场景级空间布局。

Result: 在 ARSG-110K 和 MIDI-3D-Front 上，在不同输入视角数量下均取得物体放置与场景组合的一致改进。在 ARSG-110K 上，相比强基线，场景级与物体级 Chamfer 距离分别降低 12.7% 和 17.7%。

Conclusion: 通过将多视角组合式 3D 生成转化为几何驱动的场景级位姿推理，SPOON 有效解决了跨视角位姿不一致问题，实现了更连贯的场景组合，并在多个数据集和视角设置下验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Compositional 3D scene generation aims to recover complete 3D object shapes and their spatial arrangement from visual observations. Recent image-conditioned 3D generators provide strong priors for producing high-quality object geometry, making the generation of complex scenes increasingly practical. A central challenge is therefore to spatially organize these generated assets into a globally coherent scene while remaining consistent with multi-view observations. Existing approaches either entangle scene layout with object generation or separately estimate spatial placement from view-specific observations, where pose hypotheses may remain ambiguous and inconsistent across views, often resulting in an incoherent object-camera soup. We introduce SPOON, a framework that reformulates multi-view compositional 3D generation as scene-level, geometry-grounded pose reasoning. Rather than treating view-specific object pose hypotheses independently, SPOON coordinates them using reconstruction-derived multi-view geometry through a Guide-Route-Reconcile paradigm. This progressively organizes object poses and camera configurations into a coherent scene-level spatial arrangement. Extensive experiments on ARSG-110K and MIDI-3D-Front demonstrate consistent improvements in object placement and scene composition across varying numbers of input views. On ARSG-110K, SPOON reduces scene-level and object-level Chamfer distances by 12.7% and 17.7%, respectively, compared with a strong baseline.

</details>


### [132] [Structural Limits of the Information-Theoretic Uncertainty Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39591)
*Jakob Lønborg Christensen,Christian F. Baumgartner,Morten Rieger Hannemose,Anders Bjorholm Dahl,Vedrana Andersen Dahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文从函数层面分析不确定性分解框架，发现假设的AU、EU范围在有限设置中大部分不可行，不可行区域受AU ≤ log(2)/N约束，并据此解释了认知坍缩现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中不确定性分解存在两个关键问题：AU与EU高度相关的纠缠，以及EU幅度随模型容量增加而缩小的认知坍缩。作者希望从函数层面分析标准信息论框架以揭示这些问题的根源。

Method: 在函数层面对标准信息论不确定性分解框架进行分析，刻画不可行区域随类别数和蒙特卡洛样本数N的缩放规律，并推导其边界。

Result: 发现假设的AU、EU范围在有限设置中大部分不可行，无法由任何类概率达到；不可行区域受AU ≤ log(2)/N约束。该边界能解释认知坍缩：当模型置信度高时，AU > EU由这一结构性限制保证。增加集成规模可通过缩小不可行区域缓解认知坍缩。AU ≤ log(2)/N时AU与EU耦合。

Conclusion: 增加集成规模可缓解认知坍缩；在低AU区域不应将AU和EU视为相互独立的量，因为当AU ≤ log(2)/N时二者是耦合的。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation in machine learning typically decomposes uncertainty into aleatoric uncertainty (AU) and epistemic uncertainty (EU) using the standard information-theoretic framework. However, in practice, two critical issues arise: entanglement (AU and EU are highly correlated) and epistemic collapse (EU magnitude shrinks with increasing model capacity). We analyze this framework on a functional level and discover that significant portions of the assumed AU, EU range are infeasible in finite settings, and cannot be attained with any class probabilities. We characterize how this infeasible region scales with the number of classes and Monte Carlo samples $N$ (e.g., from ensembles with $N$ members), revealing it is bounded by $\text{AU} \leq \log(2)/N$. Crucially, the infeasible region's boundary helps explain epistemic collapse: when model confidence is high, $\text{AU} > \text{EU}$ is guaranteed by this fundamental structural limitation. Our findings show that increasing ensemble size mitigates epistemic collapse by reducing the infeasible area. Lastly, we caution against interpreting AU and EU as independent quantities in low AU regimes, since we show they are coupled when $\text{AU} \leq \log(2)/N$.

</details>


### [133] [GroundAnything: Reconciling Parallel Decoding with Precise Visual Grounding at Flash Speed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39600)
*Qize Yu,Lianrui Fan,Bowen Ping,Xini Ding,Zetian Song,Junbo Niu,Kaixuan Wang,Tianxing Chen,Yue Chen,Minghua He,Yuran Wang,Jie Huang,Haojun Zhang,Min Chen,Hao Li,Wenxuan Song,Ruihai Wu,Xianming Liu,Shilong Liu,Shuchang Zhou,Ping Luo,Shiyu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 GroundAnything，一个 4B 参数的视觉定位基础模型，将定位任务重新定义为视觉证据提取，并采用双向扩散的块级去噪实现并行空间预测，在 30 个基准上取得同规模最优或接近最优表现，并实现显著推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归定位模型将空间预测序列化，引入顺序延迟，并对输出 token 施加了因果关系式的从左到右生成顺序。但作者认为，物体、位置与空间关系的依赖关系并不蕴含内在的从左到右生成顺序，因此自回归结构并非定位任务的天然选择。

Method: 将定位视为视觉证据提取，采用双向扩散模型使空间假设并行生成并通过迭代去噪联合优化。模型 GroundAnything 基于块级去噪，兼顾并行解码与精确定位。训练流程包括：公开数据集与专用数据引擎的定位预训练、带联合 AR 与扩散目标的自回归到扩散直接转换、监督微调，以及基于 GRPO 的强化学习后训练。此外还探索了熵引导解码与自推测解码策略。

Result: 在 30 个定位基准上，自回归变体 GroundAnything-VLM 以 72.42% 取得同规模模型中的整体最优，与 GPT-6 Astra（71.35%）保持竞争力。使用熵引导解码时，GroundAnything 以平均 61.75% 超过同规模此前最优的 MTP-based LocateAnything（53.32%）。自推测模式相比自回归版本实现 4.51 倍加速，COCO F1mIoU 仅下降 0.74 个百分点。基础设施实验表明渐进式推理优化可将并行解码转化为实际加速。

Conclusion: 研究表明扩散式并行解码可有效替代自回归串行定位，在保持高精度的同时显著降低延迟，为延迟敏感的实时视觉定位系统提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) grounding models serialize spatial predictions, introducing sequential latency and imposing a causal order on output tokens. We view grounding as visual evidence extraction: objects, locations, and spatial relations are jointly constrained by the image and query, yet their dependencies do not imply an intrinsic left-to-right generation order. This distinction makes bidirectional diffusion a natural fit, allowing spatial hypotheses to emerge in parallel and be jointly refined through iterative denoising. We introduce GroundAnything, a 4B-parameter grounding foundation model that reconciles fast parallel decoding with precise localization through blockwise denoising. Training combines grounding pretraining from public datasets and dedicated data engines, direct AR-to-diffusion conversion with joint AR and diffusion objectives, supervised fine-tuning, and GRPO-based reinforcement post-training. Across 30 grounding benchmarks, our autoregressive variant, GroundAnything-VLM, establishes a new overall state of the art among similarly sized models at 72.42%, remaining competitive with GPT-6 Astra (71.35%). With entropy-guided decoding, GroundAnything also surpasses the prior state of the art at this scale, averaging 61.75% versus 53.32% for the fast MTP-based LocateAnything model. We further explore decoding strategies, showing that an optional self-speculative mode achieves a $4.51\times$ speedup over the AR counterpart with a 0.74 percentage-point drop in COCO F1mIoU. Infrastructure experiments show that progressive inference optimizations translate parallel decoding into practical speedups. These support efficient visual grounding in latency-sensitive real-world systems.

</details>


### [134] [GroundingPI: A Grounding Foundation Model towards Physical Intelligence with Visual Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39601)
*Qize Yu,Lianrui Fan,Boyu Chen,Jiaqi Liang,Xini Ding,Yue Chen,Zetian Song,Yuran Wang,Yi Zou,Kaixuan Wang,Tianxing Chen,Wenxuan Song,Bohan Zhou,Mingleyang Li,Siqiao Huang,Yuqi Ye,Caigao Jiang,Wei Wei,Ruihai Wu,Hang Zhang,Yixiao Ge,Shuchang Zhou,Shilong Liu,Xianming Liu,Ping Luo,Shiyu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GroundingPI，一个 4B 的 grounding 基础模型，将点和框作为量化坐标生成于共享词表，在 34 个 grounding 基准、11 类感知能力上超越 44 个基线，平均 73.68%，并作为视觉骨干提升机器人操作与自动驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确 grounding 需要指定目标对象及其位置，且在杂乱场景和微小目标下仍要准确，并需满足闭环控制的实时性。然而现有 VLA 与世界动作模型的感知能力来自通用视觉语言与视频生成骨干，在这些场景中表现不佳。作者因此主张 grounding 应作为感知基础，并推进专门的感知预训练。

Method: GroundingPI 为 4B 的 grounding 基础模型，将点与框生成为共享词表中的量化坐标。训练包括多模态与空间预训练、监督微调，以及使用 GRPO 的强化学习，监督信号来自公开数据集与专门的数据引擎。并在规模与数据组成上系统分析预训练。

Result: 在 34 个 grounding 基准、11 类感知能力、44 个基线上达到新 SOTA，平均 73.68%，高于更大的 GPT-6 Astra（71.54%）。作为下游视觉骨干：RoboTwin 2.0 上在四种 OOD 设置中均优于所有评估的主流骨干，最强骨干相对提升最高 24.8%；RoboCasa-GR1 上用 50% 演示训练即超过用 75% 演示训练的基线；nuScenes 上平均开环 L2 误差 0.296 m。预训练规模扩大可提升下游自动驾驶与机器人操作表现；密集 grounding 对二者均有明显收益，OCR 有望成为感知学习的催化剂。

Conclusion: 结果支持将 grounding 作为感知基础，并表明专门的感知预训练是通向物理智能基础模型的一条有前景的方向。

Abstract: Precise grounding matters. It specifies which object is the target and where that object is, even in clutter and for tiny objects, and it has to be fast enough for closed-loop control. Yet vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action models (WAMs) take perception from general-purpose vision-language and video-generation backbones, which still fail in these settings. We introduce GroundingPI, a 4B grounding foundation model that generates points and boxes as quantized coordinates in a shared vocabulary. Training combines multimodal and spatial pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning with GRPO, using supervision from public datasets and dedicated data engines. Against 44 baselines across 34 grounding benchmarks spanning 11 perceptual capabilities, GroundingPI establishes a new state of the art, averaging 73.68%, above the larger GPT-6 Astra (71.54%). As a downstream visual backbone, GroundingPI improves performance on robotic manipulation and autonomous driving. On RoboTwin 2.0, it outperforms every mainstream backbone we evaluate in all four out-of-distribution settings, by up to 24.8% relative to the strongest backbone. On RoboCasa-GR1, GroundingPI trained with 50% of the demonstrations outperforms those baselines trained with 75%. On nuScenes, used as the visual backbone, GroundingPI attains an average open-loop L2 error of 0.296 m. We systematically analyze GroundingPI's pretraining in scale and data composition. Downstream autonomous driving and robotic manipulation improve as the pretraining is scaled. Analyzing the data recipe across these 11 perceptual capabilities shows dense grounding's substantial benefits for both, and OCR's potential as a catalyst for perceptual learning. These results support grounding as a perceptual foundation, and dedicated perceptual pretraining as a promising direction for foundation models of physical intelligence.

</details>


### [135] [FOMO: Forget the Concept, Don't Miss Out on the Scene in Selective Video Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39605)
*Łukasz Rudnik,Agnieszka Polowczyk,Alicja Polowczyk,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出FOMO，首个基于训练的选择性视频概念遗忘方法，将原始场景的保留作为核心目标，同时支持不安全内容、物体和运动概念的遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视频模型训练依赖大规模网络数据，难免接触不良内容，因此概念遗忘成为必要的缓解手段。但现有方法主要针对静态视觉概念（如物体、身份或不安全外观），忽视了运动概念的遗忘，且往往不重视保留周围场景，导致成功移除概念时可能意外改变背景、构图或整体视频动态。作者主张有效的遗忘应只改变目标内容，尽量不影响其余场景。

Method: FOMO将遗忘形式化为两个互补目标：改变什么（what to change）与保留什么（what to preserve）。方法定位概念相关的表示并对其进行修改，同时通过保留机制在无需辅助数据的情况下维持非目标场景信息。除单纯擦除不良概念外，FOMO还将生成显式重定向到指定的安全替代内容。该框架进一步扩展到运动遗忘，此时概念由时序行为而非固定空间区域定义。

Result: FOMO在不安全内容、物体和运动概念上均实现了有效的遗忘，并在概念移除与场景保留之间取得了最佳权衡。

Conclusion: FOMO是首个以场景保留为优先的基于训练的选择性视频遗忘方法，通过改变与保留的双重目标及安全重定向机制，统一处理内容与运动概念的遗忘，实现了概念移除与场景保留的良好平衡。代码与项目页面已公开。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative video models has enabled the synthesis of increasingly realistic and temporally coherent videos, while also raising concerns about the generation of harmful content. The reliance on large-scale web datasets during training inevitably exposes these models to undesirable material, making concept unlearning an essential mitigation. Existing methods mainly target static visual concepts, such as objects, identities, or unsafe appearance, largely overlooking motion unlearning. Furthermore, these approaches often pay little attention to preserving the surrounding scene. As a result, successful concept removal may unintentionally alter the background, composition, or overall video dynamics. We argue that effective unlearning should ideally change only what is targeted, while minimizing unnecessary changes to the remaining scene. In this work, we introduce FOMO, to the best of our knowledge the first training-based selective video unlearning method that directly treats preservation of the original scene as a priority. We formulate unlearning around two complementary objectives: what to change and what to preserve. Our method localizes concept-related representations and modifies them, while the preservation mechanism maintains non-target scene information without requiring auxiliary data. Beyond simply erasing unwanted concepts, FOMO explicitly redirects the generation toward a specified safe alternative. We further extend this formulation to motion unlearning, where the concept is defined by temporal behavior rather than a fixed spatial region. Our solution achieves effective unlearning across unsafe content, object, and motion concepts, while achieving the best trade-off between concept removal and scene preservation.
  Code: https://github.com/gmum/FOMO
  Project Page https://gmum.github.io/FOMO

</details>


### [136] [Semantic Watermarking for Malicious Image Manipulation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39623)
*Yoonseo Kim,Seungwoo Baek,Junyoung Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种语义水印框架（CLIP-VAE），将水印重建为可恢复的语义参考而非不透明标识符，并结合通道感知训练以提升噪声下的鲁棒性，下游 SDA-Net 可检测图像被篡改的语义方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真生成编辑模型使普通图像可被注入暴力或性内容且保持视觉合理性，对公共话语和弱势群体造成实际影响，因此需要更鲁棒的语义水印方法来支持内容溯源与审核。

Method: 框架由 β-VAE 二值水印（CLIP-VAE）与显式通道感知训练组成：训练中注入随机比特翻转噪声，使解码器在噪声水印信道下学会优雅退化；下游轻量模块 SDA-Net 利用恢复的语义嵌入检测图像是否被篡改及篡改的语义方向。

Result: 在与代表性二值哈希基线（SimHash、ITQ、HashNet 及其 robust-MLP 变体）的 5 路对比中，CLIP-VAE 在贴近真实的 InstructPix2Pix 比特错误率下取得最高的重建余弦相似度，并独有地支持漂移方向检测。

Conclusion: CLIP-VAE 可作为现有内容审核流程的取证补充，在语义层面实现鲁棒水印恢复与篡改方向识别；但其局限与更广泛场景下的泛化性仍需进一步讨论与验证。

Abstract: The proliferation of high-fidelity generative editing models has made it possible to inject violent or sexual content into otherwise ordinary images while preserving visual plausibility, with concrete consequences for public discourse and vulnerable populations. We propose a robust semantic watermarking framework that reframes the watermark as a recoverable semantic reference rather than an opaque identifier. Our framework combines a $β$-VAE-based binary watermark (CLIP-VAE) with explicit channel-aware training---random bit-flip noise is injected during training so that the decoder learns graceful degradation under the noisy watermarking channel. As a downstream application, a lightweight module SDA-Net uses the recovered semantic embedding to expose not only whether but in which semantic direction an image has been altered. In a 5-way comparison against representative binary hashing baselines (SimHash, ITQ, HashNet, and their robust-MLP variants), CLIP-VAE achieves the highest reconstruction cosine similarity to the original CLIP embedding under realistic InstructPix2Pix bit-error rates, and uniquely supports direction-of-drift detection---a forensic complement to existing content-moderation pipelines.

</details>


### [137] [ExpandDiff: Dynamic Range Expanding Diffusion for Single-Image HDR Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39624)
*Mehmet Emre andıran,Zhuoqian Yang,Liying Lu,Mathieu Salzmann,Sabine Süsstrunk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ExpandDiff，一种条件扩散流水线，通过动态裁剪合成与空间自适应归一化，在单次采样中联合重建 LDR 图像中被裁剪的阴影与高光，实现单图 HDR 重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图 HDR 重建需在保留 LDR 可见内容的同时推断缺失细节。受传感器动态范围与曝光差异影响，LDR 图像在阴影和高光处丢失的信息量各不相同，现有方法难以同时应对两个方向的裁剪。

Method: 提出动态裁剪合成（DCS），在由 HDR 目标构建训练输入时随机采样阴影与高光裁剪百分位；采用像素空间扩散模型，由空间自适应归一化引导，并通过有界输出头预测感知编码的 HDR，在一条采样轨迹中同时重建两个裁剪方向。

Result: 在 SI-HDR 基准上，ExpandDiff 各变体相较最强对比方法在 PU21-PSNR 上提升 3.43 dB，在双侧裁剪情形下提升 7.34 dB。

Conclusion: ExpandDiff 通过动态裁剪合成与单轨迹扩散采样，有效提升单图 HDR 重建精度，尤其在双侧裁剪场景下优势显著。

Abstract: Single-image HDR reconstruction requires inferring missing detail while preserving the visible content of an LDR image. Differences in sensor dynamic range and exposure cause LDR images to lose varying amounts of information in shadows and highlights. We present ExpandDiff, a conditional diffusion pipeline that jointly reconstructs clipped shadows and highlights. To account for this variation, we introduce Dynamic Clipping Synthesis (DCS), which randomly samples shadow and highlight clipping percentiles when constructing training inputs from HDR targets. A pixel-space diffusion model guided by spatially-adaptive normalization then predicts perceptually encoded HDR through a bounded output head, reconstructing both clipping directions in one sampling trajectory. On the SI-HDR benchmark, ExpandDiff variants improve HDR reconstruction accuracy by 3.43 dB in PU21-PSNR over the strongest evaluated competing method, and by 7.34 dB under two-sided clipping. The code and supplementary material are available at https://memreandiran.github.io/expanddiff/.

</details>


### [138] [D-Scope: Decomposing and Steering Diffusion Transformers with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39625)
*Xinyue Xu,Jiahao Zhang,Lijie Hu,Peter Hase,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 D-Scope 框架，通过共享视觉证据将扩散变换器（DiT）中稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征的可解释性与生成控制相连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE 能揭示 DiT 的视觉结构，但解释一个特征并不能证明它可以被用于控制生成，需要建立从特征解释到生成控制的联系。

Method: D-Scope 将高激活图像块的 SigLIP~2 嵌入聚合成视觉质心；在共享图文嵌入空间中将目标文本描述与视觉质心匹配，从而无需逐特征文本标注即可检索单个特征；底层图像块为选择提供证据，空间掩码干预在固定生成条件下测试对应解码器方向的强度。跨两个模型家族、五个层表征 150 个 SAE，并构建含 100 个目标概念、每个概念十种上下文的基准。

Result: 高重建保真度可能与低字典利用率和有限的视觉证据覆盖并存；在逐案例最优强度选择下，对比检索在测试的引导配置中比直接检索带来更大的平均区域 SigLIP~2 增益，但未持续改善区域外保持。

Conclusion: D-Scope 提供了一个可检视的框架，通过视觉证据及其解码器方向对生成的影响来评估稀疏 DiT 特征。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) reveal visual structure in diffusion transformers (DiTs), but interpreting a feature does not establish whether it can be used to control generation. We introduce D-Scope (Diffusion Scope), a framework that connects feature interpretation to generation control through shared visual evidence. D-Scope aggregates SigLIP~2 embeddings of highly activating image patches into visual centroids. Matching target text descriptions against these visual centroids in the shared image-text embedding space then enables retrieval of individual features without per-feature text annotations. The underlying patches provide evidence for inspecting each selection, while spatially masked interventions test the corresponding decoder direction at varying strengths under fixed generation conditions. We characterize 150 SAEs across two model families and five layers, and introduce a benchmark of 100 target concepts with ten contexts each spanning under-specified and explicit-conflict conditions. Our empirical results show that high reconstruction fidelity can coexist with low dictionary utilization and limited visual-evidence coverage. Under per-case best-of-sweep strength selection, contrastive retrieval yields larger mean regional SigLIP~2 gains than direct retrieval across the tested steering configurations, without consistently improving outside-region preservation. D-Scope provides an inspectable framework for evaluating sparse DiT features through their visual evidence and the effects of their decoder directions on generation. The demo is available at https://jiahaozhang-public.github.io/d-scope/.

</details>


### [139] [Introduction to Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39627)
*Stan Birchfield*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本书以“代码优先”方式系统介绍计算机视觉，涵盖经典2D图像处理、经典3D视觉与深度学习，共44个短章节分为三部分，所有技术均用Python和NumPy或PyTorch实现并与OpenCV或PyTorch库函数进行数值校验，可作为学生与实践者的自包含参考书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在帮助读者不仅理解计算机视觉算法的数学原理，还能看到其在真实与合成数据上的具体行为，作为一本自包含的参考书，服务希望理解计算机视觉算法及其Python实现的学生和从业者。

Method: 全书组织为三部分共44个短章节，从第一性原理构建各主题：图像算术与形态学；卷积、金字塔与频域滤波；特征检测、光流与立体视觉；投影几何、相机标定与运动恢复结构；以及从单神经元到卷积网络、反向传播、经典架构、迁移学习、目标检测、语义与实例分割的深度学习全流程，最后讨论混合精度与并行训练等工程问题。每项技术均直接用Python与NumPy或PyTorch实现，并与对应OpenCV或PyTorch库函数进行数值校验。

Result: 书中内容源自免费在线课程笔记，经AI辅助提炼，将大量可运行代码浓缩为简洁的数学阐述，同时保留经过验证且可复现的结果。

Conclusion: 本书面向希望理解计算机视觉算法及其Python实现的学生与实践者，是一本自包含的参考书。

Abstract: This book presents a code-first introduction to computer vision, spanning classical 2D image processing, classical 3D vision, and deep learning. Organized as 44 short chapters across three parts, the book builds each topic from first principles: image arithmetic and morphology; convolution, pyramids, and frequency-domain filtering; feature detection, optical flow, and stereo; projective geometry, camera calibration, and structure from motion; and the full arc of modern deep learning, from a single neuron through convolutional networks, backpropagation, classic architectures, transfer learning, object detection, and semantic and instance segmentation, concluding with engineering considerations like mixed-precision and parallel training. Every technique is implemented directly in Python and NumPy or PyTorch and checked numerically against the corresponding OpenCV or PyTorch library function, so readers see not just the mathematics but its concrete behavior on real and synthetic data. The material was distilled with AI assistance from freely available online course notes, condensing extensive working code into concise mathematical exposition while preserving verified, reproducible results throughout. It is intended as a self-contained reference for students and practitioners who want to understand computer vision algorithms and their Python implementations.

</details>


### [140] [SAGE: Salient Factor Discovery and Generation with Visual Foundation Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39635)
*Shuang Liang,Lejun Liao,Shiyuan Zhang,Max C. Zhang,Xiaolong Luo,Han Wang,Stefano Anzellotti,Yuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAGE 在冻结自编码器的高维空间潜空间中直接学习显著/共性因子，并用显著表征条件化扩散变换器，实现高保真重建、无监督子类型发现与可控生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比分析需将目标数据集的显著因子与两者的共性内容分离，且期望显著表征能捕捉目标特有细节、在无子类型标签下揭示子类型并指导新样本生成，现有方法难以同时满足高保真重建与无监督子类型发现。

Method: 提出 SAGE：在冻结表征自编码器的高维空间潜空间中直接学习显著与共性两类因子，并以参考图像的显著表征为条件驱动扩散变换器进行生成。

Result: 在 Digits-ImageNet 与 FFHQ 眼镜数据上，重建 rFID 低于 2，数字探针准确率 0.950（基线最高 0.281），发现眼镜类型、太阳镜细分风格与误标图像；条件生成将 Digits-ImageNet 子类型准确率从 27.7% 提升至 90.5%，并提升两数据集多样性；在视网膜 OCT 上仅用正常/疾病标签即可分离三种疾病。

Conclusion: SAGE 证明在冻结自编码器潜空间中进行无监督显著因子分解，可同时支撑高保真重建、子类型发现与显著条件生成，并具备跨领域泛化能力。
