This closed loop introduces a failure mode we call co-cheating: the proposer and solver increasingly agree on shared errors, so internal reward improves without a matching gain in external correctness. A post-hoc audit against source evidence shows co-cheating growing more severe over successive rounds of self-evolution, with pseudo-label correctness stagnating or declining even as the in-loop training signal improves. The most direct mitigation is to verify proposals before training: we introduce multi-sample verification (MSV), which queries the same model three times with the source and three times without it to decide task admission and replace unreliable pseudo-labels. MSV partially reduces false agreement but leaves substantial residual co-cheating and costs six extra labeler generations per candidate. These limitations motivate CrossFit, our main method: it partitions the proposer's source documents into groups A and B; questions generated from A are scored by an auxiliary solver trained only on B, and vice versa. The cross-fitted agreement determines proposer reward, so a same-source pseudo-label cannot be reproduced through the feedback solver, while the original solver's update rule is unchanged. Rerunning the loop with Qwen3.5-4B and Qwen3.5-9B, MSV reduces false-agreement mass from 6.1% to 5.7% and from 8.8% to 7.2%, whereas CrossFit reduces it to 3.0% and 3.7%. Replaying identical proposals with source-excluded feedback further reduces false agreement to 0.4% and 0.1%, isolating feedback ancestry from curriculum changes. Across seven downstream search benchmarks, CrossFit improves average performance over standard coupled self-evolution by 8.8 and 8.4 points and over Search-R1 by 8.7 and 7.8 points at 4B and 9B.

</details>


### [244] [Bongard: Training Machine Intuition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39111)
*Li Ding,Haidi Jin,Chen Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Bongard，一个开放权重的"系统1"模型，将机器直觉视为可独立设计训练的能力：用 T5Gemma 2 4B-4B 编码器-解码器分离证据阅读与判断，通过三阶段训练与联合嵌入、沙箱结果反馈提升准确率，并在决策基准上取得高效表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类智能高度依赖习得的直觉——识别模式、判断情境而无需显式展开每一步中间过程。作者希望把"机器直觉"当作一种可独立设计、训练的能力，而非大模型推理的副产品，从而为高吞吐决策任务提供开放、高效的方案。

Method: 采用 T5Gemma 2 4B-4B 编码器-解码器架构：编码器双向读取状态与问题指令，多个独立解码器分支共享该编码，使同一状态的多个判断只需一次读取；训练头直接输出候选概率而不生成文本。训练分三阶段（监督判断→语义关系→行动结果），每阶段在单块 Blackwell GPU 上更新全部 70.9 亿可训练参数；随后进行联合嵌入后训练与基于行动 rollout、精确 oracle 的沙箱阶段学习结果分布。

Result: 联合嵌入后训练将留出改写集准确率从 75.7% 提升至 85.9%；沙箱阶段将冻结沙箱面板准确率从 50.6% 提升至 64.8%。在 DecisionBench 上，最终模型在 23,900 个决策上达到 78.05% 准确率，在 61 个系统的公开比较中排名第四。单块 RTX PRO 6000 上，短请求中位延迟 36 毫秒，对同一状态的 32 个问题耗时 221 毫秒。

Conclusion: Bongard 表明机器直觉可以通过表示学习与结果反馈被系统性训练，为高吞吐决策工作负载提供了一种开放、高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Human intelligence relies heavily on learned intuition: recognising patterns and judging situations without explicitly unfolding every intermediate step. We introduce Bongard, an open-weight System One model that treats machine intuition as an independent capability to design and train. A T5Gemma 2 4B-4B encoder-decoder separates reading the evidence from making judgments. The encoder reads the state bidirectionally together with the question instructions, and separate decoder branches share this encoding, so many judgments about the same situation require only one reading of the state. A trained head returns probabilities over the supplied candidates without generating text. Training proceeds in three stages, from supervised judgments to semantic relationships to action outcomes, and each stage updates all 7.09 billion trainable parameters on one Blackwell GPU. Joint-embedding post-training raises accuracy on held-out rephrasings from 75.7% to 85.9%. A sandbox stage then learns outcome distributions from action rollouts and exact oracles, raising accuracy on a frozen sandbox panel from 50.6% to 64.8%. On DecisionBench, the final model reaches 78.05% accuracy over 23,900 decisions and ranks fourth of 61 systems in the public comparison. On one RTX PRO 6000, its median latency is 36 ms for short requests, and 32 questions about one state take 221 ms. Bongard demonstrates that machine intuition can be systematically trained via representation learning and outcome feedback, providing an open, efficient alternative for high-throughput decision workloads.

</details>


### [245] [Diagnosing On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39118)
*Yang Li,Gongle Xue,Yuheng Yuan,Yijia Guo,Shizhe Zhang,Liwen Hu,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统诊断了on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) 在数学推理中的表现，发现其仅在狭窄的兼容区间内有效，整体上是一种敏感而非普遍可靠的推理提升方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD作为提升语言模型推理能力的方法受到关注，其无需外部奖励或独立强教师，仅靠带特权信息的"自教师"提供密集信号。但其在语言推理中的行为尚不清晰，已有报告结果从温和提升到行为崩溃不等，亟需系统性诊断。

Method: 在0.6B–8B参数范围的多个模型上，针对数学推理进行受控实验与token级分析，深入剖析OPSD的机制与表现。

Result: 教师信号由推理模式对齐和完整教师前缀塑造，而非仅由特权语义决定；OPSD仅在狭窄兼容区间内提升推理，否则导致无效长度增长、稳定退化或行为崩溃；token级分析显示教师信号不稳定，无法预测下游性能。

Conclusion: OPSD是一种敏感算法，而非普遍可靠的推理提升后训练方法。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has attracted growing interest as a promising approach to improve the reasoning ability of language models. Without external rewards nor a separate stronger teacher, the self-teacher with privileged information could provide dense signals on student's trajectories. However, its behavior in language reasoning remains unclear, with reported outcomes ranging from modest gains to behavioral collapse. In this work, we diagnose OPSD for mathematical reasoning across models spanning 0.6B--8B parameters. We conduct controlled experiments and token-level analyses to fully delve into OPSD. We point out that teacher's signal is shaped by reasoning-mode alignment and the complete teacher prefix, rather than by privileged semantics alone. OPSD improves reasoning only in narrow compatibility regimes. Otherwise, it produces ineffective length growth, stable degradation, or behavioral collapse. Token-level analysis shows that teacher's signal is not stable and does not predict downstream performance. Based on these results, we argue that OPSD is a sensitive algorithm rather than a generally reliable reasoning-improvement post-training method.

</details>


### [246] [DAGent: Evaluate-then-Grow Planning for Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39154)
*Hanwen Liu,Yuanfu Sun,Qiaoyu Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DAGent：基于DAG的多智能体框架，用"评估后生长"的增量规划替代"先规划后修补"，配合DAGRPO的结构化RL，在深度研究任务上取得领先效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究任务需要智能体在庞大知识空间中导航、跨来源综合证据并动态调整计划。现有基于DAG的多智能体系统采用"先规划后修补（Plan-then-Patch）"策略，即在执行前就固化任务级计划，仅在失败或证据缺失后才修补图。这种策略在证据最薄弱时反而做出最强承诺，且后期修订会浪费算力在不应规划的支线上，因而对深度研究任务十分脆弱。

Method: 提出DAGent框架，采用"评估后生长（Evaluate-then-Grow）"增量规划：Orchestrator每次生长一批任务图，并以已完成节点的置信度与不确定性信号作为扩展条件。层级上下文层默认传递紧凑的QueryDocs，同时保留完整执行轨迹以备按需召回。记录的DAG拓扑支持结果导向方法无法定义的结构化RL信号：DAGRPO（GRPO变体）在Executor rollout上注入拓扑条件信用，并在Orchestrator计划上施加结构合规正则。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus、GAIA、xbench-DeepSearch上，DAGent在Qwen3-235B-A22B规模下分别超过最强开源基线5.3/5.8/2.0分；该优势在四个开源骨干上复现，并扩展到327K上下文的GPT-5。在Qwen3-8B规模下，DAGRPO相比同预算的结果导向GRPO基线平均Pass@1提升3.0分。同架构对比显示，证据条件规划以更低的每任务token、工具调用与步数达到更高准确率。

Conclusion: 证据条件的增量规划优于"先规划后修补"，在更小计算足迹下实现更高准确率；DAG拓扑可提供结果导向RL难以定义的结构化信用信号，DAGRPO据此有效提升性能。

Abstract: Deep research tasks require agents to navigate large knowledge spaces, synthesize evidence across many sources, and adapt their plans as findings emerge. Directed acyclic graph (DAG)-based multi-agent systems suit this setting because they support parallel execution and isolate each sub-task within a focused dependency context. Yet existing DAG-based agents instantiate a task-level plan before execution and repair the graph only after failures or missing evidence are observed. This Plan-then-Patch strategy is brittle for deep research: the system commits most strongly when its evidence is weakest, and later revisions waste computation on branches that should not have been planned. We propose DAGent, a DAG-based multi-agent framework with Evaluate-then-Grow incremental planning: an Orchestrator grows the task graph one batch at a time, conditioning each expansion on confidence and uncertainty signals from completed nodes. A hierarchical context layer propagates compact QueryDocs by default while preserving full execution traces for on-demand recall. The recorded DAG topology admits structural RL signals that outcome-only recipes cannot define; DAGRPO, a GRPO adaptation, injects topology-conditioned credit on Executor rollouts and a structural compliance regularization on Orchestrator plans. Across BrowseComp-Plus, GAIA, and xbench-DeepSearch, DAGent surpasses the strongest open-source baseline by 5.3 / 5.8 / 2.0 points at the Qwen3-235B-A22B scale, and the lead replicates across four open-source backbones and extends to GPT-5 at 327K context. At the Qwen3-8B scale, DAGRPO improves over a same-budget outcome-only GRPO baseline by 3.0 average Pass@1 points. A same-architecture comparison shows that evidence-conditioned planning reaches higher accuracy at lower per-task token, tool-call, and step footprints than its Plan-then-Patch counterpart. Code: https://github.com/hanwenliu6825/DAGent

</details>


### [247] [ViLegalExpert: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Vietnamese Legal Retrieval and Question Answering from Real-World Consultations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39189)
*Dat Tien Nguyen,Nghia Hieu Nguyen,Anh Thi-Hoang Nguyen,Dung Ha Nguyen,Kiet Van Nguyen,Ngan Luu-Thuy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ViLegalExpert：基于真实公民-律师咨询构建的大规模越南法律基准，含172K+问题、34个法律领域、专业答案与专家验证证据，支持检索、抽取式QA与生成式QA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可信法律AI需将回答建立在权威来源上，但现有越南法律基准对真实法律咨询场景覆盖有限，缺乏贴近现实的法律问答资源。

Method: 从真实的公民-律师咨询中构建大规模基准ViLegalExpert，包含172K+问题、34个法律领域，配有专业答案和专家验证的法律证据，支持法律信息检索、抽取式QA和生成式QA三类任务。

Result: 使用代表性检索方法和语言模型进行实验，结果表明证据检索与有依据的答案生成均存在显著挑战；预训练模型在QA上表现较强，而混合检索在检索任务中取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 将自然表达的法律问题映射到权威法条十分困难，ViLegalExpert可作为可靠的越南法律AI的挑战性基准。

Abstract: Trustworthy Legal AI requires systems that can answer legal questions while grounding their responses in authoritative sources. However, existing Vietnamese legal benchmarks provide limited coverage of real-world legal consultations. We introduce \textbf{ViLegalExpert}, a large-scale benchmark constructed from authentic citizen--lawyer consultations, containing over \textbf{172K} questions across \textbf{34 legal domains}, together with professional answers and expert-verified legal evidence. ViLegalExpert supports legal information retrieval, extractive QA, and abstractive QA. Experiments with representative retrieval methods and language models reveal substantial challenges in evidence retrieval and grounded answer generation. While pretrained models perform strongly on QA, hybrid retrieval achieves the best retrieval performance. These results demonstrate the difficulty of mapping naturally expressed legal questions to authoritative provisions and establish ViLegalExpert as a challenging benchmark for reliable Vietnamese Legal AI.

</details>


### [248] [Argument Structure Prediction in Online Conversations: A Comparative Study of Modeling Paradigms and Task Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39225)
*Siddharth Bhargava,Sara Tonelli,Patricia Martín-Rodilla,Javier Parapar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对对话场景下的论辩结构预测（ASP）进行系统性评估，在严格模式约束下比较监督微调与提示式大模型、单步与多步架构，基准测试表明论辩关系识别是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期ASP研究出现了统一神经模型、多步流水线和基于提示的LLM等多种方法，但它们之间的相对权衡尚不明确，尤其在对话场景中缺乏系统比较。作者希望在统一框架下厘清各类方法的优劣，明确任务的真正难点。

Method: 在严格的模式（schema）约束下，从对话输入端到端生成完整论辩结构，系统对比监督微调与提示式LLM，并比较单步与多步任务架构。在三个由Inference Anchoring Theory改编为双极论辩结构的多样化对话语料上，采用统一评估框架，考察预测性能、跨域泛化、模式合规性与计算效率。

Result: ASP仍是一项具有挑战性的任务；识别论辩关系是主要瓶颈，这主要归因于对话论辩的隐式性与语境依赖性。评估覆盖了预测性能、跨域泛化、模式合规性和计算效率四个维度。

Conclusion: 论辩结构预测在对话场景下依然困难，关系识别是核心难题。为促进后续研究，作者公开了其数据处理流水线以及在对话语料上进行端到端计算式ASP的建模框架。

Abstract: Argument structure prediction (ASP) constructs complete argument structures from discourse by identifying argumentative units and their relations. While recent work has explored diverse approaches---including unified neural models, multi-step pipelines, and prompt-based large language models (LLMs)---their relative trade-offs remain under-explored, particularly in dialogical settings.
  We present a systematic evaluation of ASP under strict schema constraints, comparing supervised fine-tuning and prompt-based LLMs across single- and multi-step task architectures, generating complete argument structures from dialogical input end-to-end. We benchmark them on three diverse dialogical corpora adapted from Inference Anchoring Theory into bipolar argument structures. Under a shared evaluation framework, we assess predictive performance, cross-domain generalization, schema compliance, and computational efficiency. Our results show that ASP remains a challenging task, with identifying argumentative relations emerging as the primary bottleneck, largely due to the implicit and context-dependent nature of dialogical argumentation. To facilitate future research, we release our data processing pipeline and end-to-end modeling framework for computational ASP on dialogical corpora.

</details>


### [249] [RAIM: Robust Aggregation of Inexpensive Models for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39229)
*Elia Onofri,Roberto Di Pietro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 RAIM 聚合方案，用一组廉价开源小模型（4–9B）评委替代前沿专有模型评委，通过稳健的堆叠回归与可采纳性检验实现低成本的事实性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动评估事实性/忠实度高度依赖大型语言模型作为评委，而最可靠的评委多为昂贵的专有前沿模型，不利于高吞吐量监控，因此需要研究廉价开源评委组合能否替代前沿模型、替代的代价及适用条件。

Method: RAIM 聚合方案：对成员间的相关误差具有稳健性，结合交叉拟合的堆叠逻辑回归（cross-fitted stacked logistic regression）与可采纳性检验（admissibility test），该检验仅从成员自身输出即可判断聚合是否优于最佳单成员并接近前沿评委。实验用来自不同家族的十个评委、覆盖八个忠实度基准，与 Claude Sonnet 对比。

Result: 相较 Claude Sonnet，评委组合保留其中位 93% 的 Cohen's κ，平衡准确率平均仅损失 2.9 个百分点；配对差异检验显示在一个基准上明显提升、三个上明显下降（仅两个降幅不可忽视），四个无定论。推理价格仅为前沿模型的 1/64，主要成本是 50–100 条标注记录的一次性域内校准。该组合在其原生基准上也可与专用检测器竞争（与 GPT-4o 差 1.3 个准确率点，与 LLM-AggreFact 榜首差 1.9），并在自建 grounded 集上超越重跑的最强检测器 6 个点。

Conclusion: 聚合是否值得取决于成员本身：当多个有能力的成员在不同样本上犯错时，组合优于最佳单评委并接近前沿模型；当某一成员占主导时，堆叠器退化为该领先者，此时前沿模型才明显领先。这两种条件均可从校准集读出且不增加额外成本，因此只要该审计通过，廉价评委组合即可替代前沿评委。

Abstract: Automatic evaluation of faithfulness increasingly relies on a large language model acting as a judge, yet the most reliable judges are proprietary frontier models, costly and ill-suited to high-throughput monitoring. We investigate whether a panel of cheap open-weight judges (4--9B) can be aggregated to stand in for a frontier one, what the substitution sacrifices, and when it is worth making. We propose RAIM, an aggregation scheme robust to the members' correlated errors, coupling a cross-fitted stacked logistic regression with an admissibility test that, read from the members' own outputs, identifies when aggregating them improves on their best member and stays within reach of the frontier judge. We instantiate RAIM with ten judges from disjoint families across eight faithfulness benchmarks. Against Claude Sonnet, the panel retains a median 93% of its Cohen's $κ$ and gives up only 2.9 points of balanced accuracy on average; read as paired differences, it clearly improves on one benchmark and clearly worsens on three (only two by a non-negligible margin), leaving four unresolved. At a sixty-fourth of the frontier's inference price, the operative expense is a one-time in-domain calibration on 50--100 labelled records. The panel is also competitive with purpose-trained detectors on their home benchmarks (within 1.3 accuracy points of GPT-4o and 1.9 of the LLM-AggreFact leader), and beats the strongest one we reran by 6 points on our grounded sets. Whether aggregation pays depends on the members themselves: where several capable members err on different items, the panel improves on its best judge and approaches the frontier; where one dominates, the stacker recovers the leader, and only there does the frontier remain materially ahead. Both conditions are read off the calibration set at no further cost, so a cheap panel can stand in for a frontier one wherever this audit admits it.

</details>


### [250] [4MT-VLM: How Coarse Is a VLMs Cognitive Map?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39238)
*Markus Frey*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出4MT-VLM基准，通过程序化生成景观并在五种刺激模式下跨视角测试16个视觉语言模型，发现模型能识别熟悉视角的地点，但视角旋转后性能急剧下降，远低于人类水平，说明当前VLM的空间认知地图分辨率过粗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体移动时需要从从未见过的视角识别地点，这是空间认知的核心能力。现有VLM是否具备稳定的三维世界理解尚不明确，缺乏系统化、可控的评测手段，且需要与人类临床常用的空间记忆范式对齐以便直接比较。

Method: 构建4MT-VLM数据集：程序化生成景观，每种场景在五个刺激模式下渲染，通过去除外观线索而保持布局不变来分离形状、颜色与地形因素，包括形状加颜色、仅形状、仅颜色、无物体的裸地形山峰，以及将山峰置于地平线的山谷视角（临床常用于探测海马功能）。在16个开源与闭源模型上进行4AFC测试，并与人类被试的成绩对照。

Result: 模型能在被研究过的视角下识别地点，但相机移动后性能骤降，在旋转135°时低于25%的随机水平，而人类观察者得分85%。前沿模型Gemini 3.8 Flash与GPT-5.6在旋转试次中正确率仅为39%和31%，只有当干扰物间距超过30米时才分别回升至85%和55%。

Conclusion: 当前VLM虽具备初步的认知地图能力，但其空间分辨率从根本上过于粗糙，无法在视角变化后维持稳定、三维的世界理解；4MT-VLM为衡量该能力提供了可控且可与人类成绩直接比较的基准。

Abstract: An agent that moves must recognise a place from a viewpoint it has never seen. We introduce 4MT-VLM, a dataset of procedurally generated landscapes, each rendered across five stimulus modes that remove appearance cues while holding layout fixed: shape and colour, shape only, colour only, bare terrain peaks with no objects, and a valley viewpoint that puts the peaks on the horizon. The last condition is commonly used in clinics to probe hippocampal function in human patients. We test this benchmark across sixteen different open and closed-source models and report 4AFC performance, a measure which is also used to grade human participants. We observe that models identify a place from the studied viewpoint but lose it once the camera moves, dropping below the 25% chance level at 135° where a human observer scores 85%. Frontier models (Gemini 3.8 Flash, GPT-5.6) answer only 39% and 31% of rotated trials correctly, recovering to 85% and 55% only when distractors are moved more than 30 meters apart. Our benchmark demonstrates that while current VLMs possess rudimentary cognitive maps, their spatial resolution remains fundamentally too coarse to maintain a stable, 3D understanding of the world once the viewpoint changes.

</details>


### [251] [Concept Subspaces Compute Beyond the Logit Lens: A Weights-Only Test for Locating Representations Upstream of Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39263)
*Aojie Yuan,Zhiyuan Julian Su,Haiyue Zhang,Zijian Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双侧几何诊断方法，评估抽取的概念子空间与解嵌入矩阵主导右奇异方向的 overlap，并通过多项实验揭示概念结构信号弱于输出导向对照，从而划清概念结构与主导读出方向之间的界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念子空间对模型行为有影响，并不能说明它与输出读出（readout）之间存在何种关系；作者希望建立一种诊断，用以区分概念结构信号与主导输出读出方向，并检验概念子空间是否具备因果充分性。

Method: 提出双侧几何诊断，在给定抽取基的情况下只需模型权重，即可测量该子空间与解嵌入矩阵主导右奇异方向的重叠，并以输出导向阳性对照作参照。测试平台为格式无关推理子空间（FARS），一个从十八个推理概念、六种表层形式中抽取的十维基。比较九种秩匹配估计器和二十六个模型，使用同层 next-token 对照（通过拟合线性翻译器做深度匹配），并在十个不相交概念上重新抽取 FARS；另做四模型三种子干预研究。

Result: 四个基于激活的概念估计器在前十读出跨度中平均能量仅 0.38–0.80%；最终层 PCA 为 3.56%，在 26 个模型中有 25 个超过 FARS；同层 next-token 对照能量约为 FARS 的十三倍，在全部 25 个测试模型中均可分离。在十个不相交概念上重新抽取 FARS，跨格式检索率达 62–100%（二十四个生成模型），表明转移的是抽取流程而非固定基。干预研究发现依赖模型的源导向效应，但远低于全向量替换。

Conclusion: 几何与干预对照共同表明，该框架能够区分概念结构与主导读出方向，同时限制了对因果充分性的主张；抽取程序具有可迁移性，但概念子空间本身并不等价于主导输出读出方向。

Abstract: A concept subspace's effect on model behavior does not establish how it relates to the output readout. We introduce a two-sided geometric diagnostic that measures an extracted subspace's overlap with the dominant right-singular directions of the unembedding matrix, evaluated against output-oriented positive controls. Given an extracted basis, the raw diagnostic requires only model weights. Our testbed is the Format-Agnostic Reasoning Subspace (FARS), a ten-dimensional basis extracted from eighteen reasoning concepts expressed in six surface forms. Across nine rank-matched estimators and twenty-six models, four activation-derived concept estimators carry only 0.38--0.80% mean energy in the top-ten readout span. Final-layer PCA carries 3.56%, exceeding FARS in 25 of 26 models. A same-layer next-token control, evaluated using a fitted linear translator for depth matching, carries approximately thirteen times more energy than FARS, with separation in all 25 tested models. Re-extracting FARS on ten disjoint concepts yields 62--100% cross-format retrieval across twenty-four generative models, demonstrating transfer of the extraction procedure rather than a fixed basis. A complementary four-model, three-seed intervention study finds model-dependent source-directed effects that remain well below full-vector replacement. Together, the geometry and intervention controls distinguish concept structure from dominant readout directions while limiting claims of causal sufficiency.

</details>


### [252] [Offline Guidance, Online Reasoning: Reusing LLM Feedback for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39346)
*Bohan Zhang,Linan Yue,Weibo Gao,Pengyu Chen,Hong Guo,Yanqi Hao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出可复用隐式纠正（RLC），将黑盒LLM的一次性自然语言指导转化为SLM隐空间中的持久纠正经验，通过外部经验库存储并按推理状态检索，使SLM在参数固定、无在线LLM调用的条件下提升推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理强但API成本高，SLM易部署但推理弱，存在能力-部署鸿沟。现有LLM-SLM协作分两类：知识蒸馏需离线训练与参数更新；在线协作需反复调用LLM，且针对特定问题的指导在推理后被丢弃，无法复用于相似推理状态的后续问题。论文关注更受限场景：LLM仅离线访问、SLM参数固定、在线推理仅由SLM完成。

Method: 提出Reusable Latent Correction（RLC）。将黑盒LLM给出的一次性自然语言指导转换为SLM隐空间中的持久“纠正经验”，存入外部经验库；推理时根据SLM当前推理状态检索相应经验，实现无需在线LLM调用的纠正复用。

Result: 在多个推理基准和不同SLM规模上，RLC在不更新参数、不进行在线LLM调用的情况下持续提升SLM推理表现。

Conclusion: RLC在离线LLM、固定SLM参数、纯SLM在线推理的受限设定下，通过可复用的隐空间纠正经验有效提升SLM推理能力，规避了蒸馏的训练开销与在线协作的反复调用及经验浪费问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer strong reasoning capabilities but are often costly to access through commercial APIs, while small language models (SLMs) are easier to deploy locally yet remain weaker in reasoning. This capability-deployment gap has motivated LLM-SLM collaboration, which aims to improve SLM reasoning using LLM capabilities while preserving the deployment advantages of SLMs. Existing approaches mainly follow two paradigms. Knowledge distillation uses LLM-generated answers and reasoning trajectories to train SLMs offline, but requires parameter updates and additional training. Alternatively, online collaboration routes difficult problems to an LLM or leverages LLM-generated guidance and corrections when an SLM encounters difficulties. Although effective, online collaboration requires repeated LLM access. Moreover, the guidance produced for a particular problem is discarded after inference and cannot benefit subsequent problems involving similar reasoning states. In the paper, we focus on a more constrained setting in which the LLM is accessed only offline, the SLM parameters remain fixed, and online inference is performed solely by the SLM. To this end, we propose Reusable Latent Correction (RLC), which converts one-off natural-language guidance from a black-box LLM into persistent corrective experiences in the hidden space of an SLM. RLC stores these experiences in an external bank and retrieves them according to the SLM's current reasoning state, enabling the SLM to reuse LLM-derived corrections during inference without any online LLM calls. Experiments across multiple reasoning benchmarks and SLM scales show that RLC consistently improves SLM reasoning without parameter updates or online LLM calls. Code is available at https://github.com/ZBH031/reusable-latent-correction.

</details>


### [253] [Working Around the Compute Ceiling: Byte-Exact Memory in Galahad Makes LLM Reading a One-Time Cost LLM Reading a One-Time Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39358)
*Sietse Schelpe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Galahad 记忆层，将提示中重复文本的 KV 状态一次性存储并复用，把 LLM 推理从无状态变为有状态，大幅降低重复阅读的算力与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 每 token 计算量有上限，而现有服务是无状态的：模型回答同一文档的第二个问题时，需从第一个 token 重新计算注意力状态。实测七个真实数据集上 98.7% 的提示 token 是模型已读过的文本，这部分重复计算被浪费。

Method: Galahad 是面向 vLLM、SGLang 和 llama.cpp 的记忆层，含两个组件：Taliesin 保存文本块的 KV 状态，在后续含相同字节的请求中直接加载而非重算；Blaise 保存文档本身，只把问题所需片段传给模型。采用位一致校验、故障关闭（校验不通过即重算）的设计。

Result: 在 97,000 token 语料藏 100 条事实的召回测试中（Gemma 4 31B），Taliesin 使模型可关注全语料，llama.cpp 上答对 98/100，每问 3.0 s、572 J；无 Galahad 时（仅能保留最后 12,000 token）为 10/100、9.3 s、2,754 J。加入 Blaise 后每问仅读约 668 token，三个运行时均答对 100/100，耗时 0.59–0.64 s、能耗 200–213 J；调优后的 RAGFlow 管线答对 77。存储语料一次性开销约 100 s、28 kJ，13 个问题后即可回收。恢复的 KV 状态位一致，262,144 个输出 logits 全部匹配。vLLM 下 30 个模型均可用。

Conclusion: Galahad 证明可将 LLM 服务从无状态推理推进到有状态推理，在保持精确性的同时显著提升效率并降低能耗。

Abstract: A transformer language model performs a bounded amount of computation per token, and recent work by Vishal Sikka, former CEO of Infosys, argues that this bound limits which tasks a model can carry out or verify (arXiv:2507.07505). We ask how much of the budget beneath that ceiling is spent on work the model has already done. Serving is stateless across requests: a model that answers a second question about a document recomputes the document's attention state from the first token. On seven real-world datasets, 98.7% of prompt tokens were text the model had already read. We present Galahad, a memory layer for vLLM, SGLang and llama.cpp that makes this reading a one-time cost. Taliesin saves the model's key-value (KV) state for a block of text and loads it on the next request that contains the same bytes, instead of recomputing it. Blaise keeps the documents themselves and passes the model only the section a question needs. On a recall test with 100 facts hidden in a 97,000-token corpus (Gemma 4 31B), Taliesin alone let the model attend to the whole corpus and answered 98 of 100 on llama.cpp at 3.0 s and 572 J per question, against 10 of 100, 9.3 s and 2,754 J for the same model without Galahad, which could hold only the last 12,000 tokens. With Blaise added, the model read about 668 tokens per question and answered 100 of 100 on all three runtimes at 0.59-0.64 s and 200-213 J; a tuned RAGFlow pipeline answered 77. Storing the corpus is a one-time cost of about 100 s and 28 kJ, whose energy is recovered after 13 questions. Restored state is bit-identical: all 262,144 output logits matched after restart, rehydration and hot-load. Galahad worked with all 30 models we tested under vLLM, and it fails closed: any load that does not pass its checks is recomputed. Together these results move LLM serving from stateless to stateful inference.

</details>


### [254] [Ready2Blend: From Natural-Language Instructions to Composable Alignment Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39365)
*Jeesu Jung,Hwan Chang,Juseon Do,Jeonghwan Choi,Jinho Choo,Sungwoo Nam,S. K. Hong,Hwanjun Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Ready2Blend 通过 AlignFormer 将自然语言需求映射为固定长度对齐提示并存入模块化提示库，配合可组合性正则化实现冻结主干下的持续对齐，达到联合训练参考的 93.1%–98.5%，训练时间减少至多 4.3 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续对齐需要 LLM 在适应新需求的同时不遗忘已有行为。自然语言指令灵活可组合但控制间接，后训练适应性强却需反复更新参数，二者各有局限，亟需兼顾灵活性与对齐强度的方案。

Method: 提出 Ready2Blend：AlignFormer 将每个需求映射为固定长度对齐提示，存储于模块化提示库，主干与已有提示保持冻结；通过可组合性正则化将文本需求的语义几何迁移到提示空间，从而支持推理时混合与重加权。

Result: 在两种实际持续对齐设置中，Ready2Blend 是唯一匹配后训练对齐方法的冻结主干方法，达到联合训练参考的 93.1%–98.5%，保持能力有竞争力，仅需少量提示 token，训练时间至多减少 4.3 倍。

Conclusion: 模块化设计进一步支持加权个性化与无需重训练的顺序无关组合，代码将在接收后发布。

Abstract: Continual alignment requires LLMs to adapt to new requirements without forgetting previously acquired behaviors. Natural-language instructions are flexible and composable but offer only indirect control, whereas post-training provides stronger adaptation at the cost of repeated parameter updates. We introduce Ready2Blend, which combines the flexibility of natural language with learned alignment. AlignFormer maps each requirement to a fixed-length alignment prompt stored in a modular prompt bank, while the backbone and prior prompts remain frozen. Composability regularization transfers the semantic geometry of textual requirements into prompt space, enabling inference-time blending and reweighting. Across two practical continual alignment settings, Ready2Blend is the only frozen-backbone method that matches post-training-based alignment methods, reaching $93.1$-$98.5\%$ of a joint-training reference with competitive retention, while requiring only a few prompt tokens and up to $4.3\times$ less training time. Its modular design further enables weighted personalization and order-free composition without retraining. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [255] [Making Grid Beam Search Less Greedy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39368)
*Sean Papay,Roman Klinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出现有 grid beam search 在满足词法约束时存在偏好易满足约束的偏差，与 DFA-constrained beam search 不同，进而提出 fair grid beam search，在保持线性前向调用次数的同时消除该偏差，并实验发现其能生成更高概率的字符串。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归文本生成中常用词法约束来强制输出包含特定词或短语。DFA-constrained beam search 需要指数级前向调用，而 grid beam search 只需线性调用，因此更受青睐。但 grid beam search 在加速的同时并未平等对待所有约束，可能优先满足较容易的约束，把较难的约束留到序列末尾，形成偏差。

Method: 作者先分析并证明 grid beam search 存在“优先纳入易满足约束”的偏差，而 DFA-constrained beam search 没有该偏差。随后提出 fair grid beam search，对 grid beam search 进行修改，以消除这种约束顺序偏差，同时仍保持线性数量的前向调用。

Result: 在两个约束生成任务上验证了 grid beam search 的偏差：与 DFA-constrained beam search 和 fair grid beam search 相比，其约束词排序存在显著差异。此外，fair grid beam search 不仅修正了偏差，还在过程中找到了更高概率的字符串。

Conclusion: fair grid beam search 在保留 grid beam search 线性效率优势的同时，消除了其对不同约束的不公平处理，并能在约束生成中获得更优概率的解，是更公平且有效的解码方法。

Abstract: A common formalism for constraining the output of autoregressive text generation models involves lexical constraints, words or phrases which are required to occur in the generated text. DFA-constrained beam search and grid beam search are two widely used paradigms for decoding from autoregressive models while enforcing lexical constraints. As the former approach requires a number of forward passes exponential in the number of constraint tokens, it is often dispreferred to the latter, which requires only linearly many forward calls. However, while grid beam search achieves an exponential speedup, it does so in a manner which does not treat all of the constraints equally. In this paper, we demonstrate that grid beam search is biased to incorporate easier-to-satisfy constraints first, leaving harder constraints to the end of the sequence. This contrasts with DFA-constrained beam search, which exhibits no such bias. To address this shortcoming, we propose fair grid beam search, a modification to grid beam search which avoids this bias while still requiring only linearly many forward passes. Experimentally, we confirm grid beam search's bias on two constrained generation tasks, finding significant differences in how it orders constraint tokens as compared to DFA-constrained beam search and fair grid beam search. Furthermore, we find that fair grid beam search not only fixes grid beam search's bias, but finds higher-probability strings in the process.

</details>


### [256] [Exploring Heterogeneous Model Merging Approach for Complex Knowledge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39369)
*Jiahe Fan,Si Chen,Yinghao Hou,Wenbo Xia,Ke Xu,Hong Xie,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究验证了无需训练的异构参数合并方法可将专家模型的特定行为迁移到通用语言模型中，在嵌入、重排序、奖励建模和MoE代码专家迁移任务中均取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业模型具备任务导向行为，但将其能力迁移到通用语言模型通常需要训练、蒸馏或表征对齐，成本高且复杂。作者希望探索能否在参数层面直接迁移这种能力，且无需梯度更新或语义对齐。

Method: 应用两种已有的免训练异构合并方法，将专家捐赠模型投影到接收模型形状并插值主干参数。IM方法注入前缀对齐的捐赠切片以匹配接收模型形状；APM方法利用前向传播激活统计选择需保留的捐赠维度后再注入。

Result: 在嵌入、重排序、奖励建模以及MoE代码专家迁移任务中，两种方法均提升了通用接收模型的性能。

Conclusion: 简单的异构合并在无需训练的情况下即可跨多种专家角色迁移能力，表明参数级迁移是无需训练即可实现专家到通用能力转移的有效途径。

Abstract: Specialized models encode task-oriented behavior, but transferring that behavior to a general language model usually requires training, distillation, or representation alignment. We study whether such ability can instead be transferred directly at the parameter level. We apply two existing training-free heterogeneous merging methods, previously shown to transfer knowledge between general language models, to specialist-to-general transfer, projecting a specialist donor into the recipient's shape and interpolating backbone parameters without gradient updates or semantic alignment. Intersection-Merge (IM) injects a prefix-aligned donor slice matching the recipient shape, while Activate-Prune-Merge (APM) uses forward-pass activation statistics to select which donor dimensions to retain before injection. Across embedding, reranking, reward modeling, and MoE code-specialist transfer, both methods improve the general recipient, showing that simple heterogeneous merging can move capabilities across diverse specialist roles.

</details>


### [257] [TTLab at Daleel 2026: STAR-Ar, Sequence Tagging for Argument Recognition in Arabic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39385)
*Bhuvanesh Verma,Ali Abusaleh,Alexander Mehler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR-Ar，一种BERT-BiLSTM-CRF架构，用于阿拉伯语论证话语单元（ADU）的检测与分类，在Daleel 2026首个阿拉伯语论证挖掘共享任务中取得验证集F1 72.69、测试集F1 73.7的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论证挖掘（AM）是重要的NLP任务，但在阿拉伯语中资源严重不足。本文针对Daleel 2026这一首个阿拉伯语论证挖掘共享任务，旨在解决辩论与社论文本中论证话语单元的识别与分类问题。

Method: 将检测与分类两个目标联合建模为词级序列标注任务，采用BERT-BiLSTM-CRF架构，结合上下文Transformer嵌入与结构化转移约束，以支持准确的跨度检测。系统命名为STAR-Ar。

Result: STAR-Ar在验证集上取得72.69的F1分数，在测试集上取得73.7的F1分数。领域特定分析表明，仅在社论上训练的模型表现劣于在辩论上训练的模型，作者主要将此差异归因于社论数据集规模较小。

Conclusion: BERT-BiLSTM-CRF架构能有效完成阿拉伯语论证话语单元的联合检测与分类，且训练数据的领域与规模对模型性能有显著影响，代码已开源。

Abstract: Argument Mining (AM) is a critical NLP task that remains significantly under-resourced in Arabic. This paper presents $\testtt{STAR-Ar}$, a BERT-BiLSTM-CRF architecture for argument discourse detection and classification, as our system for Daleel 2026, the inaugural Arabic argument mining shared task. The task requires the identification and classification of argumentative discourse units (ADUs) in debate and editorial texts.We jointly model these two objectives as a token-level sequence labeling task using a BERT-BiLSTM-CRF architecture that combines contextual transformer embeddings with structural transition constraints to support accurate span detection. $\testtt{STAR-Ar}$ achieves an F1-score of 72.69 on validation and 73.7 on test data. Our domain-specific analysis shows that models trained exclusively on editorials underperform those trained on debates, a disparity we primarily attribute to the smaller size of the editorial dataset. The code for $\testtt{STAR-Ar}$ is available at ${\href{https://github.com/ENTAILab/daleel_2026_Arabic-Argumentative-Discourse-Mining}{\faGithub~TTLab at Daleel 2026}}$

</details>


### [258] [QuantCode Model: Specializing Language Models for Executable Algorithmic Trading Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39420)
*Alexey Chernysh,Orkhan Ekhtibarov,Dmitry Zmitrovich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究如何将大语言模型专化用于可执行的算法交易代码生成，比较了继续预训练与监督微调（SFT）两种机制，并提出QuantCode-Bench基准；结果显示继续预训练提升单轮表现，SFT增益更大，同时发现领域专化会损害结构化工具调用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法交易是可执行代码生成中难度较高的专化场景：模型需将自然语言策略转化为特定交易框架下的正确程序逻辑，在历史数据上运行、产生交易，并保持语义忠实。通用代码生成模型难以满足这些要求，因此需要研究有效的专化机制。

Method: 采用两种互补机制：一是对算法交易框架代码进行继续预训练；二是在经agent验证的请求-代码对上做监督微调（SFT）。评估基于自建的QuantCode-Bench（400个Backtrader策略生成任务）及类SWE-bench的仓库级评测轨道。

Result: 继续预训练使Qwen3.5-397B-A17B的单轮评审通过率从41.5%升至47.5%，Qwen3.6-35B-A3B从27.8%升至33.0%。继续预训练后叠加SFT对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B增益更大，达到58.2%评审通过率和83.5%回测成功率；agentic评测中首轮成功率从22.3%升至58.3%，最多10轮后的最终成功率从47.5%升至79.5%。仅继续预训练虽提升首轮agentic成功率，却使修复后最终成功率从47.5%降至32.5%，与指令遵循能力退化一致，而SFT则同时提升两者。此外发现能力保持失败：领域专化损害了符合解析器的结构化工具调用，针对性恢复SFT可恢复工具调用格式，但无法恢复基座检查点的仓库级agent性能。

Conclusion: 面向框架的预训练、经验证的SFT以及显式的能力保持评估，分别针对领域特定可执行代码生成中的不同失效模式，三者需协同使用。

Abstract: Large language models are strong general-purpose code generators, but executable algorithmic trading remains a demanding specialization target: a model must translate a natural-language strategy specification into correct program logic for a specialized trading framework, execute on historical data, produce trades, and remain semantically faithful to the request. We study two complementary mechanisms for specializing language models for this setting: continued pretraining on algorithmic-trading framework code and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on agent-validated request-to-code pairs. Evaluation is centered on QuantCode-Bench, our 400-task benchmark for Backtrader strategy generation, together with a repository-level SWE-bench-like track. Continued pretraining improves single-turn Judge Pass from 41.5% to 47.5% for Qwen3.5-397B-A17B and from 27.8% to 33.0% for Qwen3.6-35B-A3B. SFT applied after continued pretraining yields a larger gain for Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, reaching 58.2% Judge Pass and 83.5% successful backtests; in agentic evaluation it raises first-turn success from 22.3% to 58.3% and final success after up to 10 turns from 47.5% to 79.5%. Continued pretraining alone improves first-turn agentic success but lowers final success after repair from 47.5% to 32.5%, consistent with degraded instruction following, whereas SFT improves both. We also identify a capability-retention failure: domain specialization degrades parser-conformant structured tool calling, and targeted recovery SFT restores tool-call formatting but not the base checkpoint's repository-level agent performance. The results show that framework-oriented pretraining, validated SFT, and explicit capability-retention evaluation address distinct failure modes in domain-specific executable code generation.

</details>


### [259] [DuplexAct-Bench: Broadening Full-Duplex Speech Evaluation toward Proactive Interaction across Diverse Behavioral Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39446)
*Keyue Xing,Wentao Ding,Mengmeng Wang,Wenming Tu,Zilong Zheng,Yipeng Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 DuplexAct-Bench 双语全双工语音交互基准，覆盖六种交互行为与三种上下文条件，评测 12 个系统后发现行为时序与语义质量常不匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工语音基准仅覆盖部分实时交互行为，且上下文条件有限，难以全面评估系统何时、是否、如何参与对话。

Method: 构建 DuplexAct-Bench，覆盖打断、让话、主动发起、主动沉默、反馈信号等六种互补行为，涵盖 Pre-session、In-session、No-explicit 三种条件，对 12 个全双工语音系统进行 1,290 次中英流式试验，从时序与内容两个维度评测。

Result: 不同行为、条件与系统之间表现差异显著，且语义质量与行为时序之间频繁出现不匹配。

Conclusion: 当前系统在实时交互中仍无法稳健地管理参与时机、参与与否及参与方式。

Abstract: Existing full-duplex speech benchmarks cover only subsets of real-time interaction behaviors, often under limited contextual conditions. We introduce DuplexAct-Bench, a bilingual benchmark that systematically covers six complementary behaviors, from interruption and yielding to proactive initiation, active silence, and backchanneling, across Pre-session, In-session, and No-explicit conditions. Across 1,290 English and Chinese streaming trials, we evaluate 12 full-duplex speech systems on both Timing and Content. Results reveal substantial variation across behaviors, conditions, and systems, as well as frequent mismatches between semantic quality and behavioral timing. These findings show that current systems remain far from robustly managing when, whether, and how to participate as real-time interaction unfolds. Project page: https://alitaxky.icu/DuplexAct-Bench/

</details>


### [260] [Synthetic Data Characterization via Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39447)
*Irene Lago,Ana Ezquerro,David Vilares*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过样本级可学习性刻画LLM生成数据的特性，比较不同LLM家族与规模及人类编写数据的差异，并评估基于该信号的数据选择策略效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解LLM生成数据的属性对其在学习任务中的效用与局限至关重要，但缺乏从样本级可学习性角度系统刻画合成数据的研究。

Method: 生成涵盖单标签与多标签分类、标注、树预测任务的合成数据集；基于编码器训练动态推导机器与人类数据的经验分布，并估计这些分布在编码器间的稳健性；评估基于可学习性信号的数据选择策略。

Result: 得到了两类数据的经验分布并评估了其在编码器间的稳健性，发现基于可学习性信号的数据选择策略对两种数据源的影响存在差异。

Conclusion: 样本级可学习性可作为刻画LLM生成数据特性的有效视角，且数据选择策略对合成数据与人类数据的作用不同，需区别对待。

Abstract: Interpreting properties of LLM-generated data is important for understanding its utility and limitations across learning tasks. In this work, we characterize synthetic data through sample-level learnability, studying variation among LLM families and scales, alongside human-written data as a reference. We first generate synthetic datasets spanning single- and multi-label classification, labeling, and tree prediction tasks. We then derive empirical data distributions from encoder training dynamics for both machine and organic data, and estimate the robustness of these distributions across encoders. Finally, we evaluate how data selection strategies based on these learnability signals affect both data sources differently.

</details>


### [261] [Right-Wing Rock or Just Rock? A Computational Linguistic Analysis of Frei.Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39460)
*Carlotta Schneeberger,Kevin Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究用计算方法分析德国乐队Frei.Wild是否属于右翼摇滚，发现其保持政治模糊性但总体倾向右翼，分类器将过半歌曲标为右翼极端主义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 右翼摇滚传播右翼意识形态并招募青少年，监管机构靠人工审查和封禁应对，但存在逃避监管的边界案例，如乐队Frei.Wild，其政治归属难以判定，需借助计算方法改进识别。

Method: 采样德国摇滚数据集，并构建右翼摇滚语料库作为参照，进行词汇分析和分类建模。

Result: 词汇分析揭示民族主义叙事，两个高性能分类器（ROC-AUC最高达97%）将超过一半的Frei.Wild歌曲标为右翼极端主义，证实其保持政治模糊性但倾向右翼。

Conclusion: 计算方法可改进音乐中右翼极端主义倾向的识别，尤其在Frei.Wild这类边界案例中；代码与数据已公开供后续研究。

Abstract: Rechtsrock is a subgenre of rock music that spreads right-wing ideology, often instrumentalized to recruit adolescents into the radical scene. Monitoring institutions counteract this by manually examining and, in some cases, banning extremist content; however, there are border cases that evade regulation. We present a study aimed at determining whether such a case, the band Frei.Wild, should be classified as politically right-leaning or as part of the general German rock genre. We sampled a German rock dataset and created a corpus for right-wing rock to use as reference in this analysis and found that we can confirm the intuitions from previous investigations that Frei.Wild successfully maintains an ambiguity with regard to their political affiliation. However, the tendency is towards the right-wing spectrum. Lexical analyses reveal nationalistic narratives and two high-performing classifiers (up to 97% ROC-AUC score) label more than half of their songs as right-wing extremist. Our analysis provides insight into how computational methods can improve the process of identifying right-wing extremist tendencies in music, especially in borderline cases like Frei.Wild. The code and data are made available for future research.

</details>


### [262] [Spike-driven Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39514)
*Shuai Wang,Malu Zhang,Mingquan Liu,Weihui Dai,Dehao Zhang,Jieyuan Zhang,Yimeng Shan,Zijian Zhou,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出首个脉冲驱动的VLA框架，支持端到端直接训练用于机器人操作，在LIBERO和Meta-World上以更少参数和更低能耗取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型多依赖大型Transformer，其延迟与能耗成本高，难以部署在资源受限平台；脉冲神经网络具备稀疏事件驱动计算，有望实现高性能与高能效计算。

Method: 框架包含三部分：面向多模态感知的脉冲视觉与指令编码器，将视觉观测和语言指令编码为稀疏可靠的脉冲表示；多赢家脉冲融合（Multi-Winner Spike Fusion），通过双向top-k赢者通吃脉冲路由进行指令引导的场景理解并抑制背景干扰；脉冲动作分块Transformer，在融合记忆与当前机器人状态上引入脉冲交叉注意力，端到端生成连续动作块。

Result: 在LIBERO和Meta-World上的大量实验表明，该脉冲驱动VLA相比传统VLA模型在参数更少、估计推理能耗更低的情况下达到有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为神经形态VLA建模建立了基础框架，为资源高效具身智能的未来发展铺平道路。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models bridge multimodal understanding and robotic control, advancing the dominant paradigm for embodied intelligence. However, most existing models rely on large Transformers, whose latency and energy costs hinder deployment on resource-constrained platforms. Through sparse event-driven computation, spiking neural networks offer a promising paradigm for high-performance and energy-efficient computing. Here, we propose the first Spike-driven VLA framework enabling end-to-end direct training for robotic manipulation, which mainly comprises three core components. First, we develop spiking visual and instruction encoders for multimodal perception, encoding visual observations and language instructions into sparse, reliable spike representations for subsequent cross-modal fusion. Then, we introduce Multi-Winner Spike Fusion for instruction-guided scene understanding, using bidirectional top-$k$ winner-take-all spike routing to suppress background interference and yield fused memory. Finally, we propose a Spike Action Chunking Transformer that incorporates spiking cross-attention over the fused memory and the current robot state, enabling efficient end-to-end generation of continuous action chunks for robotic control. Extensive experiments on LIBERO and Meta-World demonstrate that Spike-driven VLA achieves competitive performance with fewer parameters and lower estimated inference energy than conventional VLA models. This work establishes a foundational framework for neuromorphic VLA modeling, paving the way for future advances in resource-efficient embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [263] [CATCH: A Controllable Analysis Testbed for Reward Hacking in Coding RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39533)
*Shouli Wang,Yanfeng Jia,Zhihao Ou,Zitao Su,Ruize He,Haotong Xie,Hao Peng,Juanzi Li,Xiaozhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 CATCH，一个用于研究代码强化学习（coding RL）中奖励黑客（reward hacking）的可控测试平台，通过刻意暴露环境漏洞并提供基于执行的黄金标签，系统研究奖励黑客的产生动态与缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中，大语言模型可能利用环境漏洞获得高奖励却不提升目标能力，即奖励黑客。这一现象对训练效率与安全性构成风险，但由于缺乏能复现并可靠识别奖励黑客的测试平台，训练过程中的监测与缓解仍具挑战。

Method: 提出 CATCH 测试平台：刻意暴露环境漏洞；通过对比"脆弱评估器下的成功"与"独立审计下的任务正确性"提供基于执行的黄金标签；通过监督微调数据配比控制模型初始黑客倾向，通过奖励设计控制获取奖励的难度，从而支持对黑客动态与干预措施的系统比较。

Result: 实验表明 CATCH 能产生具有明显奖励黑客行为的多样化 RL 训练轨迹；分析显示初始模型与奖励难度共同塑造了奖励黑客的出现；进一步评估了不同检测与缓解方法的有效性，发现思维链监控器初期能抑制黑客行为，但随着策略模型学会用代码注释误导监控器，这种保护会逐渐失效。

Conclusion: CATCH 揭示了奖励黑客受初始模型与奖励难度影响，并强调需要在整个训练过程中评估缓解措施的有效性，因为监控器的保护可能随策略学习而退化。源代码与资源已公开。

Abstract: During reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), large language models (LLMs) can exploit loopholes in their environments to obtain high rewards without improving the intended capabilities, i.e., reward hacking. Despite its risks to training efficiency and safety, monitoring and mitigating reward hacking during training remain challenging, which is limited by a lack of testbeds that reproduce hacking and reliably identify it. We introduce CATCH, a controllable testbed for studying reward hacking in coding RL. CATCH deliberately exposes environmental loopholes and provides execution-based gold labels by comparing success under a vulnerable evaluator with task correctness under an independent audit. It also can control the model's initial hacking tendency through supervised fine-tuning data mixtures and the difficulty of earning rewards through reward designing, enabling systematic comparisons of hacking dynamics and interventions. Experiments show that CATCH can produce diverse RL training trajectories with clear reward hacking, and analyses demonstrate that both initial models and reward difficulties shape the emergence of reward hacking. We further evaluate the effectiveness of different reward hacking detection and mitigation methods. A key finding is that a chain-of-thought monitor initially suppresses hacking, but this protection erodes as the policy model learn to mislead the monitor with code comments. This highlights the need to evaluate hacking mitigations throughout training with CATCH. The source code and resources are publicly released at https://github.com/THUAIS-Lab/CATCH.

</details>


### [264] [Compact Language, Complex Model Shifts: How and Where Ambiguity and Underspecification Affect LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39572)
*Michaela Regneri,Nina Scheller,Sören Laue*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过构造人工同音异义词（伪同音词）和人工上位词（伪上位词）作为伪词，研究词汇歧义与语义欠充分性如何影响语言模型训练，并从机制层面分析歧义与消歧的内部表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词汇歧义与语义欠充分性在自然语言中普遍存在，但尚不清楚它们如何影响语言模型的训练过程与生成表现，尤其是模型能否以及如何对歧义或欠充分语句进行消歧，其内部机制也缺乏解释。

Method: 构造人工同音词与人工上位词作为伪词，并逐步增加这些歧义或欠充分伪词类型的数量来训练语言模型，考察其生成性能的变化；进一步分析模型是否会对歧义或欠充分语句进行消歧，并对歧义与消歧的内部表征给出初步的机制性解释。

Result: 歧义与欠充分性都能提升模型性能，且提升幅度随其对语言类型-形符比（type-token ratio）的影响而增大；但包含歧义词或近义词的序列，其生成准确率低于其他文本；伪同音词的内部表征体现消歧，而伪上位词的欠充分性在生成过程中得以保持。

Conclusion: 歧义与欠充分性并非单纯的语言“噪声”，而是能随类型-形符比的变化影响模型表现的因素；模型对同音歧义有消歧能力，但对上位词式欠充分性则维持不消歧状态，二者在内部表征上表现出不同模式。

Abstract: We analyze how lexical ambiguity and underspecification affect language model training. We create artificial homonyms and artificial hypernyms as pseudowords and analyze the generative performance of language models as they are trained with increasing amounts of these ambiguous or underspecified pseudoword types. We further analyze whether the models disambiguate ambiguous or underspecified statements and provide a first mechanistic account of how ambiguity and disambiguation are represented internally. Our main results show that both ambiguity and underspecification increase model performance in ways that scale with their influence on the language's type-token ratio. However, the accuracy of generating sequences containing ambiguous words or their synonyms decreases compared to other texts. We also show that internal representations of pseudowords reflect disambiguation of pseudo-homonyms, but underspecification of pseudo-hypernyms is maintained during the generative process.

</details>


### [265] [Thinking Outside the Box: Can Language Models Rely on External Guidance Selectively?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39578)
*Minghan Wang,Boyuan Wang,Jinhang Zuo,Yuxin Tao,Fang kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Box²-Bench基准，在固定模型和任务的前提下变化工作流可靠性，以隔离考察模型如何调节对外部指导的依赖，并提出"跳出框框思考"（thinking outside the box）这一能力定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent harnesses常通过人工设计的工作流提升语言模型表现，但随着模型能力增强，不可靠的指导反而会越来越多地约束其执行。因此需要一种能单独衡量模型是否能有选择地依赖指导、并在指导不可靠时予以推翻的能力评测方法。

Method: 提出Box²-Bench，保持模型与任务不变而改变工作流可靠性。在此基础上用两个开源权重模型进行训练实验：仅使用"坏工作流"训练，保留"好工作流"用于评估。采用两种互补训练策略：反事实监督微调（counterfactual SFT）和基于结果的强化学习（outcome-based RL）。

Result: 在前沿模型上，模型通常能从可靠指导中获益，但当指导具有误导性或其可靠性下降时仍表现脆弱。训练实验显示：反事实监督微调能提升鲁棒性，而基于结果的强化学习可将平衡点转向更多使用有帮助的工作流。此外，该行为可扩展至其他形式的外部信息，提升了同伴纠正（peer correction）能力和对损坏记忆（corrupted memory）的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 对可错外部信息的选择性依赖，是独立于任务表现之外的Agent可靠性维度；Box²-Bench为衡量该维度提供了可控评测手段，且该能力可通过训练习得与迁移。

Abstract: Agent harnesses often improve language models with human-designed workflows, but as models grow more capable, unreliable guidance can increasingly constrain their execution. We call the ability to benefit from useful guidance while overriding unreliable guidance thinking outside the box. We introduce Box$^2$-Bench, which holds the model and task fixed while varying workflow reliability to isolate how models regulate their reliance on guidance. On Box$^2$-Bench, frontier models often benefit from reliable guidance but remain vulnerable when it is misleading or becomes unreliable. To test whether this capability can be learned, we train two open-weight models using bad workflows, reserving good workflows for evaluation. We explore two complementary training strategies: counterfactual supervised fine-tuning improves robustness, while outcome-based reinforcement learning can shift the balance toward greater use of helpful workflows. We further find that this behavior extends beyond workflows to other forms of external information, improving peer correction and robustness to corrupted memory. Together, our results identify selective reliance on fallible external information as a dimension of agent reliability not captured by task performance alone.

</details>


### [266] [Is This Evidence Decision-Critical? Learning to Verify Rule-Governed Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39608)
*Haoyang Zhang,Jianpeng Zhao,Qi Hao,Pengyang Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出InterPact框架，通过反事实验证证据关键性，在规则治理决策中实现68.28%准确率，优于六个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规则推理（如资格审核、合同审查）需语言模型依据条件评估证据并按规则组合判断。证据评估错误可能不改变决策，但误判或忽略决策关键证据会导致决策反转。识别这类关键证据可让更强模型聚焦对应条件判断，支持准确安全的决策。识别证据关键性需理解证据如何影响条件判断及该判断如何影响决策。

Method: 提出基于干预的影响学习框架InterPact。其证据干预构造器用冻结语言模型编辑案件事实，同时固定规则与非目标条件，生成训练对供传播验证器使用。人工审核标签记录条件与决策变化，完整的状态到决策映射监督观测编辑之外的后果。训练时，验证器通过固定组合操作，用基于证据的条件概率对学习到的条件决策预测加权，将决策变化监督传播到底层模型。推理时，训练好的底层模型直接从原始案件和目标证据判断关键性，无需人类或更强模型监督。

Result: 在适配规则治理决策案例的单案例证据关键性验证上，InterPact达到68.28%准确率，超过全部六个基线。

Conclusion: 结果支持将学习到的决策敏感性作为优先证据检查的基础。

Abstract: Rule-based reasoning, as in eligibility checks and contract reviews, requires language models to assess evidence against individual conditions and combine their judgments under explicit rules. Errors in evidence assessment can leave a decision unchanged, but misinterpreting or overlooking decision-critical evidence can reverse it. Identifying such evidence allows more capable models to focus on checking the corresponding condition judgments, supporting accurate and safe decisions. Recognizing the evidence's criticality requires understanding how evidence affects a condition judgment and how that judgment affects the decision. To achieve the goal, we propose a INTERvention-based imPACT learning framework (InterPact), which enables counterfactual verification of evidence criticality in rule-governed decisions. Specifically, its evidence intervention constructor generates training pairs for a propagation verifier by editing case facts with a frozen language model while holding rules and non-target conditions fixed. Human-reviewed labels record the resulting condition and decision changes, while complete state-to-decision mappings supervise consequences beyond the observed edit. During training, the verifier weights learned conditional decision predictions by evidence-based condition probabilities through a fixed composition operation, propagating decision-change supervision into the base model. At inference, the trained base model directly judges criticality from the original case and target evidence, without human or stronger-model supervision. On single-case evidence criticality verification over adapted rule-governed decision cases, InterPact achieves 68.28% accuracy, outperforming all six baselines. These results support learned decision sensitivity as a basis for prioritizing evidence checks.

</details>


### [267] [Marginal Response Surface Elicitation for Zero-Label Tabular Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39639)
*Liangyu Teng,Yicheng Ding,Jing Liu,Hengsong Liu,Juncen Guo,Hongru Li,Jingyu Zhang,Liang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MARS将特征级LLM先验转化为可复用的零样本表格分类器，在八个基准任务上取得最高平均AUC和AP，并大幅降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表格学习依赖标注数据，而获取标注成本高。利用LLM根据任务描述和特征语义引出领域先验，有望实现无需标注数据的预测，但需要一种有效方法将特征级先验转化为可用的分类器。

Method: MARS从无标注数据中为每个特征选择代表值，提示LLM提供对应的类别支持分数和特征权重；随后使用中位数聚合多次响应，构建特征响应函数，并通过加权求和进行预测，无需进一步的LLM查询。

Result: 在八个表格基准任务上，MARS取得最高平均AUC和AP，分别比直接提示高出1.97和6.21个百分点，同时显著降低端到端成本。不同规模LLM的评估进一步证明了其相对直接提示的预测优势。

Conclusion: MARS能够高效地将LLM特征级先验转化为可复用的零样本表格分类器，在性能与成本上均优于直接提示，是一种有效的无标注表格学习方案。

Abstract: Tabular learning uses structured data to predict target outcomes. Traditionally, this process has relied on labeled data. However, large language models (LLMs) can be used to elicit domain priors based on the task description and feature semantics, thereby enabling predictions without labeled data. We propose Marginal Response Surface Elicitation (MARS), a method that transforms feature-level LLM priors into a reusable, zero-shot tabular classifier. To construct this classifier, MARS selects representative values for each feature from unlabeled data and prompts the LLM to provide corresponding class support scores and feature weights. It then aggregates multiple responses using the median to construct feature response functions, and makes predictions through their weighted sum without further LLM queries. Across eight tabular benchmark tasks, MARS achieves the highest average AUC and AP, outperforming direct prompting by 1.97 and 6.21 percentage points respectively, while substantially reducing end-to-end costs. Evaluations with LLMs of different sizes further demonstrate its predictive advantage over direct prompting.

</details>


### [268] [Zero-Compute Cross-Lingual Transferability Estimation Using Typological Feature Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39640)
*Dalton Raphael Harmsen,Swier Garst,Thomas van Osch,Zarè Palanciyan,Joaquin Vanschoren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文验证了跨语言迁移可由类型学特征预测，提出用类型学模型替代昂贵的训练测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言迁移的量化测量通常依赖大规模多语言预训练，成本高昂。作者希望探究：迁移是否可由免费获取的类型学特征预测，以及高资源源语言的显著地位究竟反映类型学还是数据质量与数量。

Method: 在24语言的前人迁移矩阵上，仅使用类型学特征训练随机森林进行预测；设置非类型学对照组；采用留一语言、留一文字系统、留一语系等协议验证稳健性；并将迁移分解为类型学项与资源—文字偏置项。

Result: 仅类型学的随机森林留一语言ρ=0.705、R²=0.49，优于非类型学对照的ρ=0.62；信号在留一文字与留一语系协议下仍然存在。分解显示最佳源语言排序受资源—文字偏置影响，而类型学不受该偏置影响。

Conclusion: 类型学数据库包含关于跨语言迁移的低成本且稠密的信息，可作为一种零计算筛选工具，用一次模型拟合替代数百次训练运行。

Abstract: Cross-lingual transfer describes how knowledge in a source language benefits a target language. Measuring it quantitatively requires broad multilingual pre-training, as prior work has done with cross-lingual transfer matrices. We ask whether transfer is predictable from freely available typological features, and whether the prominence of high-resource source languages reflects typology or data quality and quantity. We show that typological databases contain cheap and dense signals about cross-lingual transfer. Our typology-only random forest on a 24-language prior-work transfer matrix scores leave-one-language-out $ρ{=}0.705$ and $R^2{=}0.49$, beating a non-typological control at $ρ{=}0.62$, which verifies the ability of typology-only predictions to reconstruct costly measured cross-lingual transfer. The signal survives leave-one-script-out and leave-one-family-out protocols, so script and family confounding do not explain the effect. By decomposing the transfer into a typology term and a resource-and-script bias term, we find the best-source ranking sensitive to this bias. In contrast, typology is not affected by this bias, which makes it a zero-compute screening tool that replaces hundreds of training runs with a model fit. Our code is available \href{https://github.com/dharmsen/typo-x-ling-transfer}{here}.

</details>


### [269] [SEPAL: Separated Expert Pairs with Answer-Level Fusion for Reliable LLM Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39645)
*Weijie Ren,Yanwen Zhang,Hao Li,Zhuolin Qi,Hengyi Zhang,Naibo Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SEPAL通过三个独立的Actor-Critic团队（分别负责直接推理、证据支撑和验证）实现多智能体协作，避免共享讨论中的错误传播，最后仅对最终答案进行多数投票以提升问答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体协作中，共享讨论会将纠错与错误暴露耦合在一起，可能削弱投票所需的多样性；自一致性缺乏反馈机制，而单对Actor-Critic只能精炼一个候选答案，均存在局限。

Method: SEPAL为直接推理、证据支撑和验证分别配置三个私有的Actor-Critic团队，通过角色特定训练赋予各团队不同的推理目标，每个Critic仅在本团队内引导修订，最后在修订结束后仅对最终答案进行多数投票，保持推理过程相互独立。

Result: 在五个开源权重骨干模型和五个问答基准上，SEPAL相比匹配的单Actor-Critic对平均准确率提升1.81个百分点，且在全部五个骨干模型上均有提升。

Conclusion: 通过角色分离的私有Actor-Critic团队与仅对最终答案投票的机制，SEPAL有效兼顾了反馈纠错与答案多样性，显著提升多智能体问答性能。

Abstract: Multi-agent collaboration lets large language models (LLMs) improve question answering through deliberation and feedback. Yet shared discussion couples correction with exposure to the same mistakes, which can erode the diversity needed for voting. Self-consistency offers sampling diversity without feedback, while single-pair Actor-Critic collaboration refines only one candidate. We introduce SEPAL, which assigns three private Actor-Critic teams to direct reasoning, evidence grounding, and verification. Role-specific training gives the teams different reasoning objectives beyond sampling variation. Each Critic guides revisions within its own team, preventing feedback from carrying errors across candidates. Once revision ends, majority voting combines only the final answers, keeping the reasoning histories separate until the decision. Across five open-weight backbones and five question-answering benchmarks, SEPAL improves mean accuracy by 1.81 percentage points over a matched single Actor-Critic pair, with improvements across all five backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/zhansan114514/SEPAL.

</details>


### [270] [The Evolution of Attention in Large Language Models: Mechanisms, Trade-offs, and Emerging Trends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39661)
*Zhentao Tan,Jingyi Shen,Yanbo Li,Yao Liu,Yue Wu,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该综述将高效序列架构的发展统一为"模型内部上下文记忆"，提出五维分析视角，并基于59条发布记录重建机制演进，提出有状态多维记忆路由假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力虽提供细粒度、查询相关的上下文访问，但密集token交互带来二次预填充成本，且KV缓存随上下文长度增长。现有研究分散在显式记忆压缩、稀疏访问、循环状态构建、结构化状态动态和异构机制组合等多条线上，缺乏统一视角来比较这些重叠的研究方向。

Method: 引入五维分析透镜：记忆表示、记忆更新、访问、读出与整合，分别刻画表示什么、如何变化、哪些可被查询、如何读取以及读出如何形成输出。基于14个主要模型谱系和11个高性能开放权重端点的59条发布级记录，重建机制层面发展与架构采用情况。

Result: 第一，显式记忆与循环状态方法保留各自接口，但越来越多地控制重叠的记忆功能；第二，异构架构日益在网络深度上协同：逐层组合在各表示阶段分配互补记忆处理，跨层复用则将选定的记忆与路由产物向前传递；第三，由此提出有状态多维记忆路由假设——持久记忆沿时间范围、网络深度、基底类型与表示粒度组织，由协同的稀疏写入与稀疏读取决定维持什么以及每次查询的贡献。

Conclusion: 高效序列架构设计日益关注上下文记忆的组织、生命周期与选择性使用，而非孤立的注意力算子；深度成为构建与管理上下文记忆的一个维度。

Abstract: Self-attention gives LLMs fine-grained, query-dependent access to context, but dense token interactions incur quadratic prefill cost and a key--value cache growing with context length. Research thus spans explicit-memory compression, sparse access, recurrent state construction, structured state dynamics, and heterogeneous mechanism composition. This survey analyzes these developments as model-internal contextual memory. We introduce a five-dimensional lens---Memory Representation, Memory Update, Access, Readout, and Integration---describing what is represented, how it changes, what is query-eligible, how it is read, and how readouts form outputs. This lens compares overlapping research lines without imposing one computational model.
  We reconstruct mechanism-level developments and architectural adoption using 59 release-level records from 14 major model lineages and 11 high-performing open-weight endpoints. First, explicit-memory and recurrent-state methods retain distinct interfaces but increasingly control overlapping memory functions. Second, heterogeneous architectures increasingly coordinate across network depth: layer-wise composition distributes complementary memory processing across representational stages, while cross-layer reuse carries selected memory and routing artifacts forward. Depth thus becomes a dimension along which contextual memory is constructed and managed. Third, these developments motivate a stateful multidimensional memory-routing hypothesis: persistent memory is organized across temporal scope, network depth, substrate type, and representation granularity, while coordinated Sparse Write and Sparse Read determine what is maintained and what contributes to each query. Overall, efficient sequence architecture design increasingly concerns the organization, lifecycle, and selective use of contextual memory rather than an isolated Attention operator.

</details>


### [271] [Better Supervision Is Nearby: Neighborhood On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39687)
*Xincheng Wei,Yifan Ding,Yoshua Li,Yuquan Lu,Ziheng Li,Yi Lu,Dongsheng Ma,Rongxiang Weng,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Neighborhood OPSD (N-OPSD)，通过局部参数扰动构建互补专家池并在线路由，增强 on-policy 自蒸馏的数学推理监督信号，在多个基准上超越标准 OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 OPSD 在每个状态只用一组固定参数的特权教师进行监督，但邻近的参数设置可能提供额外监督。局部参数扰动能在同一参考解语境下带来互补的、与参考对齐的修正，且不同专家在不同参考位置提供修正，其合集覆盖的位置多于未扰动的特权教师。

Method: 离线阶段用贪心选择构建紧凑的冻结专家池，依据过滤后的参考 token 增益（相对于池内当前最优）进行奖励。在线路由将锚方向与其支持度分离：用 MaxPeak 选出锚 token，再用量化选择在 top token 与锚一致的专家中挑选。学生通过从 OPSD 继承的裁剪前向 KL 目标学习所选专家的完整下一 token 分布。

Result: 在 AIME 2024、AIME 2025 和 HMMT February 2025 上，每种方法三次独立运行，N-OPSD 在 Qwen3-1.7B、4B、8B 上分别将三基准 Average@12 相对 OPSD 提升 2.75、1.67、1.94 分。学生前缀续写结果支持将专家池用于参考轨迹之外；匹配消融支持以过滤参考 token 增益作为选择标准；考虑池内重叠并按状态路由进一步提升准确率。

Conclusion: N-OPSD 能有效利用局部参数扰动带来的互补监督信号，显著提升数学推理蒸馏效果，且推理阶段仅使用蒸馏后的学生模型，不增加推理开销。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains mathematical reasoning models using a privileged teacher that sees a reference solution and supervises student-sampled prefixes. Standard OPSD uses one fixed parameter setting at every state, but nearby settings may offer additional supervision. We find that local parameter perturbations reveal complementary reference-aligned corrections under the same reference context. Different experts supply these corrections at different reference positions. Their pool covers more such positions than the unperturbed privileged teacher. We introduce Neighborhood OPSD (N-OPSD) to turn these corrections into supervision at student-visited states. Offline, greedy selection builds a compact pool of frozen experts by rewarding filtered reference-token gains beyond the pool's current best at each position. The highest-peak expert need not provide the best training target. Online routing therefore separates the anchor direction from its level of support. MaxPeak selects the anchor token, and quantile selection chooses among experts whose top token matches it. The student learns from the chosen expert's full next-token distribution through the clipped forward-KL objective inherited from OPSD. We evaluate on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and HMMT February 2025. Across three independent runs per method, Neighborhood OPSD improves the three-benchmark Average@12 over OPSD by 2.75, 1.67, and 1.94 points on Qwen3-1.7B, 4B, and 8B, respectively. Student-prefix continuations support using the pool beyond the reference trajectories used for selection. Matched ablations support filtered reference-token gains as a selection criterion. Accounting for overlap within the pool and routing by state further improve student accuracy. Inference uses only the distilled student.

</details>


### [272] [Drift Inspector: Exploring and Measuring Scientific Drift with Atomic Contribution Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39710)
*Vsevolod Karimov,Stepan Ostarkov,Anastasia Poroshina,Anatoly Frolov,Alexander Panchenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Drift Inspector，一个开源系统，通过提取"原子贡献主张"（ACC）来度量研究领域随时间的演变，并应用于EMNLP及ACL Anthology数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学摘要混杂了贡献、背景、动机和元语言，现有工具直接读取摘要无法区分领域真正产出的内容与仅被讨论的内容。

Method: 从每篇摘要中由LLM提取去语境化的、承载贡献的命题（ACC），再将其跨年份聚类为可交互地图，每个趋势都可回溯到背后的主张与论文；提取经人工验证，聚类对照外部人工构建的分类体系检验。

Result: 应用于EMNLP六年数据，显示领域正从经典NLP任务转向推理、多模态等LLM时代能力，这一转变是关键词或整篇摘要计数无法呈现的；同一流程还处理了完整ACL Anthology（346k主张、80k摘要、423个会议）。

Conclusion: ACC级别的分析能更清晰地刻画研究领域的动态变化，且系统开源、数据可扩展并经过验证。

Abstract: Scientific abstracts mix contributions with background, motivation, and meta-language, so tools that read them as-is cannot separate what a field produces from what it discusses. We present Drift Inspector, an open-source system for measuring and exploring how a research field changes over time at the level of Atomic Contribution Claims (ACCs): decontextualized, contribution-bearing propositions an LLM extracts from each abstract before analysis. The system clusters these claims across years into an interactive map where every trend traces back to the claims and papers behind it. Applied to six years of EMNLP, it shows the field shifting away from classic NLP tasks toward LLM-era capabilities such as reasoning and multimodality -- a movement that keyword or whole-abstract counts blur. The released data extend beyond EMNLP: the same pipeline has processed the full ACL Anthology (346k claims, 80k abstracts, 423 venues). Extraction is human-validated and clustering checked against an external manually constructed taxonomy.

</details>


### [273] [LatentHarness: Learning Latent Actions for Memory and Reasoning via Counterfactual Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39740)
*Xiaoqiang Wang,Suyuchen Wang,Bang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LatentHarness 将长上下文中的记忆访问与潜在推理统一为顺序潜在动作选择，通过反事实策略蒸馏训练模型在 THINK/RECALL/EXIT 间决策，在 1.4B 规模上相较最强基线分别提升 2.8% 和 10.0% 相对性能，并快 5.9 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理存在两个互补瓶颈：一是需要在长输入中保留证据，二是需要在多步推理中维持计算。现有方法大多分别处理二者，如用外部记忆扩展对远处证据的访问、用潜在推理压缩多步计算，缺乏统一方案。

Method: 提出 LatentHarness，将记忆访问与潜在推理统一建模为顺序潜在动作选择。每个内部步骤中，模型选择 THINK（继续计算）、RECALL（从输入证据与中间推理状态的快速权重记忆中读取）或 EXIT（输出下一个 token）。采用反事实策略蒸馏训练该策略：对每个动作分支展开一步并评估其对输出 token 的影响，从而学习何时记忆优于继续推理，并通过反事实召回梯度学习哪些中间状态应被保留。

Result: 在六个通用与长上下文推理基准上，1.4B 规模的 LatentHarness 相较最强基线分别取得 2.8% 与 10.0% 的相对提升，且比最强的长上下文基线运行速度快 5.9 倍。

Conclusion: 将记忆访问与潜在推理统一为潜在动作选择，并通过反事实策略蒸馏进行训练，可有效协调“保留证据”与“维持计算”两类需求，在性能与效率上同时优于现有分离式方法。

Abstract: Long-context reasoning faces two complementary bottlenecks: retaining evidence across long inputs and sustaining computation across many reasoning steps. Existing approaches largely address them separately, with external memory extending access to distant evidence and latent reasoning compressing multi-step computation. We introduce LatentHarness, which unifies memory access and latent reasoning as sequential latent action selection. At each internal step, the model chooses THINK for further computation, RECALL from a fast-weight memory of input evidence and intermediate reasoning states, or EXIT to emit the next token. We train this policy with counterfactual policy distillation, which branches every action for one step and scores its effect on the emitted token. These gains teach the policy when memory is more useful than further reasoning, while gradients through counterfactual recall teach which intermediate states should be retained in memory for future use. Across six general and long-context reasoning benchmarks, LatentHarness at 1.4B improves on the strongest baselines by 2.8% and 10.0% relative, respectively, and runs 5.9x faster than the strongest long-context baseline.

</details>


### [274] [MemCodex: Self-Programming Hierarchical Memory for Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39765)
*Xiaoqiang Wang,Bang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemCodex是一个自进化分层记忆系统，将经验组织为可执行记忆程序，通过开放式程序进化搜索设计空间，实现层内实现与跨层组合的自适应。相比最强自适应记忆基线，任务成功率平均提升10.1%，上下文token减少3.4倍，推理速度提升2.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent记忆面临异构访问需求：单跳问题只需一条证据，多跳问题需组合多源证据，而预定义记忆工作流无法适应这种变化。现有自适应方法虽在记忆组件及其组合上搜索或学习，但设计空间本身仍是预定义的，限制了适应能力。

Method: 提出MemCodex自进化分层记忆系统，将经验组织为可执行记忆程序（涵盖摘要、关系知识、可复用技能与潜在记忆）。通过开放式程序进化，重写各层的构建、索引、检索与路由方式，从而搜索开放设计空间，同时适应层内实现与跨层组合。查询时读取按从粗到细遍历层次，证据充分即停止，必要时下探至原始历史。此外构建MemArena统一运行时，将异构数据与记忆系统置于统一接口之下。

Result: 相比最强自适应记忆基线，MemCodex平均任务成功率相对提升10.1%，同时使用3.4倍更少的上下文token，并实现2.1倍更快的推理。

Conclusion: 开放式的分层记忆程序进化能够突破预定义设计空间的局限，在提升任务成功率的同时显著降低上下文开销并加速推理，表明记忆系统的设计空间本身应成为可搜索对象。

Abstract: Agent memory faces heterogeneous access needs: a single-hop question may require one piece of evidence, whereas a multi-hop question must combine evidence from multiple sources. Predefined memory workflows cannot adapt to these varying needs. Recent adaptive methods search or learn over memory components and their compositions, but the design space itself remains predefined. We introduce MemCodex, a self-evolving hierarchical memory system that organizes experience into executable memory programs for summaries, relational knowledge, reusable skills, and latent memory. Open-ended program evolution searches the open design space of layer programs by rewriting how each layer is constructed, indexed, retrieved, and routed, thereby adapting both within-layer implementations and cross-layer composition. At query time, reads traverse the hierarchy from coarse to fine and stop once sufficient evidence is found, descending to the original history when needed. We further develop MemArena, a unified runtime that places heterogeneous data and memory systems behind a common interface. MemCodex improves average task success by 10.1% relative to the strongest adaptive-memory baseline, while using 3.4x fewer context tokens and achieving 2.1x faster inference.

</details>


### [275] [Explore-on-Graph: Hybrid Embedding-LLM Reasoning for Knowledge Graph Question Answering under Incompleteness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39786)
*Ola El Khatib,Djellel Difallah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: XoG是一个面向不完整知识图谱的多跳问答框架，通过结合类型级实体-关系统计与KG嵌入来恢复缺失推理路径，而非依赖LLM参数知识，在保证完整KG竞争力的同时提升不完整KG下的表现并降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的KGQA方法多依赖遍历已有图边，当推理路径因事实缺失而断裂时表现不可靠；而让LLM直接生成缺失知识又会引入幻觉证据。因此需要一种不依赖LLM参数知识、能从图结构本身恢复缺失推理路径的方法。

Method: 提出XoG框架，结合类型级实体-关系统计来识别候选关系，并利用KG嵌入检索可能的缺失实体，将LLM作为语义选择器与推理器，整体集成到迭代的规划-探索-推理过程中。

Result: 在WebQSP、CWQ及基于Wikidata的BRINK基准上，XoG在完整KG上保持竞争力，在KG不完整条件下持续优于无任务特定KGQA训练的可比方法；该优势在多种LLM骨干上均成立，且相较相近的规划类方法最多降低33%的token消耗。

Conclusion: 更强的LLM本身无法解决图证据缺失问题，从学习到的图结构恢复缺失路径是更可靠的方案，XoG兼具效果与效率优势。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly combined with knowledge graphs (KGs) to ground reasoning in structured evidence. However, most LLM-based KGQA methods rely on traversing existing graph edges and become unreliable when reasoning paths are broken by missing facts. Alternatives that ask LLMs to generate missing knowledge risk introducing hallucinated evidence. We introduce XoG (eXplore-on-Graph), a framework for multi-hop question answering over incomplete KGs that recovers missing reasoning paths from learned graph structure rather than LLM parametric knowledge. XoG combines type-level entity-relation statistics to identify candidate relations with KG embeddings to retrieve plausible missing entities, using the LLM as a semantic selector and reasoner. These mechanisms are integrated into an iterative planning-exploration-reasoning process. Experiments on WebQSP, CWQ, and the Wikidata-based BRINK benchmark show that XoG remains competitive on complete KGs and consistently outperforms comparable methods without task-specific KGQA training under KG incompleteness. These gains persist across multiple LLM backbones, indicating that stronger LLMs alone do not resolve missing graph evidence. XoG also reduces LLM token consumption by up to 33% compared with a closely related planning-based approach.

</details>


### [276] [Stress-Testing LLM Lie Detectors: Role-Play Failures and Spurious Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39807)
*Maximilian von Klinski,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Wojciech Samek,Lennart Bürger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有谎言检测探针在角色扮演情境下不可靠，常受虚假相关概念干扰，作者提出新探针并强调去相关训练数据的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谎言检测探针通常假设可从语言模型内部状态判断输出是否真实，但角色扮演使"真实"含义复杂化：模型可采纳与事实相悖的人格（如阴谋论者）。作者想探究探针在此类反事实人格下是否仍能标记虚假信息，还是会被角色信念带偏。

Method: 构建了包含8,916条人类审核、on-policy响应的数据集，来自三个采纳反事实人格的LLM；评估八种已有探针；并构建三个新颖的混淆数据集，使"真实"与潜在混淆概念（如指令遵循、响应似然）呈反相关，以揭示探针的虚假相关来源。

Result: 许多现有探针在反事实人格情境下失效，尤其在同一人格提示下评估正确与错误答案时；实验表明它们强烈追踪训练数据中与真实虚假相关的概念，如指令遵循或响应似然。

Conclusion: 当前谎言检测探针远未可靠，需在训练数据中将"真实"与混淆概念去相关；作者提出一个简单线性探针，在人格与混淆压力测试中综合表现最佳。

Abstract: Lie detection probes aim to predict from a language model's internal states whether its output is truthful or dishonest. However, role-play complicates what "truth" means for an LLM: language models can adopt a wide range of personas that take very different claims to be true, including personas whose beliefs clearly contradict reality, such as a conspiracy theorist. In this work, we investigate whether lie detection probes reliably flag falsehoods generated under such an anti-factual persona or whether they instead follow the persona's beliefs. We introduce a dataset of 8,916 human-reviewed, on-policy responses from three LLMs adopting anti-factual personas. Evaluating eight probes from prior work, we find that many fail in this setting, particularly when correct and incorrect answers are evaluated under the same persona prompt. To investigate why, we construct three novel confounder datasets in which truth is anti-correlated with a potential confounding concept. Our experiments reveal that many existing probes strongly track concepts that are spuriously correlated with truth in their training data, such as instruction compliance or response likelihood. Based on these findings, we introduce a simple linear probe that achieves the strongest overall performance on both the persona and confounder stress tests. Our results suggest that current lie detection probes are far from reliable and highlight the need for training data in which truth is decorrelated from confounding concepts.

</details>


### [277] [Synthetic Pre-pretraining Survives Scale, but Not as a Grammatical Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39827)
*Atsuki Yamaguchi,Tatsuro Inaba,Joel Niklaus,Michal Štefánik,Aline Villavicencio,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对预预训练（PPT）进行了大规模系统性研究，发现其token效率优势在更大规模和真实数据混合下依然成立，但增益来源并非语法先验，而是长程检索能力的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究认为PPT的增益来自语法先验（结构归纳偏置），但仅在不超过1B参数、PT预算低于2B token、以网页文本为主的条件下验证，尚不清楚其在更大规模及多样数据混合（如代码、数学）下是否有效。

Method: 开展涵盖5种PPT任务、4种PT数据混合、4种参数规模（500M至7B）、PT预算最高100B token的综合性实验研究。

Result: PPT的下游性能与token效率增益在规模扩大后依然存在（如3B规模下节省至少21B PT token）；但未发现增益源自语法先验的一致证据，下游性能与语法可接受性在各规模下不一致；增益来自能提升长程检索的PPT任务；增益对PT数据混合方式稳健，仅当缺少网页文本时减弱。

Conclusion: PPT是PT的低成本补充，未来PPT任务设计应针对长程检索而非自然语言语法。

Abstract: Pre-pretraining (PPT) on synthetic non-natural language data improves token efficiency during language model pre-training (PT). Prior work attributes this gain to a grammatical prior, i.e., a structural inductive bias learned during PPT that transfers to natural language grammar. However, PPT has only been tested on models of at most 1B parameters and PT budgets below 2B tokens on predominantly web text. It is unknown whether PPT is effective at larger scales and under more realistic PT data mixtures that combine diverse sources (e.g., code and math). We therefore present a comprehensive study on PPT spanning five PPT tasks, four PT data mixtures, four parameter scales (500M to 7B), and PT budgets of up to 100B tokens. Our results demonstrate that the downstream performance and token efficiency gains of PPT persist at scale, e.g., saving at least 21B PT tokens at the 3B scale. However, in contrast to prior work, we find no consistent evidence that these gains stem from a grammatical prior. Downstream performance does not consistently align with grammatical acceptability across model sizes. Instead, we find that downstream gains arise from PPT tasks that improve long-range retrieval. Finally, PPT performance gains are robust to how PT data mixtures are composed and diminish only when web text is absent. Overall, PPT is a low-cost addition to PT, and future PPT task design should target long-range retrieval rather than natural language grammar.

</details>


### [278] [When a Kindergartener Solves Calculus: Measuring Capability Leakage in Role-Prompted Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39846)
*Pakhapoom Sarapat,Saksorn Ruangtanusak,Kunat Pipatanakul,Pittawat Taveekitworachai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究"角色-能力泄漏"(RCL)问题：角色提示的推理模型能生成符合角色风格的文本，但其底层能力远超角色设定水平；作者构建了RoleCapBench基准并提出Injection干预方法以缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角色扮演提示下，模型可能在语言风格上"入戏"，但实际能力仍保持专家水平，造成角色与能力不一致。这种泄漏会使角色扮演的真实性和可靠性受损，因此需要专门的评测基准与缓解手段。

Method: 提出RCL概念并构建RoleCapBench基准，涵盖6种教育角色与4个评估层级（小学至A-level），用于评测三个开源权重推理模型的角色-能力对齐情况；同时提出推理时干预方法Injection，结合角色专属能力指南与引导性预填充回复前缀。

Result: 朴素角色提示下模型角色语气得分达1.218–1.389，但超角色准确率仍高达0.811–0.898；RCL在多种提示条件下（包括明确要求匹配角色能力）持续存在。Injection可将超角色准确率最多降低0.562，同时在多数模型上保持角色内准确率，仅下降不到0.058。

Conclusion: 角色提示无法使模型能力真正与角色匹配，RCL是一个普遍且顽固的问题；Injection作为推理时方法能有效改善角色-能力对齐，且代价很小。相关脚本与评测数据将在录用后公开。

Abstract: We investigate the problem of role-capability leakage (RCL), in which a role-prompted reasoning model generates convincing in-role text while continuing to exhibit capabilities on benchmarks that exceed those implied by the assigned role. For example, when a model is prompted to assume the role of a kindergarten student, one might expect its performance on a mathematics benchmark to reflect kindergarten-level ability rather than expert-level proficiency in solving calculus problems. We introduce RoleCapBench, a curriculum-grounded benchmark for evaluating RCL across six educational roles and four assessment levels spanning elementary school through A-level, and use it to evaluate three open-weight reasoning models. We find that although the models can generate stylistically convincing in-role responses, they consistently fail to align their underlying capabilities with their assigned roles. Naive role prompting yields strong role-voice scores of 1.218--1.389 while retaining above-role accuracy of 0.811--0.898. RCL persists across a range of prompting conditions, including prompts that explicitly instruct the model to match the role's capability level. To mitigate this problem, we propose Injection, an inference-time intervention that combines explicit, role-specific capability guidelines with a guiding prefilled response prefix. Injection improves role-capability alignment across models, reducing above-role accuracy by up to 0.562 while preserving in-role accuracy with a marginal drop of less than 0.058 across most models.
